Traced the actual code // first rather than assuming: previously, toggleEntrega() had // zero interaction with the Priorities tab at all — the // delivered RO's stale rank number just sat there unchanged, // and would still have been counted by any FUTURE // setPriority() reorder call, since readPriorityMap() had no // way to know that rank was no longer meaningful. // Two changes: (1) readPriorityMap() now treats 0 as a // sentinel meaning "removed from the queue", identical to // null everywhere it's read — safe to repurpose since // setPriority() already rejects 0 as an invalid rank // (newPri < 1), so no real priority could ever legitimately // be 0. (2) new removeFromPriorityQueue(roNumber, actor), // mirroring setPriority()'s own insert-at-N reorder math but // for removal instead of insertion — writes rank=0 for the // delivered RO and shifts every higher-ranked RO down by one, // in one single new Priorities column, same append-only // pattern every other write to that tab already uses. Lets the // frontend identify which colored pill belongs to the event // currently being viewed/rescheduled, versus other unrelated // bookings that happen to share the same day/slot — without // this, opening an event and seeing the slot grid default to // TODAY's date could look like a color bug (grey/wrong-colored // pills belonging to entirely different events). // v2.7.27: AGENDAMENTO CAPACITY — getFreeSlots no longer hides/excludes // slots that already have an event (the old 1-per-slot hard // rule). Capacity is now a SOFT cap of AGENDA_SLOT_CAPACITY // (3) TOTAL Agendamentos per slot, regardless of who created // them — one Agendador can take more than one of those 3, the // cap isn't 1-per-person. Every slot is always returned and // always selectable, even at/past capacity (a 4th can still // be created if genuinely needed) — capacity is purely // informational. Each slot's `bookings` array lists who // already booked it (name + color), letting the frontend // show colored count pills before the Aprovador picks a busy // slot. New Agendadores sheet (separate from Aprovadores — // a distinct concept: who's allowed to create Agendamentos // and what color represents them) backs isAgendadorSync/ // agendadorColor. Before an // Aprovador creates an ENTREGA event, the frontend checks whether // these 5 of the 7 default joblines are all at 100% completion, and // if not, shows a warning listing what's still pending — but the // Aprovador can dismiss it and create the event anyway. Only used to compute the LEGACY jobline_id // format ('DEF--') for backward-compat lookups against // completion history written before this stable-ID scheme existed. const LEGACY_DEFAULT_JOBLINES_ORDER = [ 'VAL de entrada', 'Checklist entrada', 'Diagnostico', 'Lavagem', 'Checklist saida', 'VAL de saida', ]; // Stable jobline_id for a default jobline, e.g. 'DEF-VAL_de_entrada-29877' // — derived from the jobline's NAME via sanitiseName(), not its // position in DEFAULT_JOBLINES. This means the array can be freely // reordered, or have new items inserted anywhere (like PQIS before // VAL de entrada), without ever disturbing existing completion/lock // history — unlike the old 'DEF--' format, where the index // alone determined identity, so any reorder silently reassigned every // default jobline's history to the wrong slot. function defaultJoblineId(desc, roNumber) { return 'DEF-' + sanitiseName(desc) + '-' + roNumber; } // Legacy 'DEF--' id, computed against the ORIGINAL // (pre-PQIS) ordering — used only as a fallback read for completion // records written before v2.7.33. Returns null for joblines that // didn't exist in the original 6-item array (i.e. PQIS itself has no // legacy id to fall back to — it's a genuinely new jobline). function legacyDefaultJoblineId(desc, roNumber) { const idx = LEGACY_DEFAULT_JOBLINES_ORDER.indexOf(desc); if (idx === -1) return null; return 'DEF-' + idx + '-' + roNumber; } // Resolves a default jobline's completion/lock record, preferring the // new stable id but transparently falling back to the legacy // index-based id if nothing's been written under the new id yet. This // is how existing completion history survives the PQIS insertion (and // any future reordering) without a manual data migration. function resolveDefaultRecord(map, desc, roNumber) { const stableId = defaultJoblineId(desc, roNumber); if (map[stableId]) return { id: stableId, record: map[stableId] }; const legacyId = legacyDefaultJoblineId(desc, roNumber); if (legacyId && map[legacyId]) return { id: stableId, record: map[legacyId] }; return { id: stableId, record: null }; } // ── ROUTER ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function doGet(e) { const action = e.parameter.action; // v3.9 — ÚNICA rota que devolve HTML em vez do JSON de sempre — sai // ANTES de tudo, já que respond() abaixo sempre envolve o retorno // como ContentService JSON. "completed" // (col H) is KEPT — it's what filters ROs out of the active list once // they're marked fechada/entregue upstream. // Normalise RO numbers for comparison: strip #, spaces, .0 → "29877" function normaliseRO(v) { return String(v).trim().replace(/^#/, '').replace(/\.0+$/, '').trim(); } // getCompletionMap() removed in v2.7.36 — read from an abandoned OI // source sheet (GID 610745324, column H) that nobody maintains // anymore since the real ENTREGA Agendamento flow replaced it. Was // silently excluding ROs from the PWA's own RO list based on // months-old stale data (confirmed case: RO 29871). ro.entregue // (built in getROs from getEntregueByPlateMap) is now the sole, // current signal for "has this vehicle actually been delivered". // ── RO-level approval rollup (v1.11) ────────────────────────────── // Reads the Aprovacoes tab ONCE and builds ro_number -> Set(job_description) // containing only joblines explicitly marked approved = TRUE. Used by // getROs() to show an "X/Y APROVADO" or "NÃO APROVADO" badge on the RO // list, since the per-jobline approval system (v1.7) removed the old // RO-level "authorized" tag and a tech had no way to see approval // status without opening the RO first. Mirrors the same Aprovacoes // source Patio already reads for its per-jobline ✅/❌ badges. // // IMPORTANT: Aprovacoes col B stores the jobline's job_description text // (NOT jobline_id) — saveApprovals() on the client keys its payload by // j.job_description (see apvCheckboxes in the PWA), and getApprovals() // already returns { jobline: , approved } on that basis. // This rollup must match on the same field or every comparison silently // fails and every RO falls through to "not_approved". // // IMPORTANT: default joblines (VAL de entrada, Checklist, etc. — the // DEFAULT_JOBLINES array) are never counted here, per spec — only rows // that exist in the Joblines sheet for that RO are eligible. function getApprovedJoblineSetByRO() { const map = {}; // ro_number -> Set(job_description) try { const data = SHEETS.aprovacoes.getDataRange().getValues(); if (data.length < 2) return map; data.slice(1).forEach(function(row) { const roNumber = String(row[0]).trim(); const jobDesc = String(row[1]).trim(); const approved = (row[2] === true || String(row[2]).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'); if (!roNumber || !jobDesc || !approved) return; if (!map[roNumber]) map[roNumber] = new Set(); map[roNumber].add(jobDesc); }); } catch(e) { Logger.log('getApprovedJoblineSetByRO error: ' + e); } return map; } // ── GET ROs ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ── Chegada-by-plate lookup for the RO list (v2.7.0) ───────────── // getROs() has no direct link to Agendamento — ROs are keyed by // ro_number, but a SERVICOS Agendamento event may exist with NO RO // number yet (Create Agendamento Path B), so matching by RO number // would silently miss exactly the appointments a tech most needs to // see. Plate is mandatory on every Agendamento event and present on // every RO row, so it's the reliable join key here. // // For each plate, multiple SERVICOS events can exist over time (the // same vehicle scheduled on different visits) — only ONE is relevant // "right now": whichever event's start time is closest to today // (today or the nearest day either direction), within a ±14-day // window so this never has to scan the whole calendar. That event's // own chegada tag is what gets surfaced; chegada is never merged or // inherited across events — each one is independent. // // v2.7.11: ALSO builds a parallel byRoNumber map, same "closest // event wins" rule, keyed by RO number instead of plate. This is a // fallback ONLY — used in getROs() only when an RO's own plate // field is blank (a real gap seen in practice: RO 29901's plate was // never populated by syncROs because the OI source system itself // never had a plate for that RO, yet the corresponding Agendamento // SERVICOS event DOES have a plate, since Agendamento lets a tech // type it in manually, independent of the OI sync). Without this // fallback, an RO with a blank plate can never show its chegada // status on the RO list/detail screens, even when a perfectly valid, // correctly-tagged Agendamento event exists for it. The plate-based // match remains the PRIMARY and preferred path — this fallback never // overrides a real plate match, it only fires when there's nothing // to plate-match against in the first place. const CHEGADA_RO_MATCH_WINDOW_DAYS = 14; function getChegadaByPlateMap() { // v4.7.3 — passou a carregar event_id junto do booleano. O card da RO // sempre leu r.servicos_event_id (data-servicos-evt), mas getROs // NUNCA produziu esse campo: chegava vazio em toda entrega manual, // e por isso markEntregaSemAgendamento nunca limpava a tag de chegada // do evento SERVICOS — o carro continuava no painel Veículos no Pátio // depois de entregue. Este é o produtor que faltava. const byPlate = {}; // plate (normalised) -> { chegada, distanceMs, event_id, start } const byRoNumber = {}; // ro_number (normalised) -> idem try { const cal = getAgendaCalendar('servicos'); const start = new Date(Date.now() - CHEGADA_RO_MATCH_WINDOW_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const end = new Date(Date.now() + CHEGADA_RO_MATCH_WINDOW_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const now = Date.now(); const events = cal.getEvents(start, end); events.forEach(function(ev) { const obj = agendaEventToObj(ev, 'servicos'); const distanceMs = Math.abs(ev.getStartTime().getTime() - now); const plate = normalisePlate(obj.plate); if (plate) { const existing = byPlate[plate]; if (!existing || distanceMs < existing.distanceMs) { // v4.7.z — start junto: é o horário agendado, usado pela faixa // Chegando do Patio pra separar "ainda vai chegar" de // "atrasado e sem confirmação". byPlate[plate] = { chegada: obj.chegada, distanceMs: distanceMs, event_id: obj.id, start: ev.getStartTime().toISOString() }; } } const roNum = normaliseRO(obj.ro_number); if (roNum) { const existingRo = byRoNumber[roNum]; if (!existingRo || distanceMs < existingRo.distanceMs) { byRoNumber[roNum] = { chegada: obj.chegada, distanceMs: distanceMs, event_id: obj.id, start: ev.getStartTime().toISOString() }; } } }); } catch (e) { Logger.log('getChegadaByPlateMap error: ' + e); } return { byPlate: byPlate, byRoNumber: byRoNumber }; } // ── Entregue-by-plate lookup for the RO list (v2.7.12) ──────────── // Mirrors getChegadaByPlateMap() exactly, but queries the ENTREGA // calendar instead of SERVICOS, and reads each event's entregue tag // instead of chegada. Same plate-primary/RO-number-fallback design, // same ±14-day window, same independence rule (never merged or // inherited across events — only the single closest-to-today ENTREGA // event for a given plate/RO is consulted). Added specifically so // getROs() can expose ro.entregue alongside ro.chegada, for any // external consumer (e.g. Patio View) that wants both states without // re-implementing this matching logic in a separate Apps Script // project. function getEntregueByPlateMap() { // v4.7.1 — passou a carregar event_id / manual_entrega / entrega_start_ms // além do booleano, para getROs poder devolver a pill de reverter a // partir do SERVIDOR (antes só existia no localStorage de quem clicou). const byPlate = {}; // plate (normalised) -> { entregue, entrega_date, distanceMs, event_id, manual_entrega, entrega_start_ms } const byRoNumber = {}; // ro_number (normalised) -> idem try { const cal = getAgendaCalendar('entrega'); const start = new Date(Date.now() - CHEGADA_RO_MATCH_WINDOW_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const end = new Date(Date.now() + CHEGADA_RO_MATCH_WINDOW_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const now = Date.now(); const events = cal.getEvents(start, end); events.forEach(function(ev) { const obj = agendaEventToObj(ev, 'entrega'); const distanceMs = Math.abs(ev.getStartTime().getTime() - now); const plate = normalisePlate(obj.plate); if (plate) { const existing = byPlate[plate]; if (!existing || distanceMs < existing.distanceMs) { byPlate[plate] = { entregue: obj.entregue, entrega_date: obj.start, distanceMs: distanceMs, event_id: obj.id, manual_entrega: obj.manual_entrega, entrega_start_ms: ev.getStartTime().getTime() }; } } const roNum = normaliseRO(obj.ro_number); if (roNum) { const existingRo = byRoNumber[roNum]; if (!existingRo || distanceMs < existingRo.distanceMs) { byRoNumber[roNum] = { entregue: obj.entregue, entrega_date: obj.start, distanceMs: distanceMs, event_id: obj.id, manual_entrega: obj.manual_entrega, entrega_start_ms: ev.getStartTime().getTime() }; } } }); } catch (e) { Logger.log('getEntregueByPlateMap error: ' + e); } return { byPlate: byPlate, byRoNumber: byRoNumber }; } // v3.6.5 — reads NEW's RepairOrders status column directly, same // constants Code.gs already defined for updateNewRepairOrderStatus_ // (v3.4.8). Separate, standalone copy of the same read Capacidade.gs // v3.7 already does — deliberately NOT sharing code with that file // tonight, since Capacidade.gs's version just got confirmed against // real production data (RO 28638, 106 total ROs caught) and touching // it again for a refactor carries more risk than ~15 duplicated // lines. Real motivating case: ROs marked "Entregue" in NEW/OLD for // months, but the OI→NEW→OLD→PWA sync never propagated that to this // PWA's own ROs tab — same root cause, same fix, applied here to the // tech-facing RO list instead of the capacity engine. function getNewStatusMap() { var map = {}; try { var newSS = SpreadsheetApp.openById(NEW_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID); var newSheet = newSS.getSheetByName(NEW_DB_MAIN_OS_SHEET); if (!newSheet) { Logger.log('getNewStatusMap: sheet "' + NEW_DB_MAIN_OS_SHEET + '" não encontrada em NEW.'); return map; } var lastRow = newSheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return map; var ids = newSheet.getRange(2, NEW_DB_OS_COL_ID, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues(); var situacoes = newSheet.getRange(2, NEW_DB_OS_COL_SITUACAO, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues(); for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) { var ro = normaliseRO(ids[i][0]); if (ro) map[ro] = String(situacoes[i][0]).trim().toLowerCase(); } } catch (e) { Logger.log('getNewStatusMap: falhou, seguindo sem esse sinal — ' + e); } return map; } // v3.6.5 — tech param added. Techs get the NEW-status filter applied // (see getNewStatusMap above); Approvers don't, per Eytan: an // Approver checking RO history needs to be able to find any RO, // including long-delivered ones, for auditing/verification — the // filter existing at all is specifically so a regular tech's daily // list isn't cluttered with vehicles that are already gone. Patio's // existing includeEntregue=1 also bypasses it, same as it already // bypasses the entregue filter below — same intent either way // ("give me everything"), no reason for the two flags to disagree. function getROs(includeEntregue, tech) { const data = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const rows = data.slice(1).map(r => rowToObj(headers, r)); const locks = getLockMap(); const compDef = getCompletedDefaultsMap(); const approvedByRO = getApprovedJoblineSetByRO(); const chegadaMaps = getChegadaByPlateMap(); const entregueMaps = getEntregueByPlateMap(); // v3.7.13 — durable, per-RO PATIO tab (see PATIO TAB block). Checked // FIRST in the chegada computation below, ahead of the plate/date // calendar heuristic — this is the fix for ROs silently missing // "NO PÁTIO" status. The old heuristic remains as a fallback only, // for ROs that haven't had a toggle fire since v3.7.13 shipped (run // backfillPatioFromExistingSignals() once to close that gap). const patioMap = getPatioMap(); // v3.2.0: priority number for the RO card (see readPriorityMap / // Priorities tab / setPriority). Read once here rather than a // per-RO getPriority() call — same batching pattern as every other // map above. const priorityMap = readPriorityMap(); // Read joblines sheet ONCE → map by ro_number const jlData = SHEETS.joblines.getDataRange().getValues(); const jlHeaders = jlData[0]; const jlByRO = {}; jlData.slice(1).forEach(function(r) { const obj = rowToObj(jlHeaders, r); const key = String(obj.ro_number); if (!jlByRO[key]) jlByRO[key] = []; jlByRO[key].push(obj); }); rows.forEach(function(ro) { const joblines = jlByRO[String(ro.ro_number)] || []; const doneSpec = joblines.filter(function(j) { return j.completion_pct >= 100; }).length; const doneDef = DEFAULT_JOBLINES.filter(function(desc) { const resolved = resolveDefaultRecord(compDef, desc, ro.ro_number); return resolved.record && resolved.record.completion_pct >= 100; }).length; ro.total_joblines = joblines.length + DEFAULT_JOBLINES.length; ro.done_joblines = doneSpec + doneDef; const lock = locks[ro.ro_number]; ro.lock_status = lock ? lock.status : 'free'; ro.locked_by = lock && lock.status === 'locked' ? lock.tech : ''; // v2.7.36: removed the old "completed" lookup from the legacy // OI source sheet (GID 610745324) — confirmed abandoned, nobody // maintains it anymore since the real ENTREGA Agendamento flow // replaced it. It was silently hiding ROs with stale entries // there (e.g. RO 29871 — open, active work logged, but excluded // from the PWA's own RO list because of months-old data in a // sheet nothing writes to anymore). ro.entregue (below) is the // real, current signal for "has this vehicle actually been // delivered" — see getEntregueByPlateMap. // Chegada — matched primarily by plate against the closest-to- // today SERVICOS Agendamento event (see getChegadaByPlateMap // above). v2.7.11: if the RO's own plate field is blank (a real // gap — e.g. the OI source never had a plate for this RO, seen // in practice with RO 29901), falls back to matching by RO // NUMBER instead, since the Agendamento event may still have a // real, manually-entered plate even when the ROs tab doesn't. // false if no match exists via either path within the window. const roPlateNormalised = normalisePlate(ro.plate); let chegadaMatch = chegadaMaps.byPlate[roPlateNormalised]; if (!chegadaMatch && !roPlateNormalised) { chegadaMatch = chegadaMaps.byRoNumber[normaliseRO(ro.ro_number)]; } // v2.7.47 — manual_patio (see setManualPatio) is a one-time // migration override for old ROs with no reachable/useful // SERVICOS event: ORed in here so a manually-tagged RO shows // 🅿️ NO PÁTIO identically to a calendar-confirmed one, everywhere // ro.chegada is read — RO list, Veículos no Pátio, Gantt, AND the // Patio dashboard (which reads ro.chegada from this same // endpoint — no separate Patio-side change needed). ro.manual_patio // is also exposed on its own so the PWA can show/toggle the // correct button state without re-deriving it. const manualPatioOn = ro.manual_patio === true || String(ro.manual_patio).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'; ro.manual_patio = manualPatioOn; // EM_ESPERA — manual hold set by an Aprovador (see setHold below). // hold_reason/hold_note/hold_since/hold_by already ride along via // rowToObj's dynamic header mapping; only the boolean needs // normalising, because Sheets returns it as true or as the string // "TRUE" depending on how the cell was written — same reason // manual_patio has the line above. // // Exposed here deliberately: the Patio dashboard reads hold_flag // from THIS endpoint rather than from ROs.capacity_flag, because // capacity_flag is only rewritten when the capacity engine runs, // and alocarCapacidadeHoraria() has a business-day guard — it does // not run on weekends. A hold set on Saturday would be invisible on // the board until Monday. This path is instant. // // Note the engine itself does NOT read from here: capCalcularAlocacao // reads the hold_flag column straight off the ROs sheet. ro.hold_flag = ro.hold_flag === true || String(ro.hold_flag).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'; // Days on hold, computed server-side so neither the PWA pill nor the // Patio banner has to do date arithmetic. null when not held or when // hold_since is unparseable. ro.hold_days = null; if (ro.hold_flag && ro.hold_since) { const dhHold = new Date(ro.hold_since); if (!isNaN(dhHold.getTime())) ro.hold_days = Math.floor((new Date() - dhHold) / 86400000); } // v3.7.13 — durable PATIO tab checked FIRST: a direct ro_number // lookup, no plate/date guessing involved. Falls through to the // old calendar-heuristic + manual_patio OR only for ROs that have // no PATIO row yet (not backfilled, or a SERVICOS event that had // no RO number tagged at toggle time — see upsertPatioRow's // no-op-on-blank-ro_number comment). const patioEntry = patioMap.byRoNumber[normaliseRO(ro.ro_number)]; ro.chegada = (patioEntry ? patioEntry.in_patio : false) || (chegadaMatch ? chegadaMatch.chegada : false) || manualPatioOn; // v4.7.3 — id do evento SERVICOS casado acima. O card da RO já // lia este campo desde a v4.1.2 (data-servicos-evt) e recebia // sempre string vazia, porque ninguém o produzia. ro.servicos_event_id = chegadaMatch ? (chegadaMatch.event_id || '') : ''; // v4.7.z — horário do evento SERVICOS casado acima, ISO. Vazio // quando não há evento casado (RO sem agendamento nenhum na janela // de ±14 dias). Consumidor previsto: a faixa Chegando do Patio. ro.servicos_event_start = chegadaMatch ? (chegadaMatch.start || '') : ''; // Entregue — same plate-primary/RO-number-fallback design as // chegada above, but matched against ENTREGA Agendamento events // instead of SERVICOS (see getEntregueByPlateMap, v2.7.12). // Added so external consumers (e.g. Patio View) can show both // states without re-implementing this matching logic themselves. // v2.7.36: this is now ALSO the authoritative signal for // excluding delivered ROs from the PWA's own RO list (see the // filter below) — replacing the abandoned legacy sheet check. let entregueMatch = entregueMaps.byPlate[roPlateNormalised]; if (!entregueMatch && !roPlateNormalised) { entregueMatch = entregueMaps.byRoNumber[normaliseRO(ro.ro_number)]; } ro.entregue = entregueMatch ? entregueMatch.entregue : false; // v3.2.0: actual scheduled delivery date/time (ISO string), not // just the entregue boolean above — null when no ENTREGA // Agendamento event has been created for this RO yet. Reuses the // same plate-primary/RO-number-fallback match already computed // for ro.entregue immediately above, so this never does a second, // separate lookup. ro.entrega_date = entregueMatch ? (entregueMatch.entrega_date || null) : null; // v4.7.1 — identidade do evento de ENTREGA e se ele foi criado // manualmente pela pill ENTREGAR AGORA. O PWA usa manual_entrega_ // event_id para desenhar a pill "REVERTER ENTREGA" em QUALQUER // aparelho e para QUALQUER Aprovador — o localStorage antigo só // funcionava no aparelho que clicou, e nem lá, porque a RO saía // da lista antes de renderizar de novo. ro.manual_entrega = entregueMatch ? !!entregueMatch.manual_entrega : false; ro.manual_entrega_event_id = (entregueMatch && entregueMatch.manual_entrega) ? (entregueMatch.event_id || '') : ''; ro.entrega_age_ms = (entregueMatch && entregueMatch.entrega_start_ms) ? (Date.now() - entregueMatch.entrega_start_ms) : null; // v3.2.0: priority number shown on the RO card (see readPriorityMap // above) — null when this RO has no priority set, matching // getPriority()'s own null convention. const priorityKey = String(ro.ro_number).trim(); ro.priority = priorityMap[priorityKey] !== undefined ? priorityMap[priorityKey] : null; // Approval rollup — default joblines NEVER count toward this. // approval_total = joblines that exist in the Joblines sheet for this RO. // approval_approved = how many of those have an approved=TRUE row in // Aprovacoes, matched by job_description (see getApprovedJoblineSetByRO). const approvedSet = approvedByRO[String(ro.ro_number)] || new Set(); const approvalTotal = joblines.length; const approvalApproved = joblines.filter(function(j) { return approvedSet.has(String(j.job_description).trim()); }).length; ro.approval_total = approvalTotal; ro.approval_approved = approvalApproved; ro.approval_status = approvalTotal === 0 ? 'none' // no eligible joblines at all — no badge : (approvalApproved > 0 ? 'approved' : 'not_approved'); }); // v3.3.0: includeEntregue lets a caller (currently: the Patio // project's getChegadaStatusForROs()) get the FULL list including // already-delivered ROs. Needed because Patio has no other way to // learn ro.entregue for a given RO — its only source for that flag // IS this endpoint, and once an RO is filtered out here, Patio can // never see the flag that would let it filter the RO out on its own // side either (see Patio Code.gs v1.7.1's getPatioData() exclusion, // which depends on exactly this flag being present in the response // it fetches). Default (false) is unchanged — the PWA app itself // always wants the filtered list, only Patio needs the exception. // ro.status === 'closed' is a SEPARATE, unrelated admin-closed state // and is never affected by this flag — only the entregue filter is // conditional. // // v3.6.5: third filter, tied to Approver status. isTechApprover // resolved ONCE here, not per-row — isApproverSync itself already // reads the whole Aprovadores sheet on every call, so calling it // inside the .filter() callback below would re-read that sheet once // per RO instead of once per request. const isTechApprover = tech ? isApproverSync(tech) : false; const bypassNewStatusFilter = includeEntregue || isTechApprover; const newStatusMap = bypassNewStatusFilter ? {} : getNewStatusMap(); // skip the NEW spreadsheet read entirely when it wouldn't be used anyway // v4.7.1 — janela de arrependimento (ver MANUAL_ENTREGA_REVERT_ // WINDOW_HOURS). Só Aprovador, só entrega manual, só recente. const manualRevertWindowMs = MANUAL_ENTREGA_REVERT_WINDOW_HOURS * 60 * 60 * 1000; // v4.8.0 — tecnico de estetica so ve RO que tenha ao menos uma // jobline de estetica. Calculado uma vez, fora do filtro. const soEstetica = tech ? isEsteticaTechSync_(tech) : false; return rows.filter(function(ro) { if (ro.status === 'closed') return false; if (soEstetica) { var jls = jlByRO[String(ro.ro_number)] || []; var temEstetica = jls.some(isEsteticaJobline_); if (!temEstetica) return false; } if (ro.entregue && !includeEntregue) { const dentroDaJanela = isTechApprover && ro.manual_entrega && ro.entrega_age_ms !== null && ro.entrega_age_ms >= 0 && ro.entrega_age_ms <= manualRevertWindowMs; if (!dentroDaJanela) return false; } if (!bypassNewStatusFilter) { var newSit = newStatusMap[normaliseRO(ro.ro_number)]; if (newSit === 'entregue' || newSit === 'fechada') return false; } return true; }); } // ── Global jobline-description exclusion rule, shared by getJoblines // (tech-facing PWA UI) and getGanttDataForRO (Gantt viewer feed) — // extracted to one place (was nested inside getJoblines only) so both // surfaces always apply the exact same rule. Skips customer-complaint // rows ("cliente reclama de...") and email-address artifacts that // sometimes slip into the OI source as a jobline description. // ── ESTETICA (v4.8.0) ──────────────────────────────────────────── // Papel lido da aba EsteticaTecnicos, mesmo padrao de isApprover. // Aba ausente ou vazia => ninguem e tecnico de estetica, e getROs // se comporta exatamente como na 4.7.7 (nenhuma RO some). function isEsteticaTechSync_(tech) { try { if (!tech) return false; var sh = SHEETS.esteticaTecnicos; if (!sh) return false; var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); var alvo = String(tech).trim().toLowerCase(); for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][0]).trim().toLowerCase() === alvo) return true; } return false; } catch (e) { Logger.log('isEsteticaTechSync_ error: ' + e); return false; // erro nunca esconde RO de ninguem } } function isEsteticaTech(tech) { return { ok: true, isEsteticaTech: isEsteticaTechSync_(tech) }; } // Uma jobline e de estetica se o codigo estiver cadastrado em // estetica_labour (fonte de verdade, ver EsteticaLabour.gs) OU se a // descricao casar uma palavra-chave. O segundo ramo existe porque as // ROs abertas hoje foram lancadas antes dos codigos existirem. function isEsteticaJobline_(jobline) { if (!jobline) return false; if (typeof estIsEstetica_ === 'function' && estIsEstetica_(jobline.jobline_id)) return true; if (typeof estPareceEstetica_ === 'function') return estPareceEstetica_(jobline.job_description); return false; } function isExcludedGlobally(description) { var d = String(description).toLowerCase().trim(); if (d.indexOf('cliente reclama') !== -1) return true; if (/\S+@\S+\.\S+/.test(d)) return true; return false; } // ── GET joblines for a specific RO ─────────────────────────────── function getJoblines(roNumber, tech) { const sheetJoblines = getJoblineRows(roNumber); const locks = getLockMap(); const lastTechs = getLastTechMap(); const compDef = getCompletedDefaultsMap(); sheetJoblines.forEach(function(r) { const l = locks[r.jobline_id] || {}; r.lock_status = l.status || 'free'; r.locked_by = l.status !== 'free' ? (l.tech || '') : ''; r.last_tech = lastTechs[r.jobline_id] || ''; r.is_default = false; }); const defaults = DEFAULT_JOBLINES.map(function(desc) { const resolvedComp = resolveDefaultRecord(compDef, desc, roNumber); const id = resolvedComp.id; // always the new stable id — writes go here, completing the migration const comp = resolvedComp.record; const legacyId = legacyDefaultJoblineId(desc, roNumber); const lock = locks[id] || (legacyId ? locks[legacyId] : null) || {}; const isCompleted = comp && comp.completion_pct >= 100; return { jobline_id: id, ro_number: roNumber, job_description: desc, suggested_tech: '', completion_pct: comp ? comp.completion_pct : 0, elapsed_seconds: 0, type: 'default', is_default: true, lock_status: isCompleted ? 'free' : (lock.status || 'free'), locked_by: isCompleted ? '' : (lock.status === 'locked' ? lock.tech : ''), last_tech: comp ? comp.tech : (lastTechs[id] || (legacyId ? lastTechs[legacyId] : '') || ''), }; }); // ── Global exclusion rules (apply to ALL techs) — see top-level // isExcludedGlobally(), shared with getGanttDataForRO so both // surfaces apply the exact same rule. // ── Tech-based keyword filter (Default Assignments tab) ────── const assignments = getDefaultAssignmentsMap(); // Accept both "default" and "(default)" as valid spellings for the // special fallback row — a missing/extra parenthesis in the sheet // shouldn't silently break this and fall through to "show everyone". // Both are always excluded from the normal substring-match loop // below, regardless of which one is actually present in the sheet. const DEFAULT_ASSIGNMENT_KEYS = ['(default)', 'default']; const DEFAULT_ASSIGNMENT_KEY = assignments['(default)'] ? '(default)' : 'default'; function isAllowedForTech(description) { if (!tech) return true; const descLower = String(description).toLowerCase(); const matchedKeyword = Object.keys(assignments).find(function(kw) { return DEFAULT_ASSIGNMENT_KEYS.indexOf(kw) === -1 && descLower.includes(kw); }); if (matchedKeyword) return assignments[matchedKeyword].includes(tech); // No real keyword matched — fall back to the '(default)' row, if // configured. If that row doesn't exist at all, keep the original // behavior (show to everyone) rather than silently hiding every // unmatched jobline from every tech because of a missing config row. const fallback = assignments[DEFAULT_ASSIGNMENT_KEY]; if (!fallback) return true; return fallback.includes(tech); } const filteredDefaults = defaults.filter(function(d) { return !isExcludedGlobally(d.job_description) && isAllowedForTech(d.job_description); }); const filteredJoblines = sheetJoblines.filter(function(j) { return !isExcludedGlobally(j.job_description) && isAllowedForTech(j.job_description); }); return filteredDefaults.concat(filteredJoblines); } // ── POST lock ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── // v1.6: same tech can reclaim their own lock (phone restart scenario) function lockRO(body) { const existing = findLockRow(body.jobline_id); if (existing && existing.status === 'locked') { if (existing.tech === body.tech) { // Same tech reclaims — keep original start time, clear released_at updateLockRow(existing.rowIndex, [ body.ro_number, body.jobline_id, 'locked', body.tech, existing.started, '', ]); return { ok: true, resumed: true }; } return { ok: false, reason: 'already_locked', by: existing.tech }; } const row = [ body.ro_number, body.jobline_id, 'locked', body.tech, new Date().toISOString(), '', ]; existing ? updateLockRow(existing.rowIndex, row) : SHEETS.lockStatus.appendRow(row); return { ok: true }; } // ── GET getMyActiveLock (v3.0.0) ────────────────────────────────── // Resume-on-restart support. Scans Lock Status for a row this tech // currently holds as 'locked' (app was killed/restarted mid-task — // same scenario lockRO()'s v1.6 "phone restart" branch already // handles for the single-RO case, just never surfaced anywhere else // in the app). Returns everything the client needs to either resume // straight into the timer screen or drop straight into the pause // screen WITHOUT re-fetching the RO/jobline list first — the tech // never has to pick the RO or task again either way. // // A tech should only ever hold one locked row at a time in normal // use (starting a new task requires releasing/pausing the previous // one first), so the first match found is returned. If Lock Status // somehow has more than one 'locked' row for the same tech (e.g. from // a bug or manual sheet edit), only the first is surfaced — this is // a recovery aid, not a full audit tool. function getMyActiveLock(tech) { if (!tech) return { ok: true, found: false }; const lockData = SHEETS.lockStatus.getDataRange().getValues(); let match = null; for (let i = 1; i < lockData.length; i++) { const row = lockData[i]; if (row[2] === 'locked' && String(row[3]) === String(tech)) { match = { ro_number: row[0], jobline_id: row[1], tech: row[3], started: row[4] }; break; } } if (!match) return { ok: true, found: false }; // Resolve job_description + is_default. Default joblines never have // their own Joblines-tab row — their id is derived purely from the // description (see defaultJoblineId) — so they're matched by // recomputing that id for each DEFAULT_JOBLINES entry rather than a // sheet lookup. const isDefault = String(match.jobline_id).indexOf('DEF-') === 0; let jobDescription = ''; if (isDefault) { const found = DEFAULT_JOBLINES.find(function(desc) { return defaultJoblineId(desc, match.ro_number) === match.jobline_id; }); jobDescription = found || ''; } else { const jl = getJoblineRows(match.ro_number).find(function(j) { return String(j.jobline_id) === String(match.jobline_id); }); jobDescription = jl ? jl.job_description : ''; } // Resolve RO display fields (vehicle/plate/vin/complaint/cust_name) // so the client can render the confirm modal and jump straight to // the timer/pause screen without a second round-trip. const roData = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const roHeaders = roData[0]; const roRow = roData.slice(1) .map(function(r) { return rowToObj(roHeaders, r); }) .find(function(r) { return String(r.ro_number) === String(match.ro_number); }); return { ok: true, found: true, ro_number: match.ro_number, jobline_id: match.jobline_id, job_description: jobDescription, is_default: isDefault, started: match.started, // ISO string — client computes elapsed = now - started vehicle: roRow ? roRow.vehicle : '', plate: roRow ? roRow.plate : '', vin: roRow ? roRow.vin : '', complaint: roRow ? roRow.complaint : '', cust_name: roRow ? roRow.cust_name : '', }; } // ── POST release ───────────────────────────────────────────────── function releaseRO(body) { const newStatus = body.pct >= 100 ? 'free' : 'paused'; const existing = findLockRow(body.jobline_id); if (!existing) return { ok: false, reason: 'lock_not_found' }; updateLockRow(existing.rowIndex, [ body.ro_number, body.jobline_id, newStatus, newStatus === 'paused' ? body.tech : '', existing.started, new Date().toISOString(), ]); if (!body.is_default) { updateCompletion(body.jobline_id, body.pct, body.elapsed_seconds); } return { ok: true, status: newStatus }; } // ── POST complete_default ──────────────────────────────────────── function completeDefault(body) { ensureCompletedDefaultsHeaders(); const map = getCompletedDefaultsMap(); const comp = map[body.jobline_id]; if (comp) { const data = SHEETS.completedDefaults.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('jobline_id'); data.slice(1).forEach(function(r, i) { if (String(r[idCol]) === String(body.jobline_id)) { SHEETS.completedDefaults.getRange(i + 2, 1, 1, 6).setValues([[ body.ro_number, body.jobline_id, body.job_description, body.tech, new Date().toISOString(), body.pct, ]]); } }); } else { SHEETS.completedDefaults.appendRow([ body.ro_number, body.jobline_id, body.job_description, body.tech, new Date().toISOString(), body.pct, ]); } return { ok: true }; } // ── POST log_work ──────────────────────────────────────────────── function logWork(body) { ensureWorkLogHeaders(); const row = [ new Date().toISOString(), body.tech || '', body.ro_number || '', body.jobline_id || '', body.job_description || '', body.event || '', body.pct || 0, body.elapsed_seconds || 0, body.gps_start || '', body.gps_stop || '', body.app_version || '', ]; SHEETS.workLog.appendRow(row); return { ok: true }; } // ── POST upload_media → Dropbox ────────────────────────────────── function uploadMedia(body) { const roNum = String(body.ro_number).replace('#', ''); const roInt = parseInt(roNum); const floor1000 = Math.floor(roInt / 1000) * 1000; const floor100 = Math.floor(roInt / 100) * 100; const rangeL1 = '#' + floor1000 + ' a #' + (floor1000 + 999); const rangeL2 = '#' + floor100 + ' a #' + (floor100 + 99); const jobDesc = sanitiseName(body.jobline_description); const dropboxPath = '/CTPRJ_NEW/vag ros/' + rangeL1 + '/' + rangeL2 + '/#' + roNum + '/fotos/' + roNum + '_' + jobDesc + '/' + body.filename; const imageBytes = Utilities.base64Decode(body.data); const blob = Utilities.newBlob(imageBytes, 'image/jpeg', body.filename); const apiArgs = JSON.stringify({ path: dropboxPath, mode: 'add', autorename: true, mute: false, }); const response = UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://content.dropboxapi.com/2/files/upload', { method: 'post', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + getDropboxAccessToken(), 'Dropbox-API-Arg': apiArgs, 'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream', }, payload: blob.getBytes(), muteHttpExceptions: true, }); const result = JSON.parse(response.getContentText()); if (result.error_summary) { return { ok: false, error: result.error_summary, path: dropboxPath }; } return { ok: true, path: result.path_display, size: result.size }; } // ── GET dropbox token for direct client upload ─────────────────── function getDropboxTokenForClient() { const token = getDropboxAccessToken(); return { ok: true, token: token }; } // ── GET action=getChecklistUrl&ro_number=X ─────────────────────── // Called by Patio's patio.html when a tech clicks a green Checklist // chip. Looks up the /docs/ folder for the given RO in Dropbox (same // #thousand/#hundred range-bucketing that uploadMedia uses for /fotos/) // finds any file whose name starts with "checklist" (case-insensitive), // and returns a temporary Dropbox link (~4h validity) so the browser // can open the PDF directly — no Dropbox credentials needed client-side. // Returns { ok: false, error: '...' } if the folder doesn't exist or // contains no checklist file — Patio shows a brief toast in that case. // Path pattern: /CTPRJ_NEW/vag ros/#L1000 a #(L1000+999)/ // #L100 a #(L100+99)/#RO/docs/ function getChecklistUrl(roNumber) { try { const roNum = String(roNumber || '').replace('#', '').trim(); const roInt = parseInt(roNum, 10); if (!roNum || isNaN(roInt)) return { ok: false, error: 'Invalid RO number' }; const floor1000 = Math.floor(roInt / 1000) * 1000; const floor100 = Math.floor(roInt / 100) * 100; const rangeL1 = '#' + floor1000 + ' a #' + (floor1000 + 999); const rangeL2 = '#' + floor100 + ' a #' + (floor100 + 99); const docsPath = '/CTPRJ_NEW/vag ros/' + rangeL1 + '/' + rangeL2 + '/#' + roNum + '/docs'; const token = getDropboxAccessToken(); // List files in the /docs/ folder const listResp = UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://api.dropboxapi.com/2/files/list_folder', { method: 'post', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', }, payload: JSON.stringify({ path: docsPath, recursive: false }), muteHttpExceptions: true, }); const listData = JSON.parse(listResp.getContentText()); if (listData.error_summary) { return { ok: false, error: 'Pasta não encontrada: ' + docsPath }; } // Find any file starting with "checklist" (case-insensitive) const entries = listData.entries || []; const checklistFile = entries.find(function(f) { return f['.tag'] === 'file' && f.name.toLowerCase().indexOf('checklist') === 0; }); if (!checklistFile) { return { ok: false, error: 'Nenhum arquivo de checklist encontrado em ' + docsPath }; } // Get a temporary direct-content link (~4h validity). // Modern browsers open PDFs from direct URLs inline without // downloading — no sharing scope needed, no size limit. const linkResp = UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://api.dropboxapi.com/2/files/get_temporary_link', { method: 'post', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', }, payload: JSON.stringify({ path: checklistFile.path_lower }), muteHttpExceptions: true, }); const linkData = JSON.parse(linkResp.getContentText()); if (linkData.error_summary) { return { ok: false, error: 'Não foi possível gerar o link: ' + linkData.error_summary }; } return { ok: true, url: linkData.link, filename: checklistFile.name, path: checklistFile.path_display, }; } catch(e) { Logger.log('getChecklistUrl error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // Kept in place as a fallback per request; no longer called from the PWA UI. function getParts(roNumber) { const data = SHEETS.parts.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const rows = data.slice(1).map(function(r) { return rowToObj(headers, r); }); const roRows = rows.filter(function(r) { return String(r.ro_number) === String(roNumber); }); if (!roRows.length) return { pending: [], received: [] }; const row = roRows[0]; function parseParts(str) { if (!str) return []; const matches = String(str).match(/\[([^\]]+)\]/g); if (!matches) return []; return matches.map(function(m) { return m.replace(/^\[|\]$/g, '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean); } const allParts = parseParts(row.part_description); const receivedParts = parseParts(row.received); const pendingParts = allParts.filter(function(p) { return !receivedParts.includes(p); }); return { pending: pendingParts, received: receivedParts, total: allParts.length, }; } // ── GET dashboard ──────────────────────────────────────────────── function getDashboard() { const locks = getLockMap(); const data = SHEETS.joblines.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const byJobline = {}; data.slice(1).forEach(function(r) { const o = rowToObj(headers, r); byJobline[o.jobline_id] = o; }); const techMap = {}; Object.values(locks).forEach(function(l) { if (l.status === 'locked') { techMap[l.tech] = Object.assign({}, l, byJobline[l.jobline_id] || {}); } }); return techMap; } // ── JOBLINE APPROVALS ──────────────────────────────────────────── function isApprover(tech) { try { var data = SHEETS.aprovadores.getDataRange().getValues(); var names = data.slice(1).map(function(r) { return String(r[0]).trim().toLowerCase(); }); return { ok: true, isApprover: names.indexOf(String(tech).trim().toLowerCase()) > -1 }; } catch(e) { Logger.log('isApprover error: ' + e); return { ok: false, isApprover: false }; } } // ── AGENDAMENTO CAPACITY — Agendadores (v2.7.27) ────────────────── // Separate from Aprovadores (jobline-approval gatekeeping) — this is // specifically "who is allowed to create Agendamentos, and what color // represents them in the slot picker". Same 3 people as Aprovadores // today, but a deliberately distinct sheet/concept since the two // systems serve different purposes and may diverge later. // // AGENDA_SLOT_CAPACITY (was 1) now allows up to 3 Agendamentos in the // same slot — one per Agendador — instead of treating any single // existing event as fully blocking that slot. const AGENDA_SLOT_CAPACITY = 3; const AGENDADOR_COLORS = { 'matheus pego': '#2563EB', // blue 'sidney lubicz': '#E11D2E', // red 'eytan lubicz': '#16A34A', // green }; const AGENDADOR_COLOR_DEFAULT = '#6B7280'; // gray fallback — an event created by someone outside the 3 named Agendadores (legacy data, etc.) function isAgendadorSync(tech) { try { if (!SHEETS.agendadores) return false; var data = SHEETS.agendadores.getDataRange().getValues(); var names = data.slice(1).map(function(r) { return String(r[0]).trim().toLowerCase(); }); return names.indexOf(String(tech).trim().toLowerCase()) > -1; } catch(e) { Logger.log('isAgendadorSync error: ' + e); return false; } } function agendadorColor(tech) { var key = String(tech || '').trim().toLowerCase(); return AGENDADOR_COLORS[key] || AGENDADOR_COLOR_DEFAULT; } // v3.1.0 fix: detects when an automated NOT APPROVED reseed (see // syncJoblines() in the PWA sync project) has silently overridden a // real human approval decision for the same RO+jobline. Root cause is // fixed at the sync layer (dedup-before-seed), but this is a UI-side // backstop in case any duplicate ever slips through again — surfaces // a warning instead of silently resolving to whichever row happens to // be last in the sheet. // // Rule: warn on a jobline if its MOST RECENT Aprovacoes row was // written by 'system' AND a prior HUMAN (non-system) row exists for // that same RO+jobline AND its value disagrees with that human row's // value. A jobline with no prior human row at all (brand new, never // reviewed by anyone) never warns — that's just the normal // not-yet-approved state, not a conflict. function getApprovals(roNumber) { try { var data = SHEETS.aprovacoes.getDataRange().getValues(); if (data.length < 2) return []; // Group all rows for this RO by jobline (job_description), in // sheet order. NOTE (v3.7.3 correction — this comment previously // claimed Aprovacoes is append-only; it isn't); saveApprovals() // actually DELETES every existing row for an RO and rewrites all // its joblines fresh on every save (see that function's own // v3.7.3 comment for the full story and the fix that limits the // damage). Practical effect on the conflict-detection logic just // below: after any human save, at most one row per jobline // usually survives for that RO — the "multiple historical rows" // this warning logic was written expecting mostly only exists // when a system-seeded row (syncJoblines()) hasn't yet been // overwritten by a human saveApprovals() call. Not fixed here — // a separate, deeper question about this conflict-detection // mechanism itself, flagged rather than assumed to still work as // originally intended. var byJobline = {}; // job_description -> [{approved, approver}, ...] data.slice(1).forEach(function(row) { if (!row[1]) return; if (String(row[0]).trim() !== String(roNumber).trim()) return; var jobline = String(row[1]); if (!byJobline[jobline]) byJobline[jobline] = []; byJobline[jobline].push({ approved: (row[2] === true || String(row[2]).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'), approver: String(row[4] || '').trim(), }); }); var result = []; Object.keys(byJobline).forEach(function(jobline) { var rows = byJobline[jobline]; var last = rows[rows.length - 1]; var lastHuman = null; for (var i = rows.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { if (rows[i].approver.toLowerCase() !== 'system') { lastHuman = rows[i]; break; } } var warning = ( last.approver.toLowerCase() === 'system' && lastHuman !== null && lastHuman.approved !== last.approved ); result.push({ jobline: jobline, approved: last.approved, warning: warning, }); }); return result; } catch(e) { Logger.log('getApprovals error: ' + e); return []; } } function saveApprovals(body) { try { var sheet = SHEETS.aprovacoes; var roNumber = String(body.ro_number); var approvals = body.approvals || []; var approver = String(body.tech || ''); var now = new Date(); // v2.7.51 — was Utilities.formatDate(...,'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm'), // a Brazilian-format STRING that new Date(string) on the // Patio side silently misreads as US MM/DD/yyyy, swapping // day/month whenever both are <=12 (e.g. "02/07/2026" for // July 2 read back as February 7). A real Date object // stores as a native Sheets date-time, no re-parsing. // v3.7.3 — capture existing per-jobline state BEFORE deleting, so // a jobline whose approval value is UNCHANGED in this save keeps // its real original timestamp/approver instead of being silently // re-stamped with "now"/whoever's saving right now. Root cause // found while investigating why Capacidade.gs v3.10's "days since // approval" priority feature could give wrong answers: this // function previously deleted and rewrote EVERY jobline's row on // EVERY save of the approval panel for an RO, even joblines whose // value didn't change in that particular save — so approving // jobline B today would silently erase jobline A's true // month-old approval date too, just because they're on the same // RO. Per Eytan: history already lost this way can't be // recovered (the old rows are already gone) — this only protects // future saves from losing data the same way going forward. var all = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var existingByJobline = {}; // job_description -> {approved, timestamp, approver} for (var i = 1; i < all.length; i++) { if (String(all[i][0]).trim() !== roNumber) continue; var jobDescExisting = String(all[i][1]).trim(); existingByJobline[jobDescExisting] = { approved: (all[i][2] === true || String(all[i][2]).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'), timestamp: all[i][3], approver: all[i][4], }; } // Delete existing rows for this RO (reverse to preserve indices) for (var r = all.length - 1; r >= 1; r--) { if (String(all[r][0]).trim() === roNumber) sheet.deleteRow(r + 1); } // Append fresh rows — reusing the ORIGINAL timestamp/approver for // any jobline whose approved value is unchanged from before; only // a genuinely new decision (new jobline, or a flipped value) gets // stamped with now/the current approver. approvals.forEach(function(a) { var jobDesc = String(a.jobline); var existing = existingByJobline[jobDesc]; var unchanged = existing && existing.approved === a.approved; var rowTimestamp = unchanged ? existing.timestamp : now; var rowApprover = unchanged ? existing.approver : approver; sheet.appendRow([roNumber, jobDesc, a.approved, rowTimestamp, rowApprover]); }); return { ok: true, saved: approvals.length }; } catch(e) { Logger.log('saveApprovals error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── APROVACOES TIMESTAMP REPAIR (v2.7.51) ────────────────────── // One-time repair for rows written before the v2.7.51 fix, when // the Timestamp column held an ambiguous 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm' string // instead of a real Date. Two-phase, manual-run only: // 1. repairAprovacoesTimestampsDryRun() — analyzes every row, // logs what it WOULD change, writes NOTHING. Run this first // and review the log carefully. // 2. repairAprovacoesTimestampsApply() — re-runs the same // analysis and actually writes the corrected values. Only // run this after reviewing the dry run. // // Handles two storage cases, since it's not known in advance which // applies to any given row: // CASE A — cell is still a raw string ("02/07/2026 22:10"): the // format was always day/month/year by construction, so this is // parsed directly and unambiguous. Always safe to fix. // CASE B — cell is already a Date object, meaning Sheets itself // auto-converted the string on write. If BOTH the stored day // and month are <=12, the conversion is ambiguous and the same // day/month swap may have happened at write-time — this is // flagged as a CANDIDATE swap, not auto-applied, since a correct // Date could coincidentally also have day<=12. Rows where the // stored day is >12 are left alone (that value could only be a // valid day, never a month, so no swap could have occurred). // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function _analyzeAprovacoesTimestamps() { const sheet = SHEETS.aprovacoes; const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const fixes = []; // { row, ro, jobline, kind, oldValue, newDate } for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { const raw = data[r][3]; // Timestamp column if (!raw) continue; if (typeof raw === 'string') { // CASE A — expect 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm' const m = raw.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/); if (!m) continue; // doesn't match the expected pattern — leave alone, flag for manual look const day = parseInt(m[1], 10), month = parseInt(m[2], 10), year = parseInt(m[3], 10); const hh = parseInt(m[4], 10), mm = parseInt(m[5], 10); const newDate = new Date(year, month - 1, day, hh, mm); fixes.push({ row: r + 1, ro: String(data[r][0]).trim(), jobline: String(data[r][1]).trim(), kind: 'STRING (unambiguous)', oldValue: raw, newDate: newDate, }); } else if (raw instanceof Date) { // CASE B — already a Date. Only flag if day/month are both // <=12, the zone where a write-time locale swap is possible. const day = raw.getDate(), month = raw.getMonth() + 1; if (day <= 12 && month <= 12 && day !== month) { const swapped = new Date(raw.getFullYear(), day - 1, month, raw.getHours(), raw.getMinutes()); fixes.push({ row: r + 1, ro: String(data[r][0]).trim(), jobline: String(data[r][1]).trim(), kind: 'DATE (ambiguous — candidate swap, NOT auto-applied)', oldValue: raw, newDate: swapped, candidateOnly: true, }); } } } return fixes; } function repairAprovacoesTimestampsDryRun() { const fixes = _analyzeAprovacoesTimestamps(); Logger.log('═══ Aprovacoes timestamp repair — DRY RUN, nothing written ═══'); Logger.log('Found ' + fixes.length + ' row(s) to review.'); fixes.forEach(function(f) { Logger.log('Row ' + f.row + ' | RO ' + f.ro + ' | "' + f.jobline + '" | ' + f.kind); Logger.log(' old: ' + f.oldValue + ' → new: ' + f.newDate.toISOString()); }); const candidatesOnly = fixes.filter(function(f) { return f.candidateOnly; }).length; Logger.log('─────────────────────────────────────────────'); Logger.log((fixes.length - candidatesOnly) + ' unambiguous string fix(es) will be applied by repairAprovacoesTimestampsApply().'); Logger.log(candidatesOnly + ' ambiguous DATE candidate(s) found — these are NOT applied automatically.'); Logger.log('Review the CANDIDATE rows above manually before deciding whether to hand-correct them.'); } function repairAprovacoesTimestampsApply() { const sheet = SHEETS.aprovacoes; const fixes = _analyzeAprovacoesTimestamps().filter(function(f) { return !f.candidateOnly; }); Logger.log('═══ Aprovacoes timestamp repair — APPLYING ' + fixes.length + ' fix(es) ═══'); fixes.forEach(function(f) { sheet.getRange(f.row, 4).setValue(f.newDate); Logger.log('✅ Row ' + f.row + ' (RO ' + f.ro + ', "' + f.jobline + '") corrected → ' + f.newDate.toISOString()); }); Logger.log('Done. Re-run repairAprovacoesTimestampsDryRun() to confirm 0 unambiguous rows remain.'); } function _loadOpenedDateByRO() { const map = {}; // ro_number -> Date | null const data = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const roCol = headers.indexOf('ro_number'); const dateCol = headers.indexOf('opened_date'); if (roCol === -1 || dateCol === -1) return map; for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { const ro = String(data[r][roCol]).trim(); if (!ro) continue; const raw = data[r][dateCol]; const d = raw instanceof Date ? raw : new Date(raw); map[ro] = isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d; } return map; } // EDIT THIS to the actual date the Aprovacoes approval feature went // live for real use — no genuine approval timestamp can be earlier // than this, regardless of how old the RO itself is (an RO can // legitimately predate the app; an approval ACTION cannot). Default // below is a placeholder — confirm the real date before running. const APROVACOES_FEATURE_LAUNCH_DATE = new Date(2026, 4, 1); // May 1, 2026 (month is 0-indexed: 4=May) function _crossCheckAmbiguousCandidates() { const candidates = _analyzeAprovacoesTimestamps().filter(function(f) { return f.candidateOnly; }); const openedByRO = _loadOpenedDateByRO(); const confirmed = []; const unclear = []; candidates.forEach(function(f) { const opened = openedByRO[f.ro] || null; // The real floor is whichever is LATER: the RO's own opened_date // (if known) or the app's launch date — an approval can't predate // either one, but an old RO can legitimately predate the app. const floor = opened && opened > APROVACOES_FEATURE_LAUNCH_DATE ? opened : APROVACOES_FEATURE_LAUNCH_DATE; const floorLabel = (floor === opened) ? ('RO opened_date=' + opened.toISOString()) : ('app launch=' + floor.toISOString()); const storedIsImpossible = f.oldValue < floor; const swappedIsConsistent = f.newDate >= floor; if (storedIsImpossible && swappedIsConsistent) { confirmed.push(Object.assign({ floor: floor, floorLabel: floorLabel }, f)); } else { unclear.push(Object.assign({ reason: storedIsImpossible ? 'swap does not resolve the inconsistency vs ' + floorLabel : 'stored date is already consistent (' + floorLabel + ') — likely NOT corrupted, leave alone', floor: floor, floorLabel: floorLabel, }, f)); } }); return { confirmed: confirmed, unclear: unclear }; } function repairAprovacoesTimestampsDryRunV2() { const result = _crossCheckAmbiguousCandidates(); Logger.log('═══ Phase 2 cross-check — DRY RUN, nothing written ═══'); Logger.log('Floor in use: app launch = ' + APROVACOES_FEATURE_LAUNCH_DATE.toISOString() + ' (edit APROVACOES_FEATURE_LAUNCH_DATE if wrong)'); Logger.log(result.confirmed.length + ' CONFIRMED (swap resolves an impossible timeline):'); result.confirmed.forEach(function(f) { Logger.log(' Row ' + f.row + ' | RO ' + f.ro + ' | floor(' + f.floorLabel + ')' + ' | old=' + f.oldValue.toISOString() + ' (BEFORE floor — impossible) → new=' + f.newDate.toISOString()); }); Logger.log('─────────────────────────────────────────────'); Logger.log(result.unclear.length + ' UNCLEAR (left for manual review, NOT applied):'); result.unclear.forEach(function(f) { Logger.log(' Row ' + f.row + ' | RO ' + f.ro + ' | reason: ' + f.reason + ' | old=' + f.oldValue + ' | candidate new=' + f.newDate.toISOString()); }); Logger.log('─────────────────────────────────────────────'); Logger.log('repairAprovacoesTimestampsApplyV2() will write only the ' + result.confirmed.length + ' CONFIRMED row(s).'); } function repairAprovacoesTimestampsApplyV2() { const sheet = SHEETS.aprovacoes; const result = _crossCheckAmbiguousCandidates(); Logger.log('═══ Phase 2 cross-check — APPLYING ' + result.confirmed.length + ' CONFIRMED fix(es) ═══'); result.confirmed.forEach(function(f) { sheet.getRange(f.row, 4).setValue(f.newDate); Logger.log('✅ Row ' + f.row + ' (RO ' + f.ro + ') corrected → ' + f.newDate.toISOString()); }); Logger.log(result.unclear.length + ' row(s) left untouched — review manually if needed.'); Logger.log('Done.'); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── PARTS REQUESTS SYSTEM (v1.8) ────────────────────────────── // Aprovadores-only feature. Tracks parts purchase authorization // and status per part, per RO. Append-only audit log in the // PartsRequests tab — "current state" of a part = latest row // for that part_key, ordered by timestamp. // // PartsRequests columns: // A timestamp ISO string, server-set on every row // B ro_number // C part_key ro_number + '_' + part_code (or _MANUAL_ + ts) // D part_code CodigoDoItem from source sheet, blank if manual // E part_description // F quantity // G status On Stock | To Be Ordered | Ordered | Confirmed | Back Order | Delivered // H po_number // I date_ordered // J eta // K delivered_date // L tech Aprovador who made this change // M source auto | manual // // External source sheet (read-only, never written to): // Spreadsheet: 1GVjgwMXSELSdB5p4sJiN8Ffj4vm3UXDxq2ReVKwo4Bc // Sheet GID: 788820693 // Col A OS | Col B CodigoDoItem | Col C DescricaoDoItem | // Col D QuantidadeDoItem | ... | Col R "peca ? " (TRUE/FALSE) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const PARTS_SOURCE_SPREADSHEET_ID = '1GVjgwMXSELSdB5p4sJiN8Ffj4vm3UXDxq2ReVKwo4Bc'; const PARTS_SOURCE_SHEET_GID = 788820693; const PARTS_STATUSES = [ 'Not Authorized', 'On Stock', 'To Be Ordered', 'Ordered', 'Confirmed', 'Back Order', 'Delivered', ]; function ensurePartsRequestsHeaders() { const sheet = SHEETS.partsRequests; if (!sheet) return; if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) { sheet.appendRow([ 'timestamp', 'ro_number', 'part_key', 'part_code', 'part_description', 'quantity', 'status', 'po_number', 'date_ordered', 'eta', 'delivered_date', 'tech', 'source', 'received_quantity', ]); return; } // Migration: sheets created before v2.7.21 won't have received_quantity. // Add it as a new trailing column so existing rows/data are untouched — // rowToObj() reads by header name, so this is safe to append at the end. const headerRow = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0]; if (headerRow.indexOf('received_quantity') === -1) { sheet.getRange(1, headerRow.length + 1).setValue('received_quantity'); } } // Read the external parts source sheet, filtered to rows where column R = TRUE, // for a single RO. Returns [{ part_code, part_description, quantity }, ...] function getSourcePartsForRO(roNumber) { try { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(PARTS_SOURCE_SPREADSHEET_ID); var sheets = ss.getSheets(); var sheet = null; for (var i = 0; i < sheets.length; i++) { if (sheets[i].getSheetId() === PARTS_SOURCE_SHEET_GID) { sheet = sheets[i]; break; } } if (!sheet) { Logger.log('getSourcePartsForRO: source sheet GID not found'); return []; } var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var roKey = normaliseRO(roNumber); var out = []; // Columns (0-indexed): A=0 OS, B=1 CodigoDoItem, C=2 DescricaoDoItem, // D=3 QuantidadeDoItem, R=17 peca? for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { var row = data[r]; if (normaliseRO(row[0]) !== roKey) continue; var isPartRow = (row[17] === true || String(row[17]).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'); if (!isPartRow) continue; out.push({ part_code: String(row[1]).trim(), part_description: String(row[2]).trim(), quantity: row[3] || 1, }); } return out; } catch(e) { Logger.log('getSourcePartsForRO error: ' + e); return []; } } // Build a map of part_key → latest audit row (current state) for a given RO, // by reading all PartsRequests rows for that RO and keeping the most recent // per part_key. function getCurrentPartsStateForRO(roNumber) { ensurePartsRequestsHeaders(); const sheet = SHEETS.partsRequests; if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return {}; const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const map = {}; // part_key -> { ...obj, _ts: Date } data.slice(1).forEach(function(r) { const obj = rowToObj(headers, r); if (String(obj.ro_number) !== String(roNumber)) return; const ts = new Date(obj.timestamp); const existing = map[obj.part_key]; if (!existing || ts >= existing._ts) { obj._ts = ts; map[obj.part_key] = obj; } }); return map; } // Lazy auto-generation: if no PartsRequests rows exist yet for this RO, // seed one "To Be Ordered"-less initial row per source part — status left // blank/"To Be Ordered" default so Aprovadores can triage from scratch. // Only ever runs once per RO (checked via getCurrentPartsStateForRO). function ensurePartsGeneratedForRO(roNumber) { const existing = getCurrentPartsStateForRO(roNumber); if (Object.keys(existing).length > 0) return; // already generated (or has manual parts) const sourceParts = getSourcePartsForRO(roNumber); if (!sourceParts.length) return; ensurePartsRequestsHeaders(); const now = new Date().toISOString(); const rows = sourceParts.map(function(p) { const partKey = roNumber + '_' + p.part_code; return [ now, roNumber, partKey, p.part_code, p.part_description, p.quantity, '', '', '', '', '', 'system', 'auto', '', ]; }); if (rows.length) { SHEETS.partsRequests.getRange(SHEETS.partsRequests.getLastRow() + 1, 1, rows.length, rows[0].length) .setValues(rows); } } // ── doGet action=partsRequests&ro_number=X ─────────────────────── // Called ONLY when an Aprovador opens the Parts panel for a specific RO. // Lazily generates the parts list on first call, then returns current // state of every part for that RO plus a rollup summary. // Google Sheets auto-converts plain "YYYY-MM-DD" strings written into a // cell into real Date objects. When read back via getValues(), those // come back as JS Date objects, which JSON.stringify() turns into full // ISO timestamps (e.g. "2026-06-18T02:17:26.626Z"). This normalises any // date-like cell value (Date object OR string) back into a plain // "YYYY-MM-DD" string — the format requires — so // the client can both populate edit fields correctly AND reformat for // display (dd/MM/yyyy) wherever it's shown as text. function normaliseDateField(value) { if (!value) return ''; if (value instanceof Date) { return Utilities.formatDate(value, 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); } var str = String(value).trim(); var isoMatch = str.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/); if (isoMatch) return isoMatch[0]; // already yyyy-MM-dd (possibly with time — strip it) return str; // unrecognised format — leave untouched rather than guess } function getPartsRequests(roNumber) { try { ensurePartsGeneratedForRO(roNumber); const stateMap = getCurrentPartsStateForRO(roNumber); const parts = Object.keys(stateMap).map(function(key) { const p = stateMap[key]; const qty = p.quantity || 1; const receivedQty = (p.received_quantity === '' || p.received_quantity === undefined || p.received_quantity === null) ? null : Number(p.received_quantity); return { part_key: p.part_key, part_code: p.part_code || '', part_description: p.part_description || '', quantity: qty, status: p.status || '', po_number: p.po_number || '', date_ordered: normaliseDateField(p.date_ordered), eta: normaliseDateField(p.eta), delivered_date: normaliseDateField(p.delivered_date), tech: p.tech || '', source: p.source || 'auto', received_quantity: receivedQty, // Only meaningful once status === 'Delivered'. true = fully received // (received_quantity >= quantity), false = partial receipt. receipt_complete: (p.status === 'Delivered' && receivedQty !== null) ? (receivedQty >= qty) : null, }; }).sort(function(a, b) { return a.part_description.localeCompare(b.part_description); }); return { ok: true, ro_number: roNumber, parts: parts, summary: summarisePartsStatus(parts) }; } catch(e) { Logger.log('getPartsRequests error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e), parts: [], summary: { color: 'none', total: 0 } }; } } // Rollup rule: // red = any part Back Order, or still To Be Ordered / blank // partial = no red, but some part Delivered with a PARTIAL receipt // (received_quantity < quantity) — needs follow-up, distinct // from a clean in-progress order (yellow) or a fully done one (green) // yellow = no red/partial, but some part Ordered/Confirmed and not yet Delivered // green = all parts fully Delivered (or On Stock) // none = no parts at all // // awaiting_order is a SEPARATE, independent count (not a color tier) — // it tracks parts specifically in 'To Be Ordered' status (authorized for // purchase but not yet ordered). It still counts toward 'red' above for // the existing red tag/pill, but is also surfaced on its own so the // Patio dashboard can show a dedicated "Peças autorizadas" pill/filter // for parts managers, distinct from Back Order or untouched parts. function summarisePartsStatus(parts) { if (!parts.length) return { color: 'none', total: 0, red: 0, partial: 0, yellow: 0, green: 0, awaiting_order: 0 }; let red = 0, partial = 0, yellow = 0, green = 0, awaitingOrder = 0; parts.forEach(function(p) { if (p.status === 'To Be Ordered') awaitingOrder++; if (p.status === 'Back Order' || p.status === 'To Be Ordered' || !p.status) { red++; } else if (p.status === 'Delivered') { if (p.receipt_complete === false) partial++; else green++; // fully received, or On Stock-equivalent (no receipt tracking needed) } else if (p.status === 'On Stock') { green++; } else { yellow++; // Ordered, Confirmed } }); let color = 'green'; if (red > 0) color = 'red'; else if (partial > 0) color = 'partial'; else if (yellow > 0) color = 'yellow'; return { color: color, total: parts.length, red: red, partial: partial, yellow: yellow, green: green, awaiting_order: awaitingOrder }; } // ── doGet action=partsSummary&ro_numbers=A,B,C ─────────────────── // Lightweight batch summary for multiple ROs at once (Patio dashboard use). // Does NOT auto-generate parts lists — only summarises whatever already // exists in PartsRequests, so it stays cheap and never triggers the // external-sheet read for ROs an Aprovador hasn't opened yet. function getPartsSummaryBatch(roNumbersCsv) { try { const roNumbers = String(roNumbersCsv || '').split(',').map(function(s) { return s.trim(); }).filter(Boolean); if (!roNumbers.length) return {}; ensurePartsRequestsHeaders(); const sheet = SHEETS.partsRequests; const result = {}; roNumbers.forEach(function(ro) { result[ro] = { color: 'none', total: 0 }; }); if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return result; const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const latestByKey = {}; // ro_number -> { part_key -> latestObj } data.slice(1).forEach(function(r) { const obj = rowToObj(headers, r); const ro = String(obj.ro_number); if (roNumbers.indexOf(ro) === -1) return; if (!latestByKey[ro]) latestByKey[ro] = {}; const ts = new Date(obj.timestamp); const existing = latestByKey[ro][obj.part_key]; if (!existing || ts >= existing._ts) { obj._ts = ts; latestByKey[ro][obj.part_key] = obj; } }); Object.keys(latestByKey).forEach(function(ro) { const parts = Object.values(latestByKey[ro]).map(function(p) { const qty = Number(p.quantity) || 1; const receivedQty = (p.received_quantity === '' || p.received_quantity === undefined || p.received_quantity === null || p.received_quantity === null) ? null : Number(p.received_quantity); p.receipt_complete = (p.status === 'Delivered' && receivedQty !== null) ? (receivedQty >= qty) : null; return p; }); result[ro] = summarisePartsStatus(parts); }); return result; } catch(e) { Logger.log('getPartsSummaryBatch error: ' + e); return {}; } } // ── doPost action=savePartStatus ────────────────────────────────── // Appends ONE new audit row for a single part's updated status/fields. // Always append-only — never overwrites a previous row. function savePartStatus(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_approver' }; ensurePartsRequestsHeaders(); const status = PARTS_STATUSES.indexOf(body.status) > -1 ? body.status : ''; // Lock: once a part has moved past blank (purchase authorized), "Autorizada // a compra" (To Be Ordered) can no longer be re-selected — this would // revert/undo the approval. All other statuses (Pedido feito, Pedido // confirmado, Back order, Peça recebida) remain freely selectable, in // any order, as many times as needed — only the authorization step itself // is protected from being undone. const currentState = getCurrentPartsStateForRO(body.ro_number); const existing = currentState[body.part_key]; // 'Not Authorized' does NOT count as past authorization — can still be authorized later. const pastAuthorization = !!(existing && existing.status && existing.status !== 'Not Authorized'); if (pastAuthorization && status === 'To Be Ordered') { return { ok: false, error: 'status_locked' }; } // Required-field validation, mirrored server-side from the frontend so // the workflow can't be bypassed via a direct API call. if (status === 'Ordered') { if (!body.po_number || !String(body.po_number).trim()) return { ok: false, error: 'po_number_required' }; if (!body.date_ordered) return { ok: false, error: 'date_ordered_required' }; } if (status === 'Delivered') { if (!body.delivered_date) return { ok: false, error: 'delivered_date_required' }; if (body.received_quantity === '' || body.received_quantity === undefined || body.received_quantity === null) { return { ok: false, error: 'received_quantity_required' }; } } SHEETS.partsRequests.appendRow([ new Date().toISOString(), String(body.ro_number), String(body.part_key), body.part_code || '', body.part_description || '', body.quantity || 1, status, body.po_number || '', body.date_ordered || '', body.eta || '', body.delivered_date || '', String(body.tech || ''), body.source || 'auto', (status === 'Delivered') ? (body.received_quantity || 0) : '', ]); return { ok: true }; } catch(e) { Logger.log('savePartStatus error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── doPost action=saveBatchPartStatus ──────────────────────────────── // Saves multiple parts in one call — same validation/lock rules as the // individual savePartStatus, just looped. Returns { ok, saved, failed, // errors } so the frontend can confirm how many were updated. function saveBatchPartStatus(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_approver' }; const parts = body.parts; if (!Array.isArray(parts) || !parts.length) return { ok: false, error: 'no_parts_provided' }; var saved = 0, failed = 0, errors = []; parts.forEach(function(partBody) { partBody.tech = body.tech; const result = savePartStatus(partBody); if (result.ok) { saved++; } else { failed++; errors.push({ part_key: partBody.part_key, error: result.error }); } }); Logger.log('saveBatchPartStatus: ' + saved + ' saved, ' + failed + ' failed for RO ' + body.ro_number); return { ok: true, saved: saved, failed: failed, errors: errors }; } catch(e) { Logger.log('saveBatchPartStatus error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── doPost action=addManualPart ─────────────────────────────────── // Aprovador adds an ad-hoc part not present in the auto-generated list. function addManualPart(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_approver' }; ensurePartsRequestsHeaders(); const now = new Date().toISOString(); const partKey = String(body.ro_number) + '_MANUAL_' + Date.now(); const status = PARTS_STATUSES.indexOf(body.status) > -1 ? body.status : ''; SHEETS.partsRequests.appendRow([ now, String(body.ro_number), partKey, '', // no part_code for manual entries body.part_description || '', body.quantity || 1, status, body.po_number || '', body.date_ordered || '', body.eta || '', body.delivered_date || '', String(body.tech || ''), 'manual', '', // received_quantity — not applicable until Delivered ]); return { ok: true, part_key: partKey }; } catch(e) { Logger.log('addManualPart error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // Synchronous approver check reused by POST handlers (isApprover() returns // the same shape but is named for the GET route; this avoids confusion // since both are plain functions, not async). function isApproverSync(tech) { return isApprover(tech).isApprover === true; } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── REMARKS SYSTEM (v1.12, extended v1.13) ──────────────────── // Lets a tech attach a remark — text, or an audio recording — to the // jobline they're actively working on, from the active-timer screen. // Append-only audit log in the Remarks tab — every remark is its own // row, never overwritten, so a jobline can accumulate a full history // across multiple sessions/techs. // // Text remarks (v1.12): typed or dictated via Web Speech API. The PWA // uploads a standalone .txt copy to Dropbox, in the SAME folder used // for that jobline's photos/videos; this function only stores the // structured row. // // Audio remarks (v1.13): tech records up to 60s of audio (e.g. an // engine noise), optionally tapping a "Marcar narração" button partway // through to mark the moment they start narrating/describing what's // being captured. The PWA uploads the audio file itself to the SAME // Dropbox folder; this function stores metadata only (filename + // narration offset) — no transcription, no remark_text. // // Remarks columns: // A timestamp ISO string, server-set on every row // B ro_number // C jobline_id // D job_description // E tech // F remark_text text remarks only; blank for audio // G type 'text' | 'audio' // H filename audio remarks only; Dropbox filename // I narration_offset_seconds audio remarks only; blank if no marker set // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function ensureRemarksHeaders() { const sheet = SHEETS.remarks; if (!sheet) return; if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) { sheet.appendRow([ 'timestamp', 'ro_number', 'jobline_id', 'job_description', 'tech', 'remark_text', 'type', 'filename', 'narration_offset_seconds', ]); } } // ── doPost action=saveRemark ────────────────────────────────────── // Appends ONE new text remark row. Always append-only — never // overwrites or edits a previous remark, mirroring the PartsRequests // pattern. function saveRemark(body) { try { const remarkText = String(body.remark_text || '').trim(); if (!remarkText) return { ok: false, error: 'empty_remark' }; ensureRemarksHeaders(); SHEETS.remarks.appendRow([ new Date().toISOString(), String(body.ro_number || ''), String(body.jobline_id || ''), body.job_description || '', String(body.tech || ''), remarkText, 'text', '', '', ]); return { ok: true }; } catch(e) { Logger.log('saveRemark error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── doPost action=saveAudioRemark ────────────────────────────────── // Appends ONE new audio remark row (metadata only — the audio file // itself is uploaded directly to Dropbox by the client, same as // photos/videos). narration_offset_seconds is the elapsed time, in // seconds from recording start, when the tech tapped "Marcar // narração" — blank if they never tapped it. function saveAudioRemark(body) { try { const filename = String(body.filename || '').trim(); if (!filename) return { ok: false, error: 'missing_filename' }; ensureRemarksHeaders(); const offset = (body.narration_offset_seconds === '' || body.narration_offset_seconds === null || body.narration_offset_seconds === undefined) ?
