'' : Number(body.narration_offset_seconds); SHEETS.remarks.appendRow([ new Date().toISOString(), String(body.ro_number || ''), String(body.jobline_id || ''), body.job_description || '', String(body.tech || ''), '', 'audio', filename, offset, ]); return { ok: true }; } catch(e) { Logger.log('saveAudioRemark error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── doGet action=getRemarks&jobline_id=X ────────────────────────── // Returns the full remark history (text + audio) for one jobline, // oldest first. function getRemarks(joblineId) { try { ensureRemarksHeaders(); const sheet = SHEETS.remarks; if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return []; const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; return data.slice(1) .map(function(r) { return rowToObj(headers, r); }) .filter(function(r) { return String(r.jobline_id) === String(joblineId); }); } catch(e) { Logger.log('getRemarks error: ' + e); return []; } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── DOCUMENTOS DE ENTREGA (v2.7.7) ──────────────────────────── // Append-only audit log of "OS de Saída" / "Pagamento" document // scans, one row per upload (never edited/overwritten — re-scans // simply add a new row with a fresh timestamp). doc_type is always // exactly 'os_saida' or 'pagamento'. The actual PDF is uploaded // DIRECTLY to Dropbox by the client (get_dropbox_token pattern, // same as video/remarks/audio) — this tab only logs the resulting // path/metadata, mirroring how saveAudioRemark() works for audio // files above. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function ensureEntregaDocsHeaders() { const sheet = SHEETS.entregaDocs; if (!sheet) return; if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) { sheet.appendRow([ 'timestamp', 'ro_number', 'doc_type', 'dropbox_path', 'tech', ]); } } // ── doPost action=saveEntregaDocStatus ──────────────────────────── // body: { ro_number, doc_type ('os_saida'|'pagamento'), dropbox_path, tech } // Called by the client AFTER the PDF has already been uploaded // directly to Dropbox — this just records that it happened. Always // append-only; never overwrites a prior row for the same RO/doc_type // (a re-scan is simply a newer row — see getEntregaDocsStatus, which // always reads the MOST RECENT row per ro_number+doc_type as "current"). function saveEntregaDocStatus(body) { try { const roNumber = String(body.ro_number || '').trim(); const docType = String(body.doc_type || '').trim(); const path = String(body.dropbox_path || '').trim(); if (!roNumber) return { ok: false, error: 'missing_ro_number' }; if (docType !== 'os_saida' && docType !== 'pagamento') { return { ok: false, error: 'invalid_doc_type' }; } if (!path) return { ok: false, error: 'missing_dropbox_path' }; ensureEntregaDocsHeaders(); SHEETS.entregaDocs.appendRow([ new Date().toISOString(), roNumber, docType, path, String(body.tech || ''), ]); return { ok: true }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('saveEntregaDocStatus error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── doGet action=getEntregaDocsStatus&ro_number=X ───────────────── // Returns the CURRENT (most recent) os_saida/pagamento upload for // one RO, so the modal can show ✅/pending correctly even if reopened // later, or if OS de Saída and Pagamento are scanned by two different // people at different times. Either field is null if that doc_type // has never been scanned for this RO. function getEntregaDocsStatus(roNumber) { try { ensureEntregaDocsHeaders(); const sheet = SHEETS.entregaDocs; const result = { os_saida: null, pagamento: null }; if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return result; const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const ro = String(roNumber || '').trim(); // Walk top-to-bottom (oldest-first, since rows are append-only) // and keep overwriting — the LAST matching row wins, which is // automatically the most recent one. data.slice(1).forEach(function(row) { const obj = rowToObj(headers, row); if (String(obj.ro_number).trim() !== ro) return; if (obj.doc_type === 'os_saida' || obj.doc_type === 'pagamento') { result[obj.doc_type] = { dropbox_path: obj.dropbox_path, tech: obj.tech, timestamp: obj.timestamp, }; } }); return result; } catch (e) { Logger.log('getEntregaDocsStatus error: ' + e); return { os_saida: null, pagamento: null }; } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── AGENDAMENTO (SCHEDULING) SYSTEM (v2.1.0) ────────────────── // Aprovadores-only feature (same gate as jobline approvals / // Parts Requests — reuses isApprover()/isApproverSync(), no new // permission list). // // Two Google Calendars, accessed via the built-in CalendarApp // service (no new credentials needed): // SERVICOS — incoming/scheduled customers + vehicles (drop-off) // ENTREGA — vehicle delivery back to customer // // Both calendars: fixed 1-hour slots, Mon–Fri 9:00–18:00. This is // a consultant intake/handover model (one slot = one customer // appointment), NOT a technician work-block reservation system. // // ENTREGA creation is hard-gated: only allowed if a SERVICOS event // already exists for that RO number (checked via the event's // extended properties, see EVT_PROP_RO below). // // Customers tab (separate from ROs, synced on every OI sync run — // see Section 4 of the spec / hook point noted below): // A CPF.CNPJ | B Client Name | C Mobile | D Data de Nascimento | // E License | F Year | G Modelo | H VIN | I MFG date // // Data model notes: // - One row = one customer+vehicle pairing. Same CPF.CNPJ can // appear in multiple rows (multiple vehicles owned). // - Same License (plate) can also appear in multiple rows — // plates get reassigned when a vehicle is sold. Row order is // append-only/chronological, so when searching by License, // the LAST matching row is authoritative (most recent owner). // - Search by CPF/CNPJ or by name returns ALL matching rows. // - Name search is partial + accent-insensitive + fuzzy // (edit-distance), so "Luis" also surfaces "Luiz", etc. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const AGENDA_CALENDAR_IDS = { servicos: '0291f090617c6368586bc67bc3f7578a243d6e579a94dabe6e356b8777ee02eb@group.calendar.google.com', entrega: '3vql5ngludil5j5sl95s85qsd8@group.calendar.google.com', }; const AGENDA_BUSINESS_HOURS = { startHour: 9, endHour: 18 }; // Mon–Fri, 09:00–18:00 const AGENDA_SLOT_MINUTES = 60; // Extended-property keys stamped onto every Agendamento event, used // to recover structured data without re-parsing the title/description, // and to drive the ENTREGA hard-gate (lookup by ro_number + type). const EVT_PROP_RO = 'psr_ro_number'; const EVT_PROP_TYPE = 'psr_calendar'; // 'servicos' | 'entrega' const EVT_PROP_PLATE = 'psr_plate'; const EVT_PROP_CPF = 'psr_cpf'; const EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY = 'psr_created_by'; // Stamped on ENTREGA events only — direct pointer to the linked // SERVICOS event's calendar ID, set once at creation. ENTREGA's // vehicle/customer fields are NEVER stored independently; they're // always read live from this linked event (see entregaResolveLink // below), so a later correction on the SERVICOS side (e.g. fixing // the customer name) is automatically reflected, and a deleted // SERVICOS event is detected rather than silently producing stale // data. RO number and plate are treated as immutable by design, so // this link is matched by event ID, not by re-comparing RO numbers. const EVT_PROP_SERVICOS_EVENT_ID = 'psr_servicos_event_id'; // Stamped on SERVICOS events only — which of AGENDA_LOCATIONS was // picked at creation. ENTREGA events have no tag of their own; they // always resolve their location live from the linked SERVICOS // event's tag (same live-mirror pattern as client_name/modelo/etc). const EVT_PROP_LOCATION_KEY = 'psr_location_key'; // Stamped on SERVICOS events only — marks that the customer has // physically arrived at Porsche Service Rio (tech-confirmed via the // "Confirmar Chegada" toggle in Management). '1' = arrived, absent // or '0' = not arrived. ENTREGA events mirror this value for DISPLAY // purposes only in agendaEventToObj() (read-only there) — but as of // v2.7.0, confirming delivery (EVT_PROP_ENTREGUE below) actively // CLEARS this tag back to '0' on the linked SERVICOS event, since a // delivered vehicle is no longer on-site. That clear is one-way — // undoing an ENTREGUE confirmation does NOT restore chegada to '1'. const EVT_PROP_CHEGADA = 'psr_chegada'; // Stamped on SERVICOS events only, alongside EVT_PROP_CHEGADA — the // ISO timestamp of the moment chegada was confirmed (v2.7.36). Only // set when toggling TO arrived; left untouched when un-arriving, so // the last known arrival time survives an accidental un-toggle. Used // by getGanttDataForRO to surface "Data de entrada" on the Gantt. // Like all event tags, this is invisible in the Calendar UI — see // AgendaLog (SHEETS.agendaLog) for the durable, human-auditable copy. const EVT_PROP_CHEGADA_AT = 'psr_chegada_at'; // Zapier webhook fired every time chegada is toggled on/off, so other // automations (e.g. notifying the service advisor) can react. Separate // from WABA_ZAPIER_WEBHOOK_URL since this isn't a WhatsApp send. Also // reused (with a different "action" payload value) for ENTREGUE // toggles as of v2.7.0 — same downstream automations may care about // both arrival and delivery events. const CHEGADA_ZAPIER_WEBHOOK_URL = 'https://hooks.zapier.com/hooks/catch/924/42diy17/'; // Stamped on ENTREGA events only — marks that the vehicle has been // physically handed back to the customer (tech-confirmed via the // "Confirmar Entrega" toggle in Management). '1' = entregue, absent // or '0' = not yet. Independent per ENTREGA event — never mirrored // anywhere, since delivery is owned exclusively by the ENTREGA side. const EVT_PROP_ENTREGUE = 'psr_entregue'; // Stamped on ENTREGA events created via markEntregaSemAgendamento — // allows the reversal function to identify and delete only events // created this way, never accidentally touching normal ENTREGA events. const EVT_PROP_MANUAL_ENTREGA = 'psr_manual_entrega'; // v4.7.1 — Janela de arrependimento da entrega manual. // markEntregaSemAgendamento marca a RO como entregue, e o filtro de // getROs tira toda RO entregue da lista — inclusive para Aprovador. // Resultado: a RO sumia no mesmo instante em que a pill "REVERTER // ENTREGA" deveria aparecer, e não havia como desfazer um clique // errado por dentro do PWA. Dentro desta janela, e SÓ para Aprovador, // e SÓ para entrega criada manualmente, a RO continua na lista // carregando a pill de reverter. Passada a janela ela some como // qualquer outra RO entregue — a pill existe para corrigir engano na // hora, não para reabrir carro entregue semana passada. const MANUAL_ENTREGA_REVERT_WINDOW_HOURS = 48; // v4.7.2 — Estado ANTERIOR, carimbado no próprio evento de ENTREGA no // momento em que a pill ENTREGAR AGORA é clicada. // // markEntregaSemAgendamento limpa três coisas de uma vez: a linha do // PATIO, a tag de chegada do evento SERVICOS ligado, e a situação da // RO no NEW. O revert desfazia só a terceira — e mesmo essa à força, // gravando string vazia por cima do que houvesse lá. Resultado: carro // revertido voltava para a lista de ROs mas sumia do quadro do pátio, // e uma RO que já estava "Entregue" no NEW antes do teste perdia esse // status para sempre. // // Guardar o estado no evento (e não em planilha à parte) é de // propósito: o evento é exatamente o objeto que o revert deleta, então // os dois nunca podem se separar nem ficar órfãos um do outro. // // Eventos criados ANTES da v4.7.2 não têm estas tags. Nesse caso o // revert não inventa estado: restaura o que dá, devolve // prev_state_unknown:true e o PWA avisa o Aprovador do que ele precisa // reconferir na mão. const EVT_PROP_PREV_PATIO = 'psr_prev_patio'; // '1' | '0' const EVT_PROP_PREV_PATIO_SOURCE = 'psr_prev_patio_source'; // 'calendar' | 'manual' | '' const EVT_PROP_PREV_CHEGADA = 'psr_prev_chegada'; // '1' | '0' const EVT_PROP_PREV_NEW_STATUS = 'psr_prev_new_status'; // situação anterior no NEW // v4.7.3 — quarto sinal. ro.chegada é um OU de TRÊS fontes (linha do // PATIO, tag do evento SERVICOS, coluna manual_patio da aba ROs) e a // entrega manual só apagava as duas primeiras. Um carro marcado NO // PÁTIO pela ferramenta manual (setManualPatio) continuava com o selo // depois de entregue, porque a terceira fonte ninguém tocava. const EVT_PROP_PREV_MANUAL_PATIO = 'psr_prev_manual_patio'; // '1' | '0' // ── WHATSAPP / WABA MESSAGING (v2.5.0) ────────────────────────── // Apps Script POSTs to a Zapier "Catch Hook" webhook; Zapier relays // to WhatsApp Business API (WABA) using an already-approved message // template (text only — see WABA_TEMPLATE_NAME comment). Hardcoded // here intentionally, per spec, for easy later adjustment. // // Template (approved, 4 variables): // "Olá {{1}}, o agendamento do seu Porsche {{2}} está confirmado // para {{3}}. Segue endereço do agendamento: {{4}}" // {{1}} = client name, {{2}} = vehicle model, {{3}} = date/time, // {{4}} = address — tech-selectable between 2 pickup locations // (see AGENDA_LOCATIONS below) as of v2.5.3. Full address strings // intentionally include the location name + neighborhood/city/state // (not just street+number) since this string is what eventually // gets passed to Maps for geocoding — the more complete the string, // the more precisely Maps can locate it. const WABA_ZAPIER_WEBHOOK_URL = 'https://hooks.zapier.com/hooks/catch/924/422ir8z/'; // Pickup location options for Create SERVICOS. Keyed by a stable // short key (stored on the event) so the full address text can be // edited here later without breaking already-created events — the // event only stores the key, the display text is always resolved // fresh from this map. ENTREGA events have no dropdown of their own; // they always mirror their linked SERVICOS event's location key (see // agendaEventToObj's "ENTREGA events" live-mirror block). const AGENDA_LOCATIONS = { service_rio: { label: 'Porsche Service Rio', address: 'Porsche Service Rio, Estr. do Itanhangá, 1079 - Itanhangá, Rio de Janeiro - RJ, 22753-005', }, pre_owned_rio: { label: 'Porsche Pre-Owned Rio', address: 'Porsche Pre-Owned Rio, Estr. do Joá, 102 - São Conrado, Rio de Janeiro - RJ, 22611-022', }, }; const AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY = 'service_rio'; // Shared fixed floor for full-history SERVICOS lookups (v2.7.46) — // used by hasServicosEvent (the ENTREGA hard-gate) and the SERVICO // Management screen's "buscar histórico completo" fallback. A fixed // calendar date rather than a rolling "N days back" so it can never // again be too short for a long-open RO (see RO 28682, a real // SERVICOS event from 2025-06-25 that a 90-day rolling window // couldn't see). Predates any realistic Agendamento calendar data. const AGENDA_HISTORY_FLOOR = new Date(2020, 0, 1); function resolveAgendaLocationAddress(locationKey) { const loc = AGENDA_LOCATIONS[locationKey]; return loc ? loc.address : AGENDA_LOCATIONS[AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY].address; } // ── doGet action=getAgendaLocations ─────────────────────────────── // Returns the pickup-location dropdown options for the Create // SERVICOS form, sourced from AGENDA_LOCATIONS so the PWA never // hardcodes location keys/labels itself — adding a third location // later only requires editing AGENDA_LOCATIONS here, no PWA change. function getAgendaLocations() { const locations = Object.keys(AGENDA_LOCATIONS).map(function(key) { return { key: key, label: AGENDA_LOCATIONS[key].label }; }); return { ok: true, locations: locations, default_key: AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY }; } // ── APPOINTMENT CARD IMAGE GENERATION (htmlcsstoimage.com) ──────── // NOT yet wired into sendAgendaWhatsApp/the live messaging flow — // this is a standalone generator + a manual test function // (testGenerateAgendaCard) so the image-header WABA template can be // validated end-to-end (htmlcsstoimage.com → Zapier → WABA) before // being hooked into the real creation/reminder triggers. image_url). // // Credentials hardcoded here intentionally per project convention — // rotate the API key in the htmlcsstoimage.com dashboard if these // are ever exposed somewhere they shouldn't be. ENTREGA now uses green (#059669, // matching the "done"/"aprovado" color already used elsewhere in // this system — Patio View, the PWA's --green variable) and reads // "Veículo pronto para retirada". serviceText (ENTREGA only) shows // what was actually done to the vehicle — sourced from the RO's // complaint field via getAgendaServiceText() below — since that's // rarely known precisely at SERVICOS-creation time but IS known by // the time the vehicle is ready for pickup. function generateAgendaCardUrl(roNumber, clientName, vehicleModel, dateTimeLabel, address, cardType, serviceText) { try { const html = buildAgendaCardHtml(roNumber, clientName, vehicleModel, dateTimeLabel, address, cardType, serviceText); const response = UrlFetchApp.fetch(HCTI_ENDPOINT, { method: 'post', headers: { Authorization: 'Basic ' + Utilities.base64Encode(HCTI_USER_ID + ':' + HCTI_API_KEY) }, payload: { html: html, viewport_width: '1080', viewport_height: '1080' }, muteHttpExceptions: true, }); const code = response.getResponseCode(); const body = JSON.parse(response.getContentText()); if (code !== 200 || !body.url) { Logger.log('generateAgendaCardUrl: HCTI error (' + code + '): ' + response.getContentText()); return null; } return body.url; } catch (e) { Logger.log('generateAgendaCardUrl error: ' + e); return null; } } // Strips a leading "Cliente reclama de" (any case/spacing) from a // complaint string, same rule as Patio View's formatComplaint() — // kept consistent across both projects since they read the same // underlying ROs-tab field. Returns '' (not the raw prefix) if // nothing remains after stripping. function stripComplaintPrefix(text) { if (!text) return ''; let t = String(text).trim(); t = t.replace(/^clientes?\s+reclama\s+de\s*:?\s*/i, ''); if (!t) return ''; return t.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + t.slice(1); } // Looks up the RO's complaint field and returns it formatted for // display on the ENTREGA card (prefix-stripped, see above). Returns // '' if the RO isn't found or has no complaint on file — the card // simply omits the "Serviço Realizado" section in that case, see // buildAgendaCardHtml. function getAgendaServiceText(roNumber) { try { const ro = findROByNumber(roNumber); return ro ? stripComplaintPrefix(ro.complaint) : ''; } catch (e) { Logger.log('getAgendaServiceText error: ' + e); return ''; } } function buildAgendaCardHtml(roNumber, clientName, vehicleModel, dateTimeLabel, address, cardType, serviceText) { const esc = function(s) { return String(s || '').replace(/[&<>]/g, function(c) { return { '&':'&', '<':'<', '>':'>' }[c]; }); }; const isEntrega = String(cardType).toLowerCase() === 'entrega'; const accentColor = isEntrega ? '#059669' : '#D5001C'; const eyebrowText = isEntrega ? 'Veículo pronto para retirada' : 'Agendamento confirmado'; // ENTREGA-only "Serviço Realizado" section — same red-bar visual // treatment as the date/time block, just in the accent color, and // placed directly under the vehicle/plate line (above date/time), // per spec. Omitted entirely (not just blank) when there's no // complaint text on file for this RO, so the layout doesn't show // an empty bar with nothing next to it. const serviceBlock = (isEntrega && serviceText) ? '
' + '
Serviço realizado
' + '
' + esc(serviceText) + '
' + '
' : ''; return '' + '
' + '
' + eyebrowText + '
RO #' + esc(roNumber) + '
' + '
' + esc(clientName) + '
' + '
' + esc(vehicleModel) + '
' + serviceBlock + '
' + '
Data e horário
' + '
' + esc(dateTimeLabel) + '
' + '
' + '
' + '
Endereço
' + '
' + esc(address).replace(', ', '
') + '
' + '
📍 Ver no mapa
' + ''; } // ── ONE-TIME MANUAL TEST — run from the editor (function dropdown // → testGenerateAgendaCard → Run) to confirm htmlcsstoimage.com // generates the card correctly. Tests the SERVICOS (red) design — // see testGenerateEntregaCard below for the ENTREGA (green) one. // Logs the resulting image URL — open it in a browser to inspect // the rendered card, or paste it directly into Zapier's WABA action // step's image-URL field to test the full image-header template // delivery end to end. function testGenerateAgendaCard() { const url = generateAgendaCardUrl( '29890', 'Ariovaldo da Silva', 'Porsche Cayenne', formatAgendaDateTimePt(new Date(Date.now() + 2 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)), resolveAgendaLocationAddress('service_rio'), 'servicos' ); Logger.log('Generated card URL: ' + url); } // ── ONE-TIME MANUAL TEST (v2.7.13) — run from the editor (function // dropdown → testGenerateEntregaCard → Run) to confirm the ENTREGA // (green, "Veículo pronto para retirada") design renders correctly, // including the "Serviço Realizado" section. Logs the resulting // image URL the same way testGenerateAgendaCard does. function testGenerateEntregaCard() { const url = generateAgendaCardUrl( '29890', 'Ariovaldo da Silva', 'Porsche Cayenne', formatAgendaDateTimePt(new Date(Date.now() + 2 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)), resolveAgendaLocationAddress('service_rio'), 'entrega', 'Revisão completa e troca de óleo' ); Logger.log('Generated ENTREGA card URL: ' + url); } // Each reminder is checked as a WINDOW, not an exact instant, since // the trigger runs hourly (see installAgendaReminderTrigger) — e.g. // the "2 days before" reminder fires if the event start falls within // [47h, 49h] from now (a 2h window centered on the 48h mark), so an // hourly check can't skip past it. Hardcoded for easy adjustment. const AGENDA_REMINDERS = [ { tag: 'psr_reminder_sent_2d', hoursBefore: 48, windowHours: 2 }, { tag: 'psr_reminder_sent_1d', hoursBefore: 24, windowHours: 2 }, { tag: 'psr_reminder_sent_4h', hoursBefore: 4, windowHours: 1 }, ]; // Sends one WhatsApp message via the Zapier webhook, using the // single approved template for all message types (creation + // reminders) per current spec. Fire-and-forget from the caller's // perspective — errors are logged, not thrown, so a messaging // failure never blocks the calendar operation that triggered it // (e.g. event creation should still succeed even if WhatsApp fails). // Returns true/false so callers CAN check success if they want to // (e.g. before stamping a "reminder sent" tag). // // v2.7.13: new cardType parameter ('servicos' | 'entrega', defaults // to 'servicos' if omitted so existing call sites that haven't been // updated yet still behave exactly as before). For 'entrega', the // ── AGENDAMENTO AUDIT LOG (v4.1.2) ────────────────────────────── // Columns: timestamp | source | created_by | placa | cliente | modelo | // ano | vin | date_created | data_agendamento | telefone_raw | // telefone_e164 | image_url | webhook_status | ro_number // v4.7.7: ro_number appended at the END on purpose — existing rows keep // their column alignment, so no data migration is needed. Historic rows // stay blank in this column; only bookings made from v4.7.7 onward fill it. const AGENDAMENTO_LOG_HEADERS = [ 'timestamp', 'source', 'created_by', 'placa', 'cliente', 'modelo', 'ano', 'vin', 'date_created', 'data_agendamento', 'telefone_raw', 'telefone_e164', 'image_url', 'webhook_status', 'ro_number', ]; function ensureAgendamentoLog() { let sheet = SHEETS.agendamentoLog || SS.getSheetByName('AGENDAMENTO'); const need = AGENDAMENTO_LOG_HEADERS.length; if (!sheet) { sheet = SS.insertSheet('AGENDAMENTO'); if (sheet.getMaxColumns() < need) { sheet.insertColumnsAfter(sheet.getMaxColumns(), need - sheet.getMaxColumns()); } sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, need).setValues([AGENDAMENTO_LOG_HEADERS]); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); } else if (sheet.getLastColumn() < need) { // v4.7.7 migration: sheet was created with the old 14-column header. // Runs once, on the first send after deploy — nothing manual needed. if (sheet.getMaxColumns() < need) { sheet.insertColumnsAfter(sheet.getMaxColumns(), need - sheet.getMaxColumns()); } sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, need).setValues([AGENDAMENTO_LOG_HEADERS]); } SHEETS.agendamentoLog = sheet; return sheet; } // Appends one row to the AGENDAMENTO audit log. // opts: { source, createdBy, placa, cliente, modelo, ano, vin, // dateCreated, dataAgendamento, telefoneRaw, telefoneE164, // imageUrl, webhookStatus, roNumber } function logAgendamento(opts) { try { const sheet = ensureAgendamentoLog(); sheet.appendRow([ new Date(), opts.source || '', opts.createdBy || '', opts.placa || '', opts.cliente || '', opts.modelo || '', opts.ano || '', opts.vin || '', opts.dateCreated || '', opts.dataAgendamento|| '', opts.telefoneRaw || '', opts.telefoneE164 || '', opts.imageUrl || '', opts.webhookStatus || '', opts.roNumber || '', ]); } catch (e) { Logger.log('logAgendamento error: ' + e); } } // RO's complaint field is looked up automatically (via // getAgendaServiceText) and passed to the card generator as the new // "Serviço Realizado" section — callers never need to fetch this // themselves, just pass cardType='entrega' and the right roNumber. // logOpts (optional): extra fields for the AGENDAMENTO audit log — // { source, createdBy, placa, ano, vin, dateCreated, dataAgendamento } // Any field omitted is logged as blank. function sendAgendaWhatsApp(toPhone, clientName, vehicleModel, dateTimeLabel, address, roNumber, cardType, logOpts) { try { // E.164 normalisation: strip non-digits, ensure country code 55, prefix +. // If the result has fewer than 10 digits the number is unreliable (no DDD // or otherwise malformed) — fall back to Eytan's number so the booking is // not silently lost and can be checked / re-sent manually. const FALLBACK_PHONE = '5521994494183'; const rawDigits = String(toPhone || '').replace(/\D/g, ''); const phone = rawDigits.length >= 10 ? '+' + (rawDigits.startsWith('55') ? rawDigits : '55' + rawDigits) : '+' + FALLBACK_PHONE; if (rawDigits.length < 10) { Logger.log('sendAgendaWhatsApp: unreliable phone "' + toPhone + '" (< 10 digits after strip) — falling back to ' + FALLBACK_PHONE); } const resolvedCardType = cardType || 'servicos'; const serviceText = (resolvedCardType === 'entrega') ? getAgendaServiceText(roNumber) : ''; // Generate the appointment-card image. Never blocks the text // send — if image generation fails, image_url is sent as an // empty string and Zapier/WABA should fall back to text-only // (exact fallback behavior depends on how the Zap is configured; // confirm this with a real test before relying on it). const imageUrl = generateAgendaCardUrl(roNumber, clientName, vehicleModel, dateTimeLabel, address, resolvedCardType, serviceText) || ''; const payload = { to: phone, template_var_1: clientName || '', template_var_2: vehicleModel || '', template_var_3: dateTimeLabel || '', template_var_4: address || resolveAgendaLocationAddress(AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY), image_url: imageUrl, }; const webhookResp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(WABA_ZAPIER_WEBHOOK_URL, { method: 'post', contentType: 'application/json', payload: JSON.stringify(payload), muteHttpExceptions: true, }); const webhookStatus = webhookResp.getResponseCode(); // Audit log — fire-and-forget, never blocks the send result. try { const opts = logOpts || {}; logAgendamento({ source: opts.source || '', createdBy: opts.createdBy || '', placa: opts.placa || '', cliente: clientName || '', modelo: vehicleModel || '', ano: opts.ano || '', vin: opts.vin || '', dateCreated: opts.dateCreated || '', dataAgendamento: dateTimeLabel || '', telefoneRaw: toPhone || '', telefoneE164: phone, imageUrl: imageUrl, webhookStatus: webhookStatus, roNumber: roNumber || '', }); } catch (logErr) { Logger.log('sendAgendaWhatsApp: logAgendamento failed: ' + logErr); } return true; } catch (e) { Logger.log('sendAgendaWhatsApp error: ' + e); return false; } } // ── ONE-TIME MANUAL TEST — run from the editor (function dropdown // → testSendWebhookWithImage → Run) to fire a real webhook call with // BOTH the phone number and a freshly-generated image_url, so the // exact field name/shape can be confirmed inside Zapier's Catch Hook // trigger before relying on it in the live creation/reminder flow. function testSendWebhookWithImage() { const result = sendAgendaWhatsApp( '5521999998888', // replace with YOUR real number to actually receive it 'Teste Webhook', 'Cayenne Teste', formatAgendaDateTimePt(new Date(Date.now() + 2 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)), resolveAgendaLocationAddress('service_rio'), '99999' ); Logger.log('sendAgendaWhatsApp (with image_url) returned: ' + result); } // Formats an event's start time into the {{3}} template value, e.g. // "Terça-feira, 24 de junho às 09:00". Hardcoded weekday/month names // since Apps Script's Utilities.formatDate doesn't reliably localize // to pt-BR full names depending on the script's locale setting. // Uses Utilities.formatDate's own tz-aware numeric output (day, // month index, weekday index, hour, minute) directly — avoids // re-parsing a formatted string back into a Date, which would get // reinterpreted in the script's runtime timezone instead of // America/Sao_Paulo. const PT_WEEKDAYS = ['Domingo','Segunda-feira','Terça-feira','Quarta-feira','Quinta-feira','Sexta-feira','Sábado']; const PT_MONTHS = ['janeiro','fevereiro','março','abril','maio','junho','julho','agosto','setembro','outubro','novembro','dezembro']; function formatAgendaDateTimePt(date) { const tz = 'America/Sao_Paulo'; const isoWeekday = Number(Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, 'u')); // 1=Mon..7=Sun const weekday = PT_WEEKDAYS[isoWeekday % 7]; // Sun(7)->0=Domingo, Mon(1)->1=Segunda, etc. const day = Number(Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, 'd')); const monthIdx = Number(Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, 'M')) - 1; const month = PT_MONTHS[monthIdx]; const hh = Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, 'HH'); const mm = Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, 'mm'); return weekday + ', ' + day + ' de ' + month + ' às ' + hh + ':' + mm; } function getAgendaCalendar(which) { const id = AGENDA_CALENDAR_IDS[String(which).toLowerCase()]; if (!id) throw new Error('Unknown agenda calendar: ' + which); return CalendarApp.getCalendarById(id); } // ── ONE-TIME AUTH TEST — run this manually from the editor (function // dropdown → testCalendarAuth → Run) to force the Calendar permission // prompt. Takes no arguments, so it actually reaches CalendarApp // (unlike running getAgendamentos/getFreeSlots directly with no args, // which throws on missing parameters before ever touching Calendar). // Safe to leave in permanently — delete later if you want a clean file. function testCalendarAuth() { const cal = CalendarApp.getCalendarById(AGENDA_CALENDAR_IDS.servicos); Logger.log('Calendar name: ' + cal.getName()); Logger.log('Auth OK — Calendar scope is working.'); } // ── Customers tab headers (fixed, matches source data exactly) ── const CUSTOMERS_HEADERS = [ 'CPF.CNPJ', 'Client Name', 'Mobile', 'Data de Nascimento', 'License', 'Year', 'Modelo', 'VIN', 'MFG date', ]; function ensureCustomersHeaders() { // NOTE: per spec, the Customers tab should be populated/updated // on every OI sync run. That sync pipeline lives in a SEPARATE // Apps Script project (Config.gs/ApiClient.gs/RoSync.gs/etc, per // project memory) — NOT this file. This function only ensures // the header row exists so Agendamento can read/write safely; // it does not populate rows. The actual sync-on-OI-run hook still // needs to be added on the OI sync project's side (e.g. inside // RoSync.gs after RepairOrders/Joblines are written, upsert a // Customers row per RO using its CPF/plate/vehicle fields). const sheet = SHEETS.customers; if (!sheet) return; if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) { sheet.appendRow(CUSTOMERS_HEADERS); } } // Read the full Customers tab as row objects, in original sheet // order (this order is relied on by findLastByLicense — append-only, // chronological, last occurrence = most recent owner). // Cached per script execution (module-level var persists across // function calls within the same doGet/doPost run, but NOT across // separate requests) — avoids re-reading the entire Customers sheet // once per event when agendaEventToObj() is called in a loop (e.g. // getAgendamentos listing 20 events would otherwise trigger 20 full // sheet reads for the auto-fill lookup). let _customersRowsCache = null; function getCustomersRows() { if (_customersRowsCache !== null) return _customersRowsCache; ensureCustomersHeaders(); const sheet = SHEETS.customers; if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) { _customersRowsCache = []; return _customersRowsCache; } const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; _customersRowsCache = data.slice(1).map(function(r) { return rowToObj(headers, r); }); return _customersRowsCache; } // ── String normalisation for search (accent + case insensitive) ── function normaliseSearchStr(str) { return String(str || '') .normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '') .toLowerCase().trim(); } function normalisePlate(str) { return normaliseSearchStr(str).replace(/[-\s]/g, ''); } // ── Levenshtein edit distance (for fuzzy name matching) ────────── function levenshteinDistance(a, b) { if (a === b) return 0; if (!a.length) return b.length; if (!b.length) return a.length; let prevRow = []; for (let j = 0; j <= b.length; j++) prevRow[j] = j; for (let i = 1; i <= a.length; i++) { const currRow = [i]; for (let j = 1; j <= b.length; j++) { const cost = a[i - 1] === b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1; currRow[j] = Math.min( prevRow[j] + 1, // deletion currRow[j - 1] + 1, // insertion prevRow[j - 1] + cost // substitution ); } prevRow = currRow; } return prevRow[b.length]; } // Fuzzy match a single query token against a single name token. // Exact/substring match always wins (distance 0 effectively). // Otherwise allow edit distance scaled to token length, so short // names don't over-match and longer names tolerate 1-2 typos. function fuzzyTokenMatches(queryToken, nameToken) { if (!queryToken || !nameToken) return false; if (nameToken.indexOf(queryToken) > -1) return true; // substring match const maxLen = Math.max(queryToken.length, nameToken.length); const allowedDistance = maxLen <= 4 ? 1 : (maxLen <= 8 ? 2 : 3); return levenshteinDistance(queryToken, nameToken) <= allowedDistance; } // A query matches a full "Client Name" if every query token has at // least one fuzzy match among the name's tokens (so "luis silva" // matches "Luiz Silva Santos", order-independent). function fuzzyNameMatches(query, fullName) { const qTokens = normaliseSearchStr(query).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); const nTokens = normaliseSearchStr(fullName).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); if (!qTokens.length || !nTokens.length) return false; return qTokens.every(function(qt) { return nTokens.some(function(nt) { return fuzzyTokenMatches(qt, nt); }); }); } // ── doGet action=searchCustomers&query=X ────────────────────────── // Search strategy, in order — first rule that looks applicable wins: // 1. Looks like a plate (short, alphanumeric, no spaces, matches // a License column value) → exact match, LAST occurrence only. // 2. Looks like a CPF/CNPJ (mostly digits, 8+ chars) → partial // match against CPF.CNPJ, ALL matching rows. // 3. Otherwise treat as a name search → partial + fuzzy match // against Client Name, ALL matching rows. // Also always checks RO number against the ROs sheet, since techs // may search by RO# too (per spec) — if found, resolves the RO's // plate and re-runs the plate search to surface that customer. function searchCustomers(query) { try { const q = String(query || '').trim(); if (!q) return { ok: true, mode: 'empty', results: [] }; const rows = getCustomersRows(); const qPlate = normalisePlate(q); const qDigits = q.replace(/\D/g, ''); // 1. RO number search — resolve to plate via ROs sheet, then // fall through to the plate-search branch below. if (/^\d+$/.test(q.replace(/^#/, '')) && SHEETS.ros) { const roMatch = findROByNumber(q); if (roMatch && roMatch.plate) { const byPlate = findCustomerRowsByPlate(rows, roMatch.plate); if (byPlate.length) return { ok: true, mode: 'ro_number', results: byPlate }; } } // 2. Plate search — short alphanumeric token, exact match, // LAST occurrence wins (most recent owner). const looksLikePlate = qPlate.length >= 5 && qPlate.length <= 8 && /^[a-z0-9]+$/.test(qPlate); if (looksLikePlate) { const byPlate = findCustomerRowsByPlate(rows, q); if (byPlate.length) return { ok: true, mode: 'plate', results: byPlate }; } // 3. CPF/CNPJ search — mostly digits, longer token, ALL matches. const looksLikeCpf = qDigits.length >= 6 && (qDigits.length / q.length) > 0.5; if (looksLikeCpf) { const byCpf = rows.filter(function(r) { return String(r['CPF.CNPJ'] || '').replace(/\D/g, '').indexOf(qDigits) > -1; }); if (byCpf.length) return { ok: true, mode: 'cpf', results: byCpf }; } // 4. Name search — partial + fuzzy, ALL matches. const byName = rows.filter(function(r) { const name = String(r['Client Name'] || ''); return normaliseSearchStr(name).indexOf(normaliseSearchStr(q)) > -1 || fuzzyNameMatches(q, name); }); return { ok: true, mode: 'name', results: byName }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('searchCustomers error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e), results: [] }; } } // Exact plate match, returns only the LAST matching row (as a // single-element array) since plates are reassigned over time and // row order is chronological/append-only. function findCustomerRowsByPlate(rows, plateQuery) { const target = normalisePlate(plateQuery); let lastMatch = null; rows.forEach(function(r) { if (normalisePlate(r['License']) === target) lastMatch = r; }); return lastMatch ? [lastMatch] : []; } function findROByNumber(roNumber) { if (!SHEETS.ros) return null; const data = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const clean = normaliseRO(roNumber); const row = data.slice(1).find(function(r) { return normaliseRO(rowToObj(headers, r).ro_number) === clean; }); return row ? rowToObj(headers, row) : null; } // ── doGet action=getROPrefillData&ro_number=X ──────────────────── // Powers Create Agendamento "Path A" (RO already exists): given an // RO number, looks it up in the ROs sheet to get plate + customer // name, then looks up that plate in the Customers tab (last // occurrence — see findCustomerRowsByPlate) to get the rest of the // vehicle/customer data (Modelo/Year/VIN/Mobile/CPF). Returns // found:false if the RO number doesn't exist at all, so the client // can tell "RO not found" apart from "RO found but no Customers // match yet" (the latter still returns plate/cust_name from the RO // itself, just with the extra fields blank). function getROPrefillData(roNumber) { try { const ro = findROByNumber(roNumber); if (!ro) return { ok: true, found: false }; const result = { ok: true, found: true, ro_number: ro.ro_number || String(roNumber), plate: ro.plate || '', client_name: ro.cust_name || '', modelo: '', year: '', vin: '', mobile: '', cpf: '', }; if (result.plate) { const rows = getCustomersRows(); const match = findCustomerRowsByPlate(rows, result.plate); if (match.length) { const c = match[0]; // RO's own cust_name takes priority if present (it's the // most current record of who actually opened this specific // RO); Customers tab only fills in if the RO sheet has none. if (!result.client_name) result.client_name = c['Client Name'] || ''; result.modelo = c['Modelo'] || ''; result.year = c['Year'] ? String(c['Year']) : ''; result.vin = c['VIN'] || ''; result.mobile = c['Mobile'] || ''; result.cpf = c['CPF.CNPJ'] || ''; } } return result; } catch (e) { Logger.log('getROPrefillData error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e), found: false }; } } // ── doPost action=createCustomer ────────────────────────────────── // Fallback when search finds no match — append a new Customers row. // Used both for genuinely new customers and for a known customer's // new vehicle (a fresh CPF.CNPJ + License pairing). function createCustomer(body) { try { ensureCustomersHeaders(); const sheet = SHEETS.customers; const row = CUSTOMERS_HEADERS.map(function(h) { const key = { 'CPF.CNPJ': 'cpf', 'Client Name': 'client_name', 'Mobile': 'mobile', 'Data de Nascimento': 'birth_date', 'License': 'license', 'Year': 'year', 'Modelo': 'modelo', 'VIN': 'vin', 'MFG date': 'mfg_date', }[h]; return body[key] || ''; }); sheet.appendRow(row); return { ok: true }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('createCustomer error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── doGet action=getFreeSlots&calendar=servicos|entrega&date=YYYY-MM-DD // Returns every 1h slot for a single day, business hours only // (Mon–Fri 09:00–18:00). If the date is today, slots already in the // past are omitted entirely (not just marked unavailable). // // v2.7.27: capacity is now a SOFT cap of AGENDA_SLOT_CAPACITY (3) per // slot, one per Agendador, not a hard 1-per-slot exclusion. Every slot // is returned regardless of how many events it already has — even a // full (3/3) slot stays selectable, since occasionally a 4th may be // genuinely needed. Each slot is annotated with `bookings`: which // Agendador(s) already created an event there (name + color), so the // frontend can render colored pills as a visual heads-up before the // Aprovador picks an already-busy slot. function getFreeSlots(which, dateStr) { try { const cal = getAgendaCalendar(which); const day = parseAgendaDate(dateStr); if (!day) return { ok: false, error: 'invalid date' }; const dow = day.getDay(); // 0=Sun..6=Sat if (dow === 0 || dow === 6) return { ok: true, slots: [], reason: 'weekend' }; const now = new Date(); const isToday = sameLocalDate(day, now); const slots = []; for (let h = AGENDA_BUSINESS_HOURS.startHour; h < AGENDA_BUSINESS_HOURS.endHour; h++) { const slotStart = new Date(day); slotStart.setHours(h, 0, 0, 0); const slotEnd = new Date(slotStart.getTime() + AGENDA_SLOT_MINUTES * 60000); if (isToday && slotStart.getTime() <= now.getTime()) continue; // hide past slots const eventsInSlot = cal.getEvents(slotStart, slotEnd); const bookings = eventsInSlot.map(function(ev) { const tech = ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY) || ''; return { id: ev.getId(), tech: tech, color: agendadorColor(tech) }; }); slots.push({ start: slotStart.toISOString(), end: slotEnd.toISOString(), label: Utilities.formatDate(slotStart, 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'HH:mm') + ' – ' + Utilities.formatDate(slotEnd, 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'HH:mm'), bookings: bookings, at_capacity: bookings.length >= AGENDA_SLOT_CAPACITY, }); } return { ok: true, slots: slots }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('getFreeSlots error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e), slots: [] }; } } function parseAgendaDate(dateStr) { // Expects YYYY-MM-DD, constructed in the server's timezone // (America/Sao_Paulo, set in appsscript.json) so hour-setting // below lines up with business hours correctly. const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(String(dateStr || '').trim()); if (!m) return null; return new Date(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])); } function sameLocalDate(a, b) { return a.getFullYear() === b.getFullYear() && a.getMonth() === b.getMonth() && a.getDate() === b.getDate(); } // ── doGet action=hasServicosEvent&ro_number=X ───────────────────── // Drives the ENTREGA hard-gate: only allow creating an ENTREGA // event if a SERVICOS event already exists for this RO number. Also // doubles as the backend for the SERVICO Management screen's // "buscar histórico completo" fallback (v2.7.46) — a single-RO // on-demand lookup, so a wide window costs nothing extra (Calendar // API charges for events returned, not range width, and .find() // short-circuits on first match). // // v2.7.46: window widened from 90-days-back to a fixed 2020-01-01 // floor (still +365 days forward). Root cause: RO 28682 had a real // SERVICOS event from 2025-06-25 that the old 90-day window couldn't // see, so hasServicosEvent wrongly reported exists:false — silently // blocking ENTREGA creation for an RO that already had a valid // SERVICOS event, and defeating the double-submit guard in // createBackfillAgendamento. AGENDA_HISTORY_FLOOR is shared with the // Management screen's fallback search for the same reason. function hasServicosEvent(roNumber) { try { const cal = getAgendaCalendar('servicos'); const start = AGENDA_HISTORY_FLOOR; const end = new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const events = cal.getEvents(start, end); const clean = normaliseRO(roNumber); const match = events.find(function(ev) { const tagRO = normaliseRO(ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_RO) || ''); const titleRO = normaliseRO(parseRONumberFromTitle(ev.getTitle())); return tagRO === clean || titleRO === clean; }); return { ok: true, exists: !!match, event: match ? agendaEventToObj(match, 'servicos') : null }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('hasServicosEvent error: ' + e); return { ok: false, exists: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── Finds the ENTREGA event for a given RO, if one exists. Same // search pattern as hasServicosEvent, just on the other calendar. // Used by getGanttDataForRO to surface delivery ETA on the Gantt // chart — does NOT gate anything (ENTREGA can exist or not, both // are valid states for a Gantt view, unlike the hard create-gate). function findEntregaEventForRO(roNumber) { try { const cal = getAgendaCalendar('entrega'); const start = new Date(Date.now() - 90 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const end = new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const events = cal.getEvents(start, end); const clean = normaliseRO(roNumber); const match = events.find(function(ev) { const tagRO = normaliseRO(ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_RO) || ''); const titleRO = normaliseRO(parseRONumberFromTitle(ev.getTitle())); return tagRO === clean || titleRO === clean; }); return match ? agendaEventToObj(match, 'entrega') : null; } catch (e) { Logger.log('findEntregaEventForRO error: ' + e); return null; } } // ── Finds the SERVICOS event's chegada_at timestamp for a given RO, // if the customer has ever been confirmed arrived (v2.7.36). Same // search pattern as hasServicosEvent/findEntregaEventForRO. Used by // getGanttDataForRO to surface "Data de entrada" on the Gantt chart, // alongside the existing ETA (Data estimada de entrega) field. // v2.7.47: window widened to AGENDA_HISTORY_FLOOR (single-RO // on-demand lookup, same reasoning as hasServicosEvent — cheap // regardless of range width). Also falls back to the ROs sheet's // manual_patio_at (see setManualPatio) when no calendar tag is // found, so the Gantt's "Data de entrada" reflects a manually // migrated legacy RO too, not just calendar-confirmed arrivals. function findChegadaAtForRO(roNumber) { try { const cal = getAgendaCalendar('servicos'); const start = AGENDA_HISTORY_FLOOR; const end = new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const events = cal.getEvents(start, end); const clean = normaliseRO(roNumber); const match = events.find(function(ev) { const tagRO = normaliseRO(ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_RO) || ''); const titleRO = normaliseRO(parseRONumberFromTitle(ev.getTitle())); return tagRO === clean || titleRO === clean; }); const calendarAt = match ? (match.getTag(EVT_PROP_CHEGADA_AT) || null) : null; if (calendarAt) return calendarAt; // Fallback: manually-tagged legacy RO with no calendar chegada_at. const ro = findROByNumber(roNumber); return (ro && ro.manual_patio_at) ? ro.manual_patio_at : null; } catch (e) { Logger.log('findChegadaAtForRO error: ' + e); return null; } } // ── Reconstructs work SESSIONS from the append-only Work Log, for // one RO's joblines. A session is a {tech, start, end, durationMin, // pct} block bounded by a 'started' event and the next 'paused' or // 'completed' event for the SAME jobline_id, in chronological order. // Multiple techs can each contribute separate session blocks to the // same jobline over time (e.g. Mike starts/pauses, Eytan later // finishes) — each shows as its own distinct block, matching the // legacy GANTT's behavior exactly. // // An orphaned trailing 'started' with no matching close yet (tech // still actively working, or app crashed/lost connection mid-session) // becomes an "in progress, no end time yet" session rather than being // dropped or breaking reconstruction for the rest of the jobline. function reconstructSessionsForRO(roNumber) { const data = SHEETS.workLog.getDataRange().getValues(); if (data.length < 2) return {}; const headers = data[0]; const tsCol = headers.indexOf('timestamp'); const techCol = headers.indexOf('tech'); const roCol = headers.indexOf('ro_number'); const jlCol = headers.indexOf('jobline_id'); const evCol = headers.indexOf('event'); const pctCol = headers.indexOf('completion_pct'); if ([tsCol, techCol, roCol, jlCol, evCol, pctCol].indexOf(-1) > -1) return {}; const clean = String(roNumber).trim(); const byJobline = {}; // jobline_id -> [{ts, tech, event, pct}, ...] data.slice(1).forEach(function(r) { if (String(r[roCol]).trim() !== clean) return; const jl = String(r[jlCol]).trim(); if (!jl) return; if (!byJobline[jl]) byJobline[jl] = []; byJobline[jl].push({ ts: new Date(r[tsCol]), tech: String(r[techCol] || ''), event: String(r[evCol] || ''), pct: Number(r[pctCol]) || 0, }); }); const sessionsByJobline = {}; Object.keys(byJobline).forEach(function(jl) { const rows = byJobline[jl].sort(function(a, b) { return a.ts - b.ts; }); const sessions = []; let open = null; rows.forEach(function(row) { if (row.event === 'started') { open = { tech: row.tech, start: row.ts, end: null, pct: row.pct }; } else if (row.event === 'paused' || row.event === 'completed') { if (open) { open.end = row.ts; open.pct = row.pct; sessions.push(open); open = null; } else { // A close event with no preceding open (shouldn't normally // happen, but data can be messy) — record as a zero-length // marker so the percentage update isn't silently lost. sessions.push({ tech: row.tech, start: row.ts, end: row.ts, pct: row.pct }); } } }); if (open) sessions.push(open); // orphaned trailing 'started' — still in progress // Re-key legacy index-based ids ('DEF--', written by app // versions before v2.7.33) to their stable name-based equivalents // ('DEF--'), so getGanttDataForRO's lookup always finds // sessions regardless of when they were logged. The RO number is // extracted from the id itself (after the last '-') to avoid // needing to thread roNumber through the per-jobline loop. // e.g. 'DEF-0-29901' → 'DEF-VAL_de_entrada-29901' var resolvedJl = jl; var legacyMatch = jl.match(/^DEF-(\d+)-(\d+)$/); if (legacyMatch) { var idx = parseInt(legacyMatch[1], 10); var roN = legacyMatch[2]; var name = LEGACY_DEFAULT_JOBLINES_ORDER[idx]; if (name) resolvedJl = 'DEF-' + sanitiseName(name) + '-' + roN; } sessionsByJobline[resolvedJl] = sessions.map(function(s) { return { tech: s.tech, start: s.start.toISOString(), end: s.end ? s.end.toISOString() : null, durationMin: s.end ? Math.round((s.end - s.start) / 60000) : null, pct: s.pct, inProgress: !s.end, }; }); }); return sessionsByJobline; } // ── doGet action=ganttData&ro_number=X ───────────────────────────── // Single-RO data feed for the Gantt viewer (separate Apps Script // project, reusing this PWA's own data — no separate legacy source). // Returns everything the Gantt chart needs in one call: // - osDetails: customer/plate/model/mileage/complaint/days elapsed // (from the ROs sheet), plus ETA (from the ENTREGA Agendamento // event, if one exists) and pendingParts (from PartsRequests). // - joblines: every real Joblines-sheet row for this RO, PLUS every // DEFAULT_JOBLINES entry ("Padrão" — VAL de entrada, PQIS, etc.), // each with estimated_hours and reconstructed work sessions // (see reconstructSessionsForRO). remainingHours is computed as // estimated_hours × (1 − completion_pct/100), for capacity // planning — null when estimated_hours is missing/zero (avoids // implying false precision). function getGanttDataForRO(roNumber) { try { const ro = String(roNumber || '').trim(); if (!ro) return { ok: false, error: 'missing_ro_number' }; // ── OS details ── const roData = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const roHeaders = roData[0]; const roRow = roData.slice(1).find(function(r) { return normaliseRO(rowToObj(roHeaders, r).ro_number) === normaliseRO(ro); }); if (!roRow) return { ok: false, error: 'ro_not_found' }; const roObj = rowToObj(roHeaders, roRow); const openedDate = normaliseDateField(roObj.opened_date); let elapsedDays = null; if (openedDate) { const opened = new Date(openedDate + 'T00:00:00'); const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0); elapsedDays = Math.round((today - opened) / 86400000); } const entregaEvent = findEntregaEventForRO(ro); const chegadaAt = findChegadaAtForRO(ro); const partsResult = getPartsRequests(ro); const osDetails = { ro_number: roObj.ro_number, customerName: roObj.cust_name || '', plate: roObj.plate || '', model: roObj.vehicle || '', mileage: roObj.mileage || '', complaint: roObj.complaint || '', status: roObj.status || '', openedDate: openedDate, elapsedDays: elapsedDays, entrada: chegadaAt || null, eta: entregaEvent ? entregaEvent.start : null, pendingParts: partsResult.ok ? partsResult.parts.filter(function(p) { return p.status !== 'Delivered'; }) : [], }; // ── Joblines (real + Padrão defaults) with sessions ── const sessionsByJobline = reconstructSessionsForRO(ro); const realJoblines = getJoblineRows(ro); const compDef = getCompletedDefaultsMap(); // Build a Set of approved job_descriptions for this RO — used to // filter out real (non-default) joblines that have no sessions yet // AND are not approved. Default joblines bypass this filter entirely // since they're not part of the Aprovacoes flow. const approvalsForRO = getApprovals(ro); const approvedDescSet = new Set( approvalsForRO .filter(function(a) { return a.approved; }) .map(function(a) { return String(a.jobline).trim(); }) ); function withSessionsAndRemaining(jl, isPadrao) { const sessions = sessionsByJobline[jl.jobline_id] || []; const estHours = Number(jl.estimated_hours) || 0; const pct = Number(jl.completion_pct) || 0; const remainingHours = estHours > 0 ? Math.max(0, estHours * (1 - pct / 100)) : null; return { jobline_id: jl.jobline_id, description: jl.job_description, isPadrao: isPadrao, estimatedHours: estHours || null, completionPct: pct, remainingHours: remainingHours, sessions: sessions, started: sessions.length > 0, }; } const joblinesOut = realJoblines .filter(function(jl) { return !isExcludedGlobally(jl.job_description); }) .filter(function(jl) { // Hide joblines that have no sessions AND are not approved — // they have nothing to show on the chart and just add visual // noise. A jobline with sessions is always shown (tech worked // it, approval may follow). An approved jobline with no sessions // yet is shown (work authorised, scheduled to start). Defaults // bypass this entirely (handled separately below). const hasSessions = (sessionsByJobline[jl.jobline_id] || []).length > 0; const isApproved = approvedDescSet.has(String(jl.job_description).trim()); return hasSessions || isApproved; }) .map(function(jl) { return withSessionsAndRemaining(jl, false); }); DEFAULT_JOBLINES.forEach(function(desc) { const resolved = resolveDefaultRecord(compDef, desc, ro); const id = resolved.id; const comp = resolved.record; joblinesOut.push(withSessionsAndRemaining({ jobline_id: id, job_description: desc, estimated_hours: '', // defaults (VAL de entrada, PQIS, etc.) have no OI-sourced estimate — always null remainingHours completion_pct: comp ? comp.completion_pct : 0, }, true)); }); // Sort: started joblines first (by earliest session start), then // unstarted — same convention as the legacy Gantt viewer. joblinesOut.sort(function(a, b) { if (a.started && !b.started) return -1; if (!a.started && b.started) return 1; if (a.started && b.started) { return new Date(a.sessions[0].start) - new Date(b.sessions[0].start); } return 0; }); return { ok: true, os: ro, osDetails: osDetails, joblines: joblinesOut }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('getGanttDataForRO error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // Backfill discovery for v2.7.24's "Agendamento ausente" feature. // Finds open ROs (status case-insensitively != 'fechada') that have // NO SERVICOS calendar event at all — these are walk-ins/urgent jobs // that never went through the normal Agendamento create flow, so // they're invisible to the ENTREGA Management list and can never be // marked delivered through the normal toggle (which requires an // existing event). Lets the Aprovador backfill both SERVICOS + a // pre-delivered ENTREGA event in one step via createBackfillAgendamento. // // Scans a 2-year-back window for SERVICOS events (same floor used by // getAgendamentos' ENTREGA fix, v2.7.23) — wider than hasServicosEvent's // 90-day window, since these ROs may have been open far longer than // that without ever being scheduled. One batch calendar fetch, not one // per RO, to keep this fast over the full ROs sheet. // v3.4.1: DIAGNOSTIC — manual-run only (no doGet/doPost route), mirrors // Patio project's diagnoseMissingRO() pattern. Run from the Apps // Script editor (select this function, Run, then View > Logs) with a // specific RO number to see exactly which of getRosMissingAgendamento()'s // two checks is excluding it: (1) its raw status field failing the // 'fechada' comparison, or (2) a SERVICOS event somewhere in the // ±730/365-day window already carrying its RO number (tag or // title-parsed) — including possibly an orphaned/stray event the // normal Agendamento Management view wouldn't surface, since that // view uses a much narrower date range by default. function diagnoseAgendamentoAusente(roToCheck) { const target = normaliseRO(String(roToCheck || '').trim()); if (!target) { Logger.log('Pass an RO number, e.g. diagnoseAgendamentoAusente("29914")'); return; } Logger.log('══════ Diagnosing RO ' + target + ' — Agendamento Ausente ══════'); const data = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const roRow = data.slice(1).map(function(r) { return rowToObj(headers, r); }) .find(function(ro) { return normaliseRO(ro.ro_number) === target; }); if (!roRow) { Logger.log('❌ NOT FOUND in the ROs sheet at all — this is a different'); Logger.log(' problem entirely (not an Agendamento Ausente issue).'); return; } const rawStatus = String(roRow.status || ''); const statusNorm = rawStatus.trim().toLowerCase(); const passesStatusCheck = statusNorm !== 'fechada'; Logger.log('Raw status field: "' + rawStatus + '" (normalised: "' + statusNorm + '")'); Logger.log(passesStatusCheck ? '✅ Passes the status check (not literally "fechada")' : '❌ EXCLUDED HERE — status is literally "fechada", filtered out' + ' before the calendar is even checked.'); const cal = getAgendaCalendar('servicos'); const start = new Date(Date.now() - 730 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const end = new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const events = cal.getEvents(start, end); Logger.log('Scanned ' + events.length + ' SERVICOS events in the ' + Utilities.formatDate(start, 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy') + ' → ' + Utilities.formatDate(end, 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy') + ' window.'); let matchedEvent = null; let matchedVia = ''; events.forEach(function(ev) { if (matchedEvent) return; // report only the first match found const tagRO = normaliseRO(ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_RO) || ''); const titleRO = normaliseRO(parseRONumberFromTitle(ev.getTitle())); if (tagRO === target) { matchedEvent = ev; matchedVia = 'EVT_PROP_RO tag'; } else if (titleRO === target) { matchedEvent = ev; matchedVia = 'title parsing'; } }); if (matchedEvent) { Logger.log('❌ EXCLUDED HERE — found a SERVICOS event matching this RO'); Logger.log(' via ' + matchedVia + ':'); Logger.log(' Event ID: ' + matchedEvent.getId()); Logger.log(' Title: "' + matchedEvent.getTitle() + '"'); Logger.log(' Start: ' + matchedEvent.getStartTime()); Logger.log(' If this event looks stale/wrong/orphaned (e.g. from a'); Logger.log(' deleted-and-recreated RO, a copy-paste error in another'); Logger.log(' event, or a genuinely old event outside what the normal'); Logger.log(' Agendamento Management view shows you), that mismatch —'); Logger.log(' not a code bug — is what\'s hiding this RO from'); Logger.log(' Agendamento Ausente. Fix by correcting/removing that event.'); } else { Logger.log('✅ No matching SERVICOS event found in this window.'); } Logger.log('══════ Verdict: ' + (passesStatusCheck && !matchedEvent ? 'SHOULD be showing in Agendamento Ausente — if it still isn\'t, ' + 'something else is wrong (check the frontend fetch/render, not this logic).' : 'Correctly excluded per the reasons logged above.') + ' ══════'); } // v3.4.3: DIAGNOSTIC — manual-run only, same pattern as // diagnoseAgendamentoAusente() above. Pinpoints exactly why Patio // (or the PWA itself) isn't recognising a given RO as entregue=true, // by re-running getEntregueByPlateMap()'s exact matching logic but // with full visibility into every candidate event considered — // including ones OUTSIDE the ±14-day CHEGADA_RO_MATCH_WINDOW_DAYS // window the real matching logic actually uses, so a too-narrow- // window problem (the same class of bug hasServicosEvent hit before — // see v2.7.46) is immediately obvious rather than guessed at. function diagnoseEntregaMatch(roToCheck) { const target = normaliseRO(String(roToCheck || '').trim()); if (!target) { Logger.log('Pass an RO number, e.g. diagnoseEntregaMatch("29940")'); return; } Logger.log('══════ Diagnosing RO ' + target + ' — entregue matching ══════'); const data = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const roRow = data.slice(1).map(function(r) { return rowToObj(headers, r); }) .find(function(ro) { return normaliseRO(ro.ro_number) === target; }); if (!roRow) { Logger.log('❌ NOT FOUND in the ROs sheet at all.'); return; } const roPlate = String(roRow.plate || ''); const roPlateNorm = normalisePlate(roPlate); Logger.log('RO plate: "' + roPlate + '" (normalised: "' + roPlateNorm + '")' + (roPlateNorm ? '' : ' — BLANK, will rely on RO-number fallback only')); // Wide scan — same range as hasServicosEvent's AGENDA_HISTORY_FLOOR // fix, deliberately much wider than the real ±14-day matching // window, so we can see EVERY candidate event regardless of date. const cal = getAgendaCalendar('entrega'); const wideStart = AGENDA_HISTORY_FLOOR; const wideEnd = new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const allEvents = cal.getEvents(wideStart, wideEnd); const now = Date.now(); const candidates = allEvents .map(function(ev) { const obj = agendaEventToObj(ev, 'entrega'); const plateMatch = roPlateNorm && normalisePlate(obj.plate) === roPlateNorm; const roMatch = normaliseRO(obj.ro_number) === target; return { ev: ev, obj: obj, plateMatch: plateMatch, roMatch: roMatch, distanceDays: Math.round(Math.abs(ev.getStartTime().getTime() - now) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)), }; }) .filter(function(c) { return c.plateMatch || c.roMatch; }) .sort(function(a, b) { return a.distanceDays - b.distanceDays; }); if (!candidates.length) { Logger.log('❌ No ENTREGA event found matching this plate or RO number,'); Logger.log(' even in the wide ' + Utilities.formatDate(wideStart, 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy') + ' → ' + Utilities.formatDate(wideEnd, 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy') + ' scan.'); Logger.log(' Either no ENTREGA event was ever created for this RO, or'); Logger.log(' the plate/RO number on the event genuinely differs from'); Logger.log(' what\'s in the ROs sheet — check for typos on either side.'); return; } Logger.log('Found ' + candidates.length + ' candidate event(s), nearest first:'); candidates.forEach(function(c, i) { const withinWindow = c.distanceDays <= CHEGADA_RO_MATCH_WINDOW_DAYS; Logger.log(' [' + (i + 1) + '] ' + (c.plateMatch ? 'plate match' : 'RO-number match') + ' — entregue=' + c.obj.entregue + ' — ' + c.distanceDays + ' day(s) from today — ' + (withinWindow ? '✅ within ±' + CHEGADA_RO_MATCH_WINDOW_DAYS + '-day window' : '❌ OUTSIDE ±' + CHEGADA_RO_MATCH_WINDOW_DAYS + '-day window — invisible to real matching')); Logger.log(' Event ID: ' + c.ev.getId() + ' — Start: ' + c.ev.getStartTime()); }); const nearest = candidates[0]; const nearestInWindow = nearest.distanceDays <= CHEGADA_RO_MATCH_WINDOW_DAYS; Logger.log('══════ Verdict: ' + (!nearestInWindow ? 'The nearest matching event is OUTSIDE the ±' + CHEGADA_RO_MATCH_WINDOW_DAYS + '-day window used by getEntregueByPlateMap()/getChegadaByPlateMap() — ' + 'this is the same class of bug hasServicosEvent hit before (v2.7.46). ' + 'ro.entregue is coming back false for this RO purely because of window ' + 'width, regardless of the event\'s real entregue tag.' : (nearest.obj.entregue ? 'Nearest event IS within the window and IS entregue=true — matching ' + 'should be working correctly. If Patio still shows this RO, re-check ' + 'that Patio is actually using include_entregue=1 (v1.7.2) and that both ' + 'projects are on their latest deployed versions.' : 'Nearest event is within the window but entregue=false — this RO ' + 'genuinely has NOT been confirmed delivered yet (working as intended).') ) + ' ══════'); } function getRosMissingAgendamento() { try { const data = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const openROs = data.slice(1) .map(function(r) { return rowToObj(headers, r); }) .filter(function(ro) { const status = String(ro.status || '').trim().toLowerCase(); return status !== 'fechada'; }); const cal = getAgendaCalendar('servicos'); const start = new Date(Date.now() - 730 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const end = new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const events = cal.getEvents(start, end); const scheduledROs = {}; events.forEach(function(ev) { const tagRO = normaliseRO(ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_RO) || ''); const titleRO = normaliseRO(parseRONumberFromTitle(ev.getTitle())); if (tagRO) scheduledROs[tagRO] = true; if (titleRO) scheduledROs[titleRO] = true; }); const missing = openROs .filter(function(ro) { return !scheduledROs[normaliseRO(ro.ro_number)]; }) .map(function(ro) { return { ro_number: ro.ro_number, plate: ro.plate || '', vin: ro.vin || '', cust_name: ro.cust_name || '', status: ro.status || '', opened_date: normaliseDateField(ro.opened_date), }; }); return { ok: true, ros: missing, total: missing.length }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('getRosMissingAgendamento error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e), ros: [] }; } } // ── doGet action=getAgendamentos&calendar=X&range_start=Y&range_end=Z&include_delivered=1 // Lists existing events for the Management screen. range_start/end // are ISO date strings (YYYY-MM-DD); defaults to 7 days BACK through // 60 days forward from today if omitted (v2.7.1 — was "today onward" // only, which meant a missed/no-show appointment from yesterday had // no way to be found and caught up on, e.g. for Confirmar Chegada). // // include_delivered (ENTREGA only, ignored for SERVICOS): when // falsy/absent (the default), ENTREGA events already marked entregue // are filtered OUT of the result, so the default list only shows // deliveries still pending — declutters the common case. Pass '1' to // include them too (used by the PWA's "Mostrar entregues" switch). // SERVICOS results are NEVER filtered by chegada state — every event // in range is always returned, since arrived/not-arrived both need // to be visible together to catch up on no-shows. function getAgendamentos(which, rangeStartStr, rangeEndStr, includeDeliveredParam) { try { const cal = getAgendaCalendar(which); const includeDelivered = String(includeDeliveredParam) === '1' || includeDeliveredParam === true; // ENTREGA: delivery timing can't be predicted from when the vehicle // arrived — a car that's been at the shop 40+ days can still be ready // for delivery tomorrow. So when no explicit range_start is given AND // we're not including already-delivered events, there's no lower // bound at all: every not-yet-delivered ENTREGA event should surface, // however long ago it was scheduled. When include_delivered=1, the // list would otherwise grow unbounded with years of old delivered // events, so that path uses a 30-day window instead (covers "mostrar // entregues" use cases without scanning the full calendar history). // SERVICOS is unaffected — it keeps the original -7/+60 day default. const defaultStart = (which === 'entrega') ? (includeDelivered ? new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) : new Date(Date.now() - 730 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)) // ~2 years back — effectively unbounded for any realistic case, without scanning decades of calendar history : new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const start = rangeStartStr ? parseAgendaDate(rangeStartStr) : defaultStart; const end = rangeEndStr ? parseAgendaDate(rangeEndStr) : new Date(Date.now() + 60 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); let events = cal.getEvents(start, end) .map(function(ev) { return agendaEventToObj(ev, which); }); if (which === 'entrega' && !includeDelivered) { events = events.filter(function(ev) { return !ev.entregue; }); } return { ok: true, events: events.sort(function(a, b) { return new Date(a.start) - new Date(b.start); }), }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('getAgendamentos error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e), events: [] }; } } function agendaEventToObj(ev, which) { const title = ev.getTitle(); const description = ev.getDescription(); const taggedRO = ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_RO) || ''; const taggedPlate = ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_PLATE) || ''; // Fallback for events created manually (before this tool existed, // or directly in Google Calendar) — no extended-property tags, so // try to parse RO number / plate / client name / model out of the // title text instead. Confirmed title format: // "OS - , " const parsedRO = taggedRO ? '' : parseRONumberFromTitle(title); const parsedPlate = taggedPlate ? '' : parsePlateFromTitle(title); const parsedClient = parseClientNameFromTitle(title); const parsedModelo = parseModeloFromTitle(title); // Parse the structured "Label: value" description lines this tool // writes (see createAgendamento/updateAgendamento), so the edit // form can be pre-filled. Legacy/manual events with no matching // description simply come back with empty strings here — that's // expected, not an error. const fields = parseAgendaDescription(description); let obj = { id: ev.getId(), calendar: which, title: title, description: description, start: ev.getStartTime().toISOString(), end: ev.getEndTime().toISOString(), ro_number: taggedRO || parsedRO || fields.ro_number, plate: taggedPlate || parsedPlate || fields.plate, cpf: ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_CPF) || '', created_by: ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY) || fields.created_by, client_name: fields.client_name || parsedClient, modelo: fields.modelo || parsedModelo, year: fields.year, vin: fields.vin, mobile: fields.mobile, remark: fields.remark, location_key: ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_LOCATION_KEY) || AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY, chegada: ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_CHEGADA) === '1', entregue: which === 'entrega' && ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_ENTREGUE) === '1', // v4.7.1 — entrega criada pela pill ENTREGAR AGORA (não agendada). // Exposto aqui porque getEntregueByPlateMap/getROs precisam saber // se a entrega desta RO é reversível pela pill, e a tag era lida // até agora só dentro de revertEntregaSemAgendamento. manual_entrega: which === 'entrega' && ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_MANUAL_ENTREGA) === '1', ro_source: taggedRO ? 'tag' : (parsedRO ? 'title_fallback' : (fields.ro_number ? 'description' : 'none')), plate_source: taggedPlate ? 'tag' : (parsedPlate ? 'title_fallback' : (fields.plate ? 'description' : 'none')), }; obj.location_address = resolveAgendaLocationAddress(obj.location_key); // Auto-fill vehicle/customer data from the Customers tab using the // event's plate — but ONLY for fields that are still empty after // the tag/title/description checks above, so we never clobber data // that's already correctly on the event (e.g. an existing event // that already names the customer in its title). if (obj.plate) { const custRows = getCustomersRows(); const custMatches = findCustomerRowsByPlate(custRows, obj.plate); if (custMatches.length) { const c = custMatches[0]; if (!obj.client_name) obj.client_name = c['Client Name'] || ''; if (!obj.modelo) obj.modelo = c['Modelo'] || ''; if (!obj.year) obj.year = c['Year'] ? String(c['Year']) : ''; if (!obj.vin) obj.vin = c['VIN'] || ''; if (!obj.mobile) obj.mobile = c['Mobile'] || ''; if (!obj.cpf) obj.cpf = c['CPF.CNPJ'] || ''; } } // ENTREGA events: vehicle/customer data is ALWAYS a live mirror of // the linked SERVICOS event (by event ID, stamped at creation — // see EVT_PROP_SERVICOS_EVENT_ID), never an independent copy. This // means a later correction on the SERVICOS side (e.g. fixing the // customer name) is automatically reflected here, and a deleted // SERVICOS event is surfaced as a broken link rather than silently // showing stale data. RO number and plate are excluded from this // override — those are treated as immutable by design and already // came through correctly above; only the mutable fields (client // name in particular) are overridden here, alongside an explicit // servicos_link_status the client can act on. if (which === 'entrega') { const linkedId = ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_SERVICOS_EVENT_ID) || ''; if (linkedId) { const linked = resolveServicosLink(linkedId); if (linked) { obj.client_name = linked.client_name; obj.modelo = linked.modelo; obj.year = linked.year; obj.vin = linked.vin; obj.mobile = linked.mobile; obj.cpf = linked.cpf; // Location is NOT live-mirrored anymore (unlike the fields // above) — ENTREGA's pickup location defaults to SERVICOS' // location only at creation time (see createAgendamento), then // becomes its own independent, freely-editable field, since a // customer may want to collect the vehicle from a different // address than where the repair happened. // // BACKWARD COMPATIBILITY: ENTREGA events created before this // change (pre-v2.7.26) have no EVT_PROP_LOCATION_KEY tag of // their own at all, so the tag-read above silently fell back // to AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY — which could be WRONG if the // linked SERVICOS event was at a non-default location (this // used to be correctly covered by the live mirror this comment // replaces). So: only fall back to the linked SERVICOS // location when this event has no own tag yet; once it does // (a fresh creation, or any edit under the new logic), that // own tag always wins and the event is fully independent. if (!ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_LOCATION_KEY)) { obj.location_key = linked.location_key; obj.location_address = linked.location_address; } obj.chegada = linked.chegada; obj.servicos_link_status = 'ok'; } else { obj.servicos_link_status = 'broken'; // linked event was deleted } } else { obj.servicos_link_status = 'unlinked'; // pre-v2.4.1 ENTREGA event, created before this linkage existed } } return obj; } // Looks up a SERVICOS event by its exact calendar event ID (not by // RO number — RO/plate are immutable by design, so ID is the more // direct and reliable pointer). Returns null if the event no longer // exists (deleted), which the caller treats as a broken link. function resolveServicosLink(servicosEventId) { try { const cal = getAgendaCalendar('servicos'); const ev = cal.getEventById(servicosEventId); if (!ev) return null; return agendaEventToObj(ev, 'servicos'); } catch (e) { Logger.log('resolveServicosLink error: ' + e); return null; } } // Matches the name between "OS " and " - ,", e.g. // "OS 29890 ARIOVALDO DA SILVA ROCHA FILHO - KYL8G63, CAYENNE" // → "ARIOVALDO DA SILVA ROCHA FILHO" function parseClientNameFromTitle(title) { const m = /^OS\s+\d+\s+(.+?)\s*-\s*[A-Z0-9]{6,8}\s*,/i.exec(String(title || '').trim()); return m ? m[1].trim() : ''; } // Matches whatever follows the last comma in the title, e.g. // "... - KYL8G63, CAYENNE" → "CAYENNE" function parseModeloFromTitle(title) { const m = /,\s*([^,]+)$/.exec(String(title || '').trim()); return m ? m[1].trim() : ''; } // Parses the "Label: value" lines written by createAgendamento / // updateAgendamento. "Veículo: Cayenne 2023" is split back into // modelo + year on a best-effort basis (last whitespace-separated // token treated as year if it's 4 digits, rest is modelo). function parseAgendaDescription(description) { // Note: the "Local:" line written into the description (see // createAgendamento) is intentionally NOT parsed back out here — // location_key/location_address are resolved from the // EVT_PROP_LOCATION_KEY tag in agendaEventToObj (the authoritative // source), not from this human-readable description text. The // description's "Local:" line exists only for anyone reading the // raw calendar event directly in Google Calendar. const out = { ro_number:'', client_name:'', plate:'', modelo:'', year:'', vin:'', mobile:'', created_by:'', remark:'' }; if (!description) return out; String(description).split('\n').forEach(function(line) { const m = /^([^:]+):\s*(.*)$/.exec(line.trim()); if (!m) return; const label = m[1].trim(); const value = m[2].trim(); if (label === 'RO') out.ro_number = value; if (label === 'Cliente') out.client_name = value; if (label === 'Placa') out.plate = value; if (label === 'VIN') out.vin = value; if (label === 'Telefone') out.mobile = value; if (label === 'Agendado por') out.created_by = value; if (label === 'Observação') out.remark = value; if (label === 'Veículo') { const parts = value.split(/\s+/); const last = parts[parts.length - 1]; if (/^\d{4}$/.test(last) && parts.length > 1) { out.year = last; out.modelo = parts.slice(0, -1).join(' '); } else { out.modelo = value; } } }); return out; } // Matches "OS 29890 ..." at the start of the title → "29890" function parseRONumberFromTitle(title) { const m = /^OS\s+(\d+)/i.exec(String(title || '').trim()); return m ? m[1] : ''; } // Matches the plate token right after " - " (before the comma), e.g. // "OS 29890 ARIOVALDO DA SILVA ROCHA FILHO - KYL8G63, CAYENNE" → "KYL8G63" function parsePlateFromTitle(title) { const m = /-\s*([A-Z0-9]{6,8})\s*,/i.exec(String(title || '')); return m ? m[1].toUpperCase() : ''; } // ── doPost action=createAgendamento ─────────────────────────────── // body: { calendar:'servicos'|'entrega', ro_number, plate, cpf, // client_name, modelo, year, vin, mobile, start, end, tech } // ENTREGA creation is hard-gated on an existing SERVICOS event for // the same RO number — checked server-side regardless of what the // client believes, since this is the authoritative gate. function createAgendamento(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_authorized' }; const which = String(body.calendar || '').toLowerCase(); if (which !== 'servicos' && which !== 'entrega') { return { ok: false, error: 'invalid_calendar' }; } // For ENTREGA, capture the matched SERVICOS event's ID — this // becomes a permanent, direct pointer (EVT_PROP_SERVICOS_EVENT_ID) // stamped on the new ENTREGA event, NOT a re-derivable RO-number // match. See agendaEventToObj's "ENTREGA events" block for how // this is used to live-mirror vehicle/customer data afterward. // Location key (pickup address) follows the same rule: ENTREGA // For ENTREGA, default the pickup location to whatever the linked // SERVICOS event currently has — but allow body.location_key to // override it (e.g. the customer wants to collect the vehicle from // a different address). This is a ONE-TIME default at creation, // not a live mirror: once created, ENTREGA's location is its own // independent, freely-editable field (see updateAgendamento) — it // does NOT follow SERVICOS if SERVICOS' location changes later, // since by the time ENTREGA is being scheduled, SERVICOS has // already happened and isn't expected to change anyway. let linkedServicosEventId = ''; let locationKey = ''; if (which === 'entrega') { const gate = hasServicosEvent(body.ro_number); if (!gate.exists) return { ok: false, error: 'no_servicos_event_for_ro' }; linkedServicosEventId = gate.event ? gate.event.id : ''; const servicosDefault = gate.event ?
