(gate.event.location_key || AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY) : AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY; locationKey = AGENDA_LOCATIONS[body.location_key] ? body.location_key : servicosDefault; } else { locationKey = AGENDA_LOCATIONS[body.location_key] ? body.location_key : AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY; } const resolvedAddress = resolveAgendaLocationAddress(locationKey); const cal = getAgendaCalendar(which); const start = new Date(body.start); const end = new Date(body.end); if (isNaN(start.getTime()) || isNaN(end.getTime())) { return { ok: false, error: 'invalid_datetime' }; } const title = 'RO #' + body.ro_number + ' — ' + (body.client_name || '') + ' — ' + (body.plate || ''); const descriptionLines = [ 'RO: ' + (body.ro_number || ''), 'Cliente: ' + (body.client_name || ''), 'Placa: ' + (body.plate || ''), 'Veículo: ' + [body.modelo, body.year].filter(Boolean).join(' '), 'VIN: ' + (body.vin || ''), 'Telefone: ' + (body.mobile || ''), 'Local: ' + resolvedAddress, 'Observação: ' + (body.remark || ''), 'Agendado por: ' + (body.tech || ''), 'Criado em: ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm'), ]; const event = cal.createEvent(title, start, end, { description: descriptionLines.join('\n') }); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_RO, String(body.ro_number || '')); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_TYPE, which); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_PLATE, String(body.plate || '')); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_CPF, String(body.cpf || '')); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY, String(body.tech || '')); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_LOCATION_KEY, locationKey); if (which === 'entrega' && linkedServicosEventId) { event.setTag(EVT_PROP_SERVICOS_EVENT_ID, linkedServicosEventId); } // Trigger 1 of 4: "just after creating". Fire-and-forget — a // messaging failure must never block event creation from // succeeding, so this is deliberately not allowed to throw (see // sendAgendaWhatsApp's own try/catch). v2.7.13: passes `which` // as cardType so SERVICOS/ENTREGA get visually distinct cards — // previously this call had no cardType at all, so both used the // same red SERVICOS design regardless of which calendar. sendAgendaWhatsApp( body.mobile, body.client_name, [body.modelo, body.year].filter(Boolean).join(' '), formatAgendaDateTimePt(start), resolvedAddress, body.ro_number, which, { source: 'Aprovador', createdBy: body.tech || '', placa: body.plate || '', ano: body.year || '', vin: body.vin || '', dateCreated: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm'), } ); return { ok: true, event: agendaEventToObj(event, which) }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('createAgendamento error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── PUBLIC ONLINE BOOKING (customer self-service, no login) ────── // Added 2026-07-20 for Service Intelligence: each SI card's WhatsApp // message can include a personalized link (see si_bookingUrl_, // SI.gs's getSICards) that opens a public calendar page — the // customer picks a day/slot themselves and the SERVICOS event gets // created automatically, same as an Aprovador creating it by hand. // // SECURITY MODEL: the link's token is an HMAC-signed {placa, expiry} // pair (si_makeBookingToken_/si_validateBookingToken_) — NOT a login. // Anyone with a valid link can book ONE vehicle (the one it was // generated for), nothing else. All customer/vehicle data used to // create the event and send the confirmation is resolved SERVER-SIDE // from the token's plate (si_lookupVehicleForBooking_) — the page // never trusts client-submitted name/phone/model, only which slot // was picked. // // TODO(Eytan) before going live: // 1. Replace SI_BOOKING_SECRET below with a real random secret — // anyone who can read this source can currently forge links. // Treat this file as sensitive once that's set. // 2. Fill in SI_APPROVER_EMAILS so someone gets notified when a // customer books online — empty for now, so that notification // is currently a silent no-op. (WhatsApp-to-approver instead of // email is possible too — same sendAgendaWhatsApp mechanism, // just needs your numbers; ask if you'd rather do that.) // 3. Test the whole flow end to end with a real link before // sending it to a real customer — this is unreviewed v1. const SI_BOOKING_SECRET = 'REPLACE_ME_WITH_A_LONG_RANDOM_SECRET'; // TODO(Eytan) const SI_APPROVER_EMAILS = []; // TODO(Eytan): e.g. ['eytan@...','sidney@...','matheus@...'] // Public image URL shown as the thumbnail when this link's preview card // renders in WhatsApp — hosted logo from the porsche-service-rio repo. // Verify it actually resolves in a browser (couldn't confirm from here); // if it 404s, WhatsApp just shows the card without a thumbnail. const SI_BOOKING_OG_IMAGE_URL = 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/porscherio/porsche-service-rio/main/assets/logo.png'; const SI_BOOKING_TOKEN_DAYS = 30; // link expires this many days after being generated const SI_BOOKING_DAYS_AHEAD = 14; // how many calendar days the picker shows // phone is the number the Aprovador used to send the SI card — it gets // embedded in the token so the public booking handler can confirm to the // correct customer without any CRM lookup at booking time. // payload format: placa|phone_digits|expiresAt function si_makeBookingToken_(placa, phone) { const expiresAt = Date.now() + SI_BOOKING_TOKEN_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; const safePhone = String(phone || '').replace(/\D/g, ''); const payload = String(placa) + '|' + safePhone + '|' + expiresAt; const sig = Utilities.base64EncodeWebSafe( Utilities.computeHmacSha256Signature(payload, SI_BOOKING_SECRET) ); const payloadB64 = Utilities.base64EncodeWebSafe(Utilities.newBlob(payload).getBytes()); return payloadB64 + '.' + sig; } // Supports both old tokens (placa|expiresAt) and new tokens // (placa|phone|expiresAt) — old links already sent to customers will // keep working; val.phone will be '' for them and sendAgendaWhatsApp // will fall back to the Eytan number for manual handling. function si_validateBookingToken_(token) { try { const parts = String(token || '').split('.'); if (parts.length !== 2) return { ok: false, error: 'malformed' }; const payload = Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64DecodeWebSafe(parts[0])).getDataAsString(); const expectedSig = Utilities.base64EncodeWebSafe( Utilities.computeHmacSha256Signature(payload, SI_BOOKING_SECRET) ); if (expectedSig !== parts[1]) return { ok: false, error: 'bad_signature' }; const lastBar = payload.lastIndexOf('|'); const firstBar = payload.indexOf('|'); const expiresAt = Number(payload.substring(lastBar + 1)); const placa = payload.substring(0, firstBar); // phone is the middle segment; empty string for old-format tokens const phone = firstBar === lastBar ? '' : payload.substring(firstBar + 1, lastBar); if (!placa || !expiresAt || Date.now() > expiresAt) return { ok: false, error: 'expired' }; return { ok: true, placa: placa, phone: phone }; } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // Where the branded redirect page (agendar/index.html — see that file's // own header comment for what it does and why) is actually served. // TODO(Eytan): confirm this is the real domain — swap for your custom // domain here if porscherio.github.io/porsche-service-rio isn't it. const SI_BOOKING_REDIRECT_BASE = 'https://porscherio.github.io/porsche-service-rio/agendar/'; // Called from SI.gs's getSICards() to attach a ready-to-send link to // each vehicle. Points at the branded redirect page (own domain, real // Open Graph preview) rather than the raw script.google.com/.../exec // URL — falls back to the raw URL only if SI_BOOKING_REDIRECT_BASE is // ever cleared out. // phone: the customer's number the Aprovador is messaging — gets baked // into the token so createAgendamentoPublic can confirm to the right // number without a CRM lookup. Called from SI.gs as si_bookingUrl_(placa, telefone). function si_bookingUrl_(placa, phone) { const token = si_makeBookingToken_(placa, phone); const base = SI_BOOKING_REDIRECT_BASE || (ScriptApp.getService().getUrl() + '?action=agendar&'); const sep = base.indexOf('?') === -1 ? '?' : '&'; return base + sep + 'token=' + encodeURIComponent(token); } // Resolves customer/vehicle info for a plate from the SI cache (same // data getSICards already returns) — used so the public booking flow // never has to trust anything the client submits about who's booking. function si_lookupVehicleForBooking_(placa) { const cards = getSICards(); return cards.find(function (c) { return c.placa === placa; }) || null; } function si_notifyApprovers_(vehicle, start, address) { if (!SI_APPROVER_EMAILS.length) return; try { const subject = 'Novo agendamento online — ' + vehicle.placa; const body = 'Cliente: ' + vehicle.cliente + '\n' + 'Veículo: ' + vehicle.modelo + ' (' + vehicle.placa + ')\n' + 'Telefone: ' + vehicle.telefone + '\n' + 'Data/Hora: ' + formatAgendaDateTimePt(start) + '\n' + 'Local: ' + address + '\n' + 'Origem: Agendamento Online (link do Service Intelligence)'; MailApp.sendEmail(SI_APPROVER_EMAILS.join(','), subject, body); } catch (e) { Logger.log('si_notifyApprovers_ error: ' + e); } } // doPost action=createAgendamentoPublic — body: { token, start, end } function createAgendamentoPublic(body) { try { const val = si_validateBookingToken_(body.token); if (!val.ok) return { ok: false, error: 'invalid_token: ' + val.error }; const placa = val.placa; const vehicle = si_lookupVehicleForBooking_(placa); if (!vehicle) return { ok: false, error: 'vehicle_not_found' }; const start = new Date(body.start); const end = new Date(body.end); if (isNaN(start.getTime()) || isNaN(end.getTime())) return { ok: false, error: 'invalid_datetime' }; // Re-check availability server-side at write time — the page's own // slot list could be a few minutes stale by the time the customer // actually picks one. const dateStr = Utilities.formatDate(start, 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); const fresh = getFreeSlots('servicos', dateStr); const match = (fresh.slots || []).find(function (s) { return s.start === start.toISOString(); }); if (!match || match.at_capacity) return { ok: false, error: 'slot_unavailable' }; const cal = getAgendaCalendar('servicos'); const resolvedAddress = resolveAgendaLocationAddress(AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY); const title = 'Agendamento Online — ' + (vehicle.cliente || '') + ' — ' + placa; const descriptionLines = [ 'Cliente: ' + (vehicle.cliente || ''), 'Placa: ' + placa, 'Veículo: ' + (vehicle.modelo || ''), 'Telefone: ' + (vehicle.telefone || ''), 'Local: ' + resolvedAddress, 'Origem: Agendamento Online (link do Service Intelligence)', 'Criado em: ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm'), ]; const event = cal.createEvent(title, start, end, { description: descriptionLines.join('\n') }); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_TYPE, 'servicos'); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_PLATE, placa); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY, 'Cliente (Online)'); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_LOCATION_KEY, AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY); // Same customer-confirmation mechanism createAgendamento already // uses (real WABA send via Zapier, server-side — no browser // involved, unlike SI's own PDF/message buttons). // Phone comes from the token (baked in by si_bookingUrl_ at the time // the Aprovador sent the SI card) — this is the number the Aprovador // was already messaging, so it's guaranteed to be reachable. // sendAgendaWhatsApp will fall back to the Eytan number if the token // predates the phone-in-token change (old links still in the wild). sendAgendaWhatsApp( val.phone, vehicle.cliente, vehicle.modelo, formatAgendaDateTimePt(start), resolvedAddress, '', 'servicos', { source: 'SI (Online)', createdBy: 'Cliente (Online)', placa: placa, ano: vehicle.ano || '', vin: vehicle.vin || '', dateCreated: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm'), } ); si_notifyApprovers_(vehicle, start, resolvedAddress); return { ok: true, start: start.toISOString(), label: formatAgendaDateTimePt(start) }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('createAgendamentoPublic error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // doGet action=agendar&token=... — the public calendar page itself. // Self-contained HTML/JS: fetches this SAME web app's own public // getFreeSlots action (already unauthenticated, no changes needed // there) and posts to createAgendamentoPublic above. function renderAgendarPublicPage_(token) { const val = si_validateBookingToken_(token); const baseUrl = ScriptApp.getService().getUrl(); if (!val.ok) { return HtmlService.createHtmlOutput( '' + '

Link inválido ou expirado

' + '

Entre em contato com a Porsche Service Rio para agendar sua manutenção.

' + '' ).setTitle('Agendamento — Porsche Service Rio'); } const vehicle = si_lookupVehicleForBooking_(val.placa) || {}; const html = ` Agendar Manutenção — Porsche Service Rio ${SI_BOOKING_OG_IMAGE_URL ? `` : ''}
PORSCHE
SERVICE · RIO DE JANEIRO
${vehicle.cliente || ''}
${vehicle.modelo || ''} · ${vehicle.placa || ''}

Escolha o dia

Horários disponíveis

Selecione um dia acima.
`; return HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(html) .setTitle('Agendar Manutenção — Porsche Service Rio') .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1'); } // ── doPost action=createBackfillAgendamento ─────────────────────── // body: { ro_number, servicos_start, servicos_end, servicos_remark?, // create_entrega, entrega_date?, entrega_remark?, tech } // Companion to getRosMissingAgendamento (v2.7.24) — for an open RO // that has NO SERVICOS event at all (a walk-in/urgent job that never // went through the normal scheduling flow), creates a SERVICOS event // (the repair window) and, OPTIONALLY (create_entrega truthy), also a // linked ENTREGA event already marked entregue=true. SERVICOS-only is // for vehicles still actively under service that were simply never // scheduled — not every backfilled RO has already been delivered. // When both are created, the result is fully consistent with the // normal Agendamento data model — no special-casing needed anywhere // downstream (Patio, reports, the Management list itself). // // Deliberately does NOT call sendAgendaWhatsApp or // sendAgendaStatusWebhook — these are historical/catch-up records // being backfilled, not live scheduling/delivery events, so no // customer notification or Zapier webhook should fire for either event. // // entrega_date (when create_entrega is set) must be >= servicos_end // (delivery can't predate the end of the repair) — validated // server-side, mirroring whatever the frontend's date picker enforces. function createBackfillAgendamento(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_authorized' }; const roNumber = String(body.ro_number || '').trim(); if (!roNumber) return { ok: false, error: 'missing_ro_number' }; const createEntrega = !!(body.create_entrega === true || body.create_entrega === '1' || body.create_entrega === 1); // Re-check the RO genuinely still has no SERVICOS event — guards // against a double-submit or another Aprovador having already // backfilled this RO in the meantime. const gate = hasServicosEvent(roNumber); if (gate.exists) return { ok: false, error: 'servicos_event_already_exists' }; const servicosStart = new Date(body.servicos_start); const servicosEnd = new Date(body.servicos_end); if (isNaN(servicosStart.getTime()) || isNaN(servicosEnd.getTime())) { return { ok: false, error: 'invalid_datetime' }; } if (servicosEnd.getTime() < servicosStart.getTime()) { return { ok: false, error: 'servicos_end_before_start' }; } let entregaDate = null; if (createEntrega) { entregaDate = new Date(body.entrega_date); if (isNaN(entregaDate.getTime())) return { ok: false, error: 'invalid_datetime' }; if (entregaDate.getTime() < servicosEnd.getTime()) { return { ok: false, error: 'entrega_before_servicos_end' }; } } // Pull plate/VIN/client name from the ROs sheet itself — a backfill // has no interactive form data to draw from like createAgendamento does. const roData = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const roHeaders = roData[0]; const roRow = roData.slice(1).find(function(r) { return normaliseRO(rowToObj(roHeaders, r).ro_number) === normaliseRO(roNumber); }); const ro = roRow ? rowToObj(roHeaders, roRow) : {}; const locationKey = AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY; const resolvedAddress = resolveAgendaLocationAddress(locationKey); const clientName = ro.cust_name || ''; const plate = ro.plate || ''; const vin = ro.vin || ''; const servicosRemark = String(body.servicos_remark || '').trim(); const entregaRemark = String(body.entrega_remark || '').trim(); const title = 'RO #' + roNumber + ' — ' + clientName + ' — ' + plate; function buildDescription(remark) { return [ 'RO: ' + roNumber, 'Cliente: ' + clientName, 'Placa: ' + plate, 'VIN: ' + vin, 'Local: ' + resolvedAddress, 'Observação: ' + (remark || '[Backfill] Agendamento ausente — criado retroativamente.'), 'Agendado por: ' + (body.tech || ''), 'Criado em: ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm'), ].join('\n'); } // ── SERVICOS event ── const servicosCal = getAgendaCalendar('servicos'); const servicosEvent = servicosCal.createEvent(title, servicosStart, servicosEnd, { description: buildDescription(servicosRemark) }); servicosEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_RO, roNumber); servicosEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_TYPE, 'servicos'); servicosEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_PLATE, plate); servicosEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY, String(body.tech || '')); servicosEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_LOCATION_KEY, locationKey); const result = { ok: true, servicos_event: agendaEventToObj(servicosEvent, 'servicos'), entrega_event: null, }; // ── ENTREGA event — only when requested, created already delivered ── if (createEntrega) { const entregaEnd = new Date(entregaDate.getTime() + 60 * 60000); // 1h slot, same convention as a normal entrega event const entregaCal = getAgendaCalendar('entrega'); const entregaEvent = entregaCal.createEvent(title, entregaDate, entregaEnd, { description: buildDescription(entregaRemark) }); entregaEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_RO, roNumber); entregaEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_TYPE, 'entrega'); entregaEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_PLATE, plate); entregaEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY, String(body.tech || '')); entregaEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_SERVICOS_EVENT_ID, servicosEvent.getId()); entregaEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_ENTREGUE, '1'); // pre-delivered — this is the whole point of the backfill result.entrega_event = agendaEventToObj(entregaEvent, 'entrega'); } return result; } catch (e) { Logger.log('createBackfillAgendamento error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // Both reschedule and field-edit can be sent together or separately. // Field edits rebuild the title + description from scratch (same // format as createAgendamento) and re-stamp tags, so editing stays // consistent with how new events are built. Any field omitted from // body falls back to the event's current tagged/parsed value, so a // partial edit doesn't blank out the rest. Does not change which RO // the event is linked to in a way that breaks history — if you need // to fully relink to a different RO, delete + recreate instead. function updateAgendamento(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_authorized' }; const which = String(body.calendar || '').toLowerCase(); const cal = getAgendaCalendar(which); const event = cal.getEventById(body.event_id); if (!event) return { ok: false, error: 'event_not_found' }; // ── Reschedule (optional) ── if (body.start || body.end) { const start = new Date(body.start); const end = new Date(body.end); if (isNaN(start.getTime()) || isNaN(end.getTime())) { return { ok: false, error: 'invalid_datetime' }; } event.setTime(start, end); // v2.7.30: a pure reschedule (no field edit) on an event with NO // creator tag yet also claims it for whoever is doing the // reschedule — same safe rule as the field-edit path below: only // fills in when genuinely missing, never overwrites a known // creator. Without this, fixing a grey/historical event required // using "Salvar alterações" specifically — an awkward workaround // when all the Aprovador actually wanted to do was reschedule it. if (!event.getTag(EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY)) { event.setTag(EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY, String(body.tech || '')); } } // ── Field edit (optional) — only runs if at least one editable // field was sent in the request, so a pure reschedule call // doesn't unnecessarily rewrite title/description. // // ENTREGA restriction: RO/Cliente/Placa/Veículo/VIN/Telefone are // locked once a SERVICOS event exists for that RO — it doesn't // make sense to edit vehicle/customer data at the delivery stage, // only WHEN it happens (reschedule, handled above) and any // handover note (remark). Enforced server-side, not just hidden // in the UI, so a stray/old client request can't bypass it. // // location_key IS editable for ENTREGA (v2.7.26) — the customer // may want to collect the vehicle from a different address than // where the repair happened. It defaults to SERVICOS' location // only once, at creation time (see createAgendamento); from then // on it's ENTREGA's own independent field, same as remark. const structuralFields = ['ro_number', 'client_name', 'plate', 'modelo', 'year', 'vin', 'mobile', 'location_key']; const editableFields = which === 'entrega' ? ['remark', 'location_key'] : structuralFields.concat('remark'); const isFieldEdit = editableFields.some(function(f) { return body[f] !== undefined; }); if (isFieldEdit) { // Fall back to current values (tag, or title-parsed) for any // field not included in this request, so partial edits don't // blank out the rest. const current = agendaEventToObj(event, which); const merged = { ro_number: which === 'entrega' ? current.ro_number : (body.ro_number !== undefined ? body.ro_number : current.ro_number), client_name: which === 'entrega' ? (current.client_name || '') : (body.client_name !== undefined ? body.client_name : (current.client_name || '')), plate: which === 'entrega' ? current.plate : (body.plate !== undefined ? body.plate : current.plate), modelo: which === 'entrega' ? (current.modelo || '') : (body.modelo !== undefined ? body.modelo : (current.modelo || '')), year: which === 'entrega' ? (current.year || '') : (body.year !== undefined ? body.year : (current.year || '')), vin: which === 'entrega' ? (current.vin || '') : (body.vin !== undefined ? body.vin : (current.vin || '')), mobile: which === 'entrega' ? (current.mobile || '') : (body.mobile !== undefined ? body.mobile : (current.mobile || '')), remark: body.remark !== undefined ? body.remark : (current.remark || ''), location_key: AGENDA_LOCATIONS[body.location_key] ? body.location_key : current.location_key, }; const resolvedAddress = resolveAgendaLocationAddress(merged.location_key); // v2.7.29: if this event has NO creator TAG at all yet (older // events that predate EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY/the color system — the // ones showing grey in the slot picker), claim it for whoever is // editing it now, so it picks up a color going forward. An event // that already has a known creator tag is left untouched, even // if a different Agendador is the one doing this edit — editing // someone else's correctly-attributed event should never steal // its color/credit. const hasOwnCreatedByTag = !!event.getTag(EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY); const attributedCreatedBy = hasOwnCreatedByTag ? event.getTag(EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY) : (body.tech || current.created_by || ''); const title = 'RO #' + merged.ro_number + ' — ' + merged.client_name + ' — ' + merged.plate; const descriptionLines = [ 'RO: ' + merged.ro_number, 'Cliente: ' + merged.client_name, 'Placa: ' + merged.plate, 'Veículo: ' + [merged.modelo, merged.year].filter(Boolean).join(' '), 'VIN: ' + merged.vin, 'Telefone: ' + merged.mobile, 'Local: ' + resolvedAddress, 'Observação: ' + merged.remark, 'Agendado por: ' + attributedCreatedBy, 'Editado por: ' + (body.tech || '') + ' em ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm'), ]; event.setTitle(title); event.setDescription(descriptionLines.join('\n')); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_RO, String(merged.ro_number || '')); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_PLATE, String(merged.plate || '')); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_LOCATION_KEY, merged.location_key); if (!hasOwnCreatedByTag) { event.setTag(EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY, String(attributedCreatedBy || '')); } } return { ok: true, event: agendaEventToObj(event, which) }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('updateAgendamento error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── doPost action=deleteAgendamento ─────────────────────────────── // body: { calendar, event_id, tech } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── MARCAR ENTREGUE SEM AGENDAMENTO (v4.1.2) ───────────────────── // // Aprovador-only. Used when a vehicle is delivered on the spot without // a prior ENTREGA agendamento — creates a backdated ENTREGA calendar // event (start = now, duration = 30 min), marks it entregue=1 // immediately, and runs the full set of side-effects that toggleEntrega // normally handles (PATIO clear, NEW status, priority queue, webhooks, // audit log). Also sends a WhatsApp delivery confirmation to the // customer using the same sendAgendaWhatsApp path as normal ENTREGA. // // The created event is tagged EVT_PROP_MANUAL_ENTREGA='1' so it can be // identified and deleted cleanly by revertEntregaSemAgendamento without // touching any normal ENTREGA events that may exist for the same RO. // // body: { tech, ro_number, plate, client_name, mobile, modelo, year, // vin, cpf, location_key, linked_servicos_event_id? } // Returns: { ok, event_id } on success. // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function markEntregaSemAgendamento(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_authorized' }; const locationKey = AGENDA_LOCATIONS[body.location_key] ? body.location_key : AGENDA_DEFAULT_LOCATION_KEY; const resolvedAddress = resolveAgendaLocationAddress(locationKey); // Event window: now → now + 30 min const start = new Date(); const end = new Date(start.getTime() + 30 * 60 * 1000); const title = 'RO #' + body.ro_number + ' — ' + (body.client_name || '') + ' — ' + (body.plate || ''); const descriptionLines = [ 'RO: ' + (body.ro_number || ''), 'Cliente: ' + (body.client_name || ''), 'Placa: ' + (body.plate || ''), 'Veículo: ' + [body.modelo, body.year].filter(Boolean).join(' '), 'VIN: ' + (body.vin || ''), 'Telefone: ' + (body.mobile || ''), 'Local: ' + resolvedAddress, 'Agendado por: ' + (body.tech || ''), 'Criado em: ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm'), '** Entrega sem agendamento prévio — criada manualmente pelo Aprovador **', ]; const cal = getAgendaCalendar('entrega'); const event = cal.createEvent(title, start, end, { description: descriptionLines.join('\n') }); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_RO, String(body.ro_number || '')); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_TYPE, 'entrega'); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_PLATE, String(body.plate || '')); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_CPF, String(body.cpf || '')); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_CREATEDBY, String(body.tech || '')); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_LOCATION_KEY, locationKey); if (body.linked_servicos_event_id) { event.setTag(EVT_PROP_SERVICOS_EVENT_ID, String(body.linked_servicos_event_id)); } event.setTag(EVT_PROP_ENTREGUE, '1'); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_MANUAL_ENTREGA, '1'); const eventData = agendaEventToObj(event, 'entrega'); // v4.7.2 — CARIMBAR O ESTADO ANTERIOR ANTES DE MEXER EM NADA. // Cada bloco lê o estado atual e grava no evento; só depois limpa. // Ler depois de limpar leria o estado já destruído, então a ordem // aqui não é estilo, é requisito. // 1. Chegada no evento SERVICOS ligado. // v4.7.3 — se o cliente não mandou o id (todo card anterior à // v4.7.3 mandava vazio, e qualquer outro chamador pode omitir), // procura o evento pelo mesmo casamento placa-primeiro/RO-depois // que getROs usa. Sem isto o passo inteiro era um no-op silencioso // e o carro entregue continuava no painel Veículos no Pátio. try { let linkedId = body.linked_servicos_event_id || ''; if (!linkedId) { const chegadaMaps = getChegadaByPlateMap(); const placaNorm = normalisePlate(body.plate); const achado = (placaNorm && chegadaMaps.byPlate[placaNorm]) || chegadaMaps.byRoNumber[normaliseRO(body.ro_number)]; if (achado && achado.event_id) { linkedId = achado.event_id; Logger.log('markEntregaSemAgendamento: evento SERVICOS resolvido por busca (RO ' + body.ro_number + ') — o cliente não enviou linked_servicos_event_id.'); } } if (linkedId) { const linkedEvent = getAgendaCalendar('servicos').getEventById(linkedId); if (linkedEvent) { event.setTag(EVT_PROP_SERVICOS_EVENT_ID, String(linkedId)); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_PREV_CHEGADA, linkedEvent.getTag(EVT_PROP_CHEGADA) === '1' ? '1' : '0'); linkedEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_CHEGADA, '0'); } } } catch (e) { Logger.log('markEntregaSemAgendamento: chegada clear failed — ' + e); } // 2. Linha do PATIO try { const prevPatio = getPatioMap().byRoNumber[normaliseRO(body.ro_number)]; event.setTag(EVT_PROP_PREV_PATIO, (prevPatio && prevPatio.in_patio) ? '1' : '0'); event.setTag(EVT_PROP_PREV_PATIO_SOURCE, String((prevPatio && prevPatio.source) || '')); upsertPatioRow(body.ro_number, false, 'manual_entrega', body.tech); } catch (e) { Logger.log('markEntregaSemAgendamento: PATIO clear failed — ' + e); } // 3. Situação da RO no NEW try { const prevStatus = readNewRepairOrderStatus_(body.ro_number); if (prevStatus !== null) event.setTag(EVT_PROP_PREV_NEW_STATUS, prevStatus); updateNewRepairOrderStatus_(body.ro_number, 'Entregue'); } catch (e) { Logger.log('markEntregaSemAgendamento: NEW status failed — ' + e); } // 4. v4.7.3 — coluna manual_patio da aba ROs: a TERCEIRA fonte do // OU que forma ro.chegada. Sem apagar esta, um carro marcado NO // PÁTIO manualmente continuava com o selo depois de entregue. // Usa _setManualPatioFlag_ e não setManualPatio, para não // sobrescrever a linha do PATIO que o passo 2 acabou de gravar. try { const prevManual = _setManualPatioFlag_(body.ro_number, false); if (prevManual !== null) event.setTag(EVT_PROP_PREV_MANUAL_PATIO, prevManual ? '1' : '0'); } catch (e) { Logger.log('markEntregaSemAgendamento: manual_patio clear failed — ' + e); } // 4. Remove from priority queue try { removeFromPriorityQueue(body.ro_number, body.tech); } catch (e) { Logger.log('markEntregaSemAgendamento: priority queue failed — ' + e); } // 5. Status webhook + audit log sendAgendaStatusWebhook(eventData, 'delivered', body.tech); appendAgendaLog(eventData, 'entrega', 'delivered', body.tech); // 6. WhatsApp delivery confirmation sendAgendaWhatsApp( body.mobile, body.client_name, [body.modelo, body.year].filter(Boolean).join(' '), formatAgendaDateTimePt(start), resolvedAddress, body.ro_number, 'entrega', { source: 'Aprovador (Manual)', createdBy: body.tech || '', placa: body.plate || '', ano: body.year || '', vin: body.vin || '', dateCreated: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm'), } ); return { ok: true, event_id: event.getId(), event: eventData }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('markEntregaSemAgendamento error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── REVERT ENTREGA SEM AGENDAMENTO (v4.1.2) ────────────────────── // body: { tech, ro_number, event_id } // ── PATCH 2 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // revertEntregaSemAgendamento: clear NEW's status even when the event // has already been deleted. // // The old early return on 'event_not_found' is the bug: an RO whose // ENTREGA event is gone is precisely the RO whose status is stranded, // and it was the one case the reset refused to handle. Now the missing // event is treated as "already half-reverted" — finish the job. // // Returns ok:true on that path so the UI clears the pill rather than // leaving the Aprovador staring at an error for an action that did // succeed. `recovered: true` distinguishes it if the client wants to // message it differently. // // The not_a_manual_entrega_event guard is deliberately unchanged: if // the event exists but is a scheduled delivery, this is the wrong // entry point and toggleEntrega() is the correct one. // ── v4.7.2 — QUEBRA-VIDRO: reverter entrega manual pelo NÚMERO DA RO, // rodando direto do editor do Apps Script, sem passar pelo PWA. // // Existe para o caso em que a RO não está alcançável pela interface: // evento criado antes da v4.7.1 (quando a RO sumia da lista no mesmo // clique), ou passadas as 48h de MANUAL_ENTREGA_REVERT_WINDOW_HOURS. // Localiza o evento pela tag de RO + tag de entrega manual, então // entrega tudo para revertEntregaSemAgendamento — mesma função, mesmo // caminho, mesma auditoria. Não duplica lógica de reversão. // // Uso no editor: reverterEntregaManualPorRO('28638', 'Eytan') function reverterEntregaManualPorRO(roNumber, tech) { const alvo = normaliseRO(roNumber); if (!alvo) { Logger.log('reverterEntregaManualPorRO: RO em branco.'); return { ok: false, error: 'ro_vazia' }; } const cal = getAgendaCalendar('entrega'); const desde = new Date(Date.now() - 90 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const ate = new Date(Date.now() + 90 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const achados = cal.getEvents(desde, ate).filter(function(ev) { return ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_MANUAL_ENTREGA) === '1' && normaliseRO(ev.getTag(EVT_PROP_RO)) === alvo; }); if (!achados.length) { Logger.log('reverterEntregaManualPorRO: nenhuma entrega MANUAL encontrada para a RO ' + alvo + ' na janela de 90 dias. Entrega agendada normal não entra aqui — use o toggle ' + 'Confirmar Entrega no Agendamento.'); return { ok: false, error: 'evento_nao_encontrado' }; } if (achados.length > 1) { Logger.log('reverterEntregaManualPorRO: ' + achados.length + ' eventos manuais para a RO ' + alvo + '. Revertendo o mais recente; rode de novo para os anteriores.'); achados.sort(function(a, b) { return b.getStartTime().getTime() - a.getStartTime().getTime(); }); } const res = revertEntregaSemAgendamento({ tech: tech, ro_number: alvo, event_id: achados[0].getId(), }); Logger.log('reverterEntregaManualPorRO(' + alvo + '): ' + JSON.stringify(res)); return res; } function revertEntregaSemAgendamento(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_authorized' }; const cal = getAgendaCalendar('entrega'); const event = cal.getEventById(body.event_id); if (!event) { // Event already gone — clear the orphaned status if we can // identify the RO, then report success. const ro = String(body.ro_number || '').trim(); if (!ro) { Logger.log('revertEntregaSemAgendamento: event missing AND no ro_number in body — ' + 'cannot clear NEW status.'); return { ok: false, error: 'event_not_found' }; } try { updateNewRepairOrderStatus_(ro, ''); Logger.log('revertEntregaSemAgendamento: event ' + body.event_id + ' already deleted — cleared orphaned NEW status for RO ' + ro + '.'); } catch (e) { Logger.log('revertEntregaSemAgendamento: orphan status clear failed — ' + e); return { ok: false, error: 'status_clear_failed' }; } return { ok: true, recovered: true }; } if (event.getTag(EVT_PROP_MANUAL_ENTREGA) !== '1') { return { ok: false, error: 'not_a_manual_entrega_event' }; } const eventData = agendaEventToObj(event, 'entrega'); const roNum = String(body.ro_number || eventData.ro_number || '').trim(); // v4.7.2 — LER AS TAGS DE ESTADO ANTERIOR ANTES DE DELETAR O EVENTO. // Depois do deleteEvent() elas deixam de existir e o revert perderia // a única cópia do estado que precisa restaurar. const prevPatioTag = event.getTag(EVT_PROP_PREV_PATIO); const prevPatioSource = event.getTag(EVT_PROP_PREV_PATIO_SOURCE) || ''; const prevChegadaTag = event.getTag(EVT_PROP_PREV_CHEGADA); const prevNewStatus = event.getTag(EVT_PROP_PREV_NEW_STATUS); const prevManualPatio = event.getTag(EVT_PROP_PREV_MANUAL_PATIO); const linkedServicos = event.getTag(EVT_PROP_SERVICOS_EVENT_ID) || ''; // Evento de antes da v4.7.2: nenhuma tag de estado anterior. O // revert não adivinha — restaura o que dá e avisa o Aprovador. const prevStateUnknown = (prevPatioTag === null || prevPatioTag === undefined || prevPatioTag === ''); event.deleteEvent(); // 1. Situação no NEW — devolve a string anterior quando existe; // só apaga quando não há nada guardado (comportamento antigo, que // continua correto para o caso normal: RO aberta, situação vazia). try { updateNewRepairOrderStatus_(roNum, prevNewStatus != null ? prevNewStatus : ''); } catch (e) { Logger.log('revertEntregaSemAgendamento: NEW status failed — ' + e); } // 2. Linha do PATIO — este era o buraco: o carro voltava para a // lista de ROs e sumia do quadro do pátio, porque marcar entrega // grava in_patio=false e nada nunca desfazia isso. try { if (prevPatioTag === '1') { upsertPatioRow(roNum, true, prevPatioSource || 'entrega_revert', body.tech); } else if (prevPatioTag === '0') { upsertPatioRow(roNum, false, 'entrega_revert', body.tech); } // prevPatioTag ausente → não toca. Ver prevStateUnknown. } catch (e) { Logger.log('revertEntregaSemAgendamento: PATIO restore failed — ' + e); } // 3. Tag de chegada no evento SERVICOS ligado. try { if (linkedServicos && (prevChegadaTag === '1' || prevChegadaTag === '0')) { const linkedEvent = getAgendaCalendar('servicos').getEventById(linkedServicos); if (linkedEvent) linkedEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_CHEGADA, prevChegadaTag); } } catch (e) { Logger.log('revertEntregaSemAgendamento: chegada restore failed — ' + e); } // 4. v4.7.3 — coluna manual_patio. Só mexe quando a tag existe: // entrega anterior à v4.7.3 não carimbou nada, e ligar o flag por // conta própria colocaria no pátio um carro que talvez nunca // estivesse marcado assim. try { if (prevManualPatio === '1' || prevManualPatio === '0') { _setManualPatioFlag_(roNum, prevManualPatio === '1'); } } catch (e) { Logger.log('revertEntregaSemAgendamento: manual_patio restore failed — ' + e); } sendAgendaStatusWebhook(eventData, 'un-delivered', body.tech); appendAgendaLog(eventData, 'entrega', 'un-delivered', body.tech); return { ok: true, prev_state_unknown: prevStateUnknown }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('revertEntregaSemAgendamento error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── PATCH 1 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // deleteAgendamento: clear NEW's status when deleting a confirmed // delivery. // // Tags are read BEFORE deleteEvent() — they are unreadable afterwards. // The status is cleared AFTER the delete succeeds, so a failed delete // never clears the status of a delivery that still exists. // // ro_number comes from the event's own EVT_PROP_RO tag rather than // body: the delete call is generic (any calendar, any event type) and // its body carries only { calendar, event_id, tech }. body.ro_number is // accepted as a fallback in case a caller does supply it. function deleteAgendamento(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_authorized' }; const which = String(body.calendar || '').toLowerCase(); const cal = getAgendaCalendar(which); const event = cal.getEventById(body.event_id); if (!event) return { ok: false, error: 'event_not_found' }; // Capture what we need while the event still exists. let entregaRoToClear = ''; try { if (which === 'entrega' && event.getTag(EVT_PROP_ENTREGUE) === '1') { entregaRoToClear = String(event.getTag(EVT_PROP_RO) || body.ro_number || '').trim(); } } catch (tagErr) { Logger.log('deleteAgendamento: tag read failed — ' + tagErr); } event.deleteEvent(); // Best-effort, mirroring every other NEW write in this file: NEW // lives in a separate project, so a permissions or lookup failure // must not turn a successful delete into a reported error. if (entregaRoToClear) { try { updateNewRepairOrderStatus_(entregaRoToClear, ''); Logger.log('deleteAgendamento: cleared NEW status for RO ' + entregaRoToClear + ' (delivered event deleted).'); } catch (statusErr) { Logger.log('deleteAgendamento: NEW status clear failed — ' + statusErr); } } return { ok: true, status_cleared_for_ro: entregaRoToClear || null }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('deleteAgendamento error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── doPost action=toggleChegada ─────────────────────────────────── // body: { event_id, tech } // SERVICOS-only — toggles whether the customer has physically arrived. // Flips the EVT_PROP_CHEGADA tag on the event ('1' <-> absent/'0'), // and fires CHEGADA_ZAPIER_WEBHOOK_URL with the full event snapshot // every time, so Zapier always gets an explicit "arrived" or // "un-arrived" signal rather than having to infer direction from // silence. Same Aprovadores gate as the rest of Agendamento. ENTREGA // events only ever mirror this value for display (see // agendaEventToObj's ENTREGA live-mirror block) — they're never // passed to this function directly. NOTE: confirming delivery // (toggleEntrega, below) can ALSO clear this tag, independently of // this function — see toggleEntrega's comment for why. function toggleChegada(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_authorized' }; const cal = getAgendaCalendar('servicos'); const event = cal.getEventById(body.event_id); if (!event) return { ok: false, error: 'event_not_found' }; const wasArrived = event.getTag(EVT_PROP_CHEGADA) === '1'; const nowArrived = !wasArrived; event.setTag(EVT_PROP_CHEGADA, nowArrived ? '1' : '0'); // Only stamp/overwrite the timestamp when arriving — un-arriving // intentionally leaves the last known arrival time in place (see // EVT_PROP_CHEGADA_AT comment above). if (nowArrived) { event.setTag(EVT_PROP_CHEGADA_AT, Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX")); } const data = agendaEventToObj(event, 'servicos'); sendAgendaStatusWebhook(data, nowArrived ? 'arrived' : 'un-arrived', body.tech); appendAgendaLog(data, 'servicos', nowArrived ? 'arrived' : 'un-arrived', body.tech); // v3.7.13 — write the durable per-RO PATIO row here too, not just // the calendar tag. This is what getROs() now checks FIRST, ahead // of the plate/date heuristic — see PATIO TAB block above. upsertPatioRow(data.ro_number, nowArrived, 'calendar', body.tech); return { ok: true, chegada: nowArrived, event: data }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('toggleChegada error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── doPost action=toggleEntrega ─────────────────────────────────── // body: { event_id, tech } // ENTREGA-only — toggles whether the vehicle has been physically // handed back to the customer. Flips EVT_PROP_ENTREGUE on the // ENTREGA event itself ('1' <-> absent/'0'). Same Aprovadores gate // as the rest of Agendamento, and fires CHEGADA_ZAPIER_WEBHOOK_URL // (reused — same webhook, different action value: 'delivered' / // 'un-delivered') exactly like toggleChegada. // // SIDE EFFECT, ONE-WAY ONLY: when toggling TO entregue=true, this // also clears EVT_PROP_CHEGADA back to '0' on the LINKED SERVICOS // event (via EVT_PROP_SERVICOS_EVENT_ID — the same permanent pointer // agendaEventToObj's ENTREGA mirror block already relies on), since // a delivered vehicle is no longer on-site. Undoing an ENTREGUE // confirmation (toggling back to false) does NOT restore chegada on // SERVICOS — that's intentionally one-way, per spec, since "entregue // was a mistake" doesn't mean "the vehicle magically reappeared in // the patio." If the SERVICOS link is missing or broken (deleted // event), the entregue toggle still succeeds — clearing chegada is a // best-effort side effect, not a precondition. function toggleEntrega(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_authorized' }; const cal = getAgendaCalendar('entrega'); const event = cal.getEventById(body.event_id); if (!event) return { ok: false, error: 'event_not_found' }; const wasEntregue = event.getTag(EVT_PROP_ENTREGUE) === '1'; const nowEntregue = !wasEntregue; event.setTag(EVT_PROP_ENTREGUE, nowEntregue ? '1' : '0'); // v3.7.13 — moved up from below (was previously computed after the // clear-chegada block) so ro_number is available here too, for the // PATIO tab clear alongside the calendar tag clear. ro_number on an // ENTREGA event is immutable/reliable by design (see agendaEventToObj's // comment on the ENTREGA live-mirror block) — safe to use before the // rest of the function's side effects run. const data = agendaEventToObj(event, 'entrega'); // Clear chegada on the linked SERVICOS event — only when turning // ENTREGUE on, never when undoing it. Best-effort: a missing or // broken link must not block the entregue toggle from succeeding. if (nowEntregue) { try { const linkedId = event.getTag(EVT_PROP_SERVICOS_EVENT_ID) || ''; if (linkedId) { const servicosCal = getAgendaCalendar('servicos'); const linkedEvent = servicosCal.getEventById(linkedId); if (linkedEvent) linkedEvent.setTag(EVT_PROP_CHEGADA, '0'); } } catch (innerErr) { Logger.log('toggleEntrega: failed to clear linked chegada — ' + innerErr); } // v3.7.13 — mirror the same clear into the durable PATIO row: a // delivered vehicle is no longer in the workshop, same reasoning // as the calendar-tag clear right above. Best-effort, same as // that block — never blocks the entregue toggle from succeeding. try { upsertPatioRow(data.ro_number, false, 'entrega_clear', body.tech); } catch (innerErr) { Logger.log('toggleEntrega: failed to clear PATIO row — ' + innerErr); } } // v3.4.8 — update NEW's authoritative status both ways: "Entregue" // on, or blank (re-syncable) on undo. Best-effort: NEW lives in a // separate spreadsheet/project, so a permissions or lookup failure // here must not block the calendar toggle. try { updateNewRepairOrderStatus_(data.ro_number, nowEntregue ? 'Entregue' : ''); } catch (statusErr) { Logger.log('toggleEntrega: failed to update NEW RO status — ' + statusErr); } // v3.7.2 — when confirming delivery (not undoing), shift the // manual Priorities queue so a gap doesn't open up where this // RO's rank used to be (RO #9 becomes #8, etc.) — see // removeFromPriorityQueue's own comment for the full mechanics. // Never fires on undo — un-delivering an RO shouldn't try to // re-insert it back into some arbitrary priority position, that // was never asked for and doesn't have an obvious "correct" spot // to land in anyway. Already wrapped in its own try/catch // internally, matching every other best-effort side-effect here. if (nowEntregue) { removeFromPriorityQueue(data.ro_number, body.tech); } sendAgendaStatusWebhook(data, nowEntregue ? 'delivered' : 'un-delivered', body.tech); appendAgendaLog(data, 'entrega', nowEntregue ? 'delivered' : 'un-delivered', body.tech); return { ok: true, entregue: nowEntregue, event: data }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('toggleEntrega error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── Writes NEW's RepairOrders status column for one RO ───────────── // Called only from toggleEntrega() above — "Entregue" when confirming // delivery, "" (blank) when undoing it, so the RO drops out of // RoSync.gs's entregue-exclusion and gets its status refilled by the // next real OI sync rather than being left on a stale guess. NEW lives // in a SEPARATE Apps Script project (the "NEW-database" project — home // of RoSync.gs / BridgeToOld.gs / Config.gs) that this PWA project // shares no constants with, so NEW is opened directly by spreadsheet // ID here rather than through SPREADSHEET_ID/OS_COL. These four // literals mirror that project's own Config.gs exactly — if OS_COL.ID // or OS_COL.SITUACAO ever change there, update them here too, since // there's no shared source of truth across projects. // // Deliberately does NOT write to OLD directly — BridgeToOld.gs mirrors // NEW into OLD on its own 5-min schedule regardless, and RoSync.gs's // ENTREGUE/ABERTA/FECHADA guard protects the "Entregue" direction from // being overwritten by the next OI sync. Writing straight to OLD // instead would just get clobbered by the very next bridge run, since // bridgeRepairOrders_ copies NEW's situacao into OLD unconditionally, // every time, with no guard of its own. const NEW_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID = '1Wm_MdEczdovRdvV23HNK7DAyjyI2FIWIrdhEwfO9hR4'; const NEW_DB_MAIN_OS_SHEET = 'RepairOrders'; const NEW_DB_OS_COL_ID = 1; // A — matches OS_COL.ID in NEW's Config.gs const NEW_DB_OS_COL_SITUACAO = 12; // L — matches OS_COL.SITUACAO in NEW's Config.gs // v4.7.2 — contrapartida de leitura de updateNewRepairOrderStatus_. // Existe para que markEntregaSemAgendamento possa guardar a situação // ANTES de escrever 'Entregue' por cima, e o revert devolver aquela // mesma string em vez de apagar a célula. Não usa getNewStatusMap // porque aquela lê a planilha NEW inteira para montar um mapa — // desperdício quando se quer uma RO só. Devolve null (não '') quando // a RO não existe no NEW, para distinguir "não achei" de "achei e // estava vazio". function readNewRepairOrderStatus_(roNumber) { try { const ro = String(roNumber || '').trim(); if (!ro) return null; const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(NEW_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID) .getSheetByName(NEW_DB_MAIN_OS_SHEET); if (!sheet) return null; const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return null; const ids = sheet.getRange(2, NEW_DB_OS_COL_ID, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues(); for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) { if (String(ids[i][0]).trim() === ro) { return String(sheet.getRange(i + 2, NEW_DB_OS_COL_SITUACAO).getValue()).trim(); } } return null; } catch (e) { Logger.log('readNewRepairOrderStatus_: falhou — ' + e); return null; } } function updateNewRepairOrderStatus_(roNumber, newStatus) { const ro = String(roNumber || '').trim(); if (!ro) { Logger.log('updateNewRepairOrderStatus_: ENTREGA event has no ro_number — skipped.'); return; } const newSS = SpreadsheetApp.openById(NEW_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID); const sheet = newSS.getSheetByName(NEW_DB_MAIN_OS_SHEET); if (!sheet) { Logger.log('updateNewRepairOrderStatus_: "' + NEW_DB_MAIN_OS_SHEET + '" sheet not found in NEW.'); return; } const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); if (lastRow < 2) return; // RO number is the unique key in NEW's RepairOrders — column A only, // no need to read the full row width just to locate it. const idValues = sheet.getRange(2, NEW_DB_OS_COL_ID, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues(); let targetRow = -1; for (let i = 0; i < idValues.length; i++) { if (String(idValues[i][0]).trim() === ro) { targetRow = i + 2; break; } } if (targetRow === -1) { Logger.log('updateNewRepairOrderStatus_: RO ' + ro + ' not found in NEW RepairOrders — skipped.'); return; } sheet.getRange(targetRow, NEW_DB_OS_COL_SITUACAO).setValue(newStatus); Logger.log('updateNewRepairOrderStatus_: RO ' + ro + ' status set to "' + newStatus + '" in NEW.'); } // Fire-and-forget, like sendAgendaStatusWebhook — a logging failure // must never block the chegada/entregue toggle itself from succeeding. // Appends one row to AgendaLog (v2.7.36) as a durable, human-readable // audit trail independent of the Calendar tags, which are invisible // in the Calendar UI and lost forever if the event is ever deleted. // Logs ALL four toggle directions (arrived/un-arrived/delivered/ // un-delivered) — not just the "on" cases — for a complete history. function appendAgendaLog(eventData, calendarType, action, tech) { try { if (!SHEETS.agendaLog) { Logger.log('appendAgendaLog: AgendaLog sheet not found — skipping'); return; } SHEETS.agendaLog.appendRow([ Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX"), eventData.ro_number || '', eventData.plate || '', eventData.id || eventData.event_id || '', calendarType, action, tech || '', ]); } catch (e) { Logger.log('appendAgendaLog error: ' + e); } } // Fire-and-forget, like sendAgendaWhatsApp — a webhook failure must // never block the chegada/entregue toggle itself from succeeding. // Shared by toggleChegada and toggleEntrega; the action string is // passed explicitly by the caller rather than inferred here, since // the two toggles use different vocabularies ('arrived'/'un-arrived' // vs 'delivered'/'un-delivered'). function sendAgendaStatusWebhook(eventData, action, tech) { try { const payload = { action: action, ro: eventData.ro_number || '', plate: eventData.plate || '', cliente: eventData.client_name || '', telefone: eventData.mobile || '', tech: tech || '', timestamp: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX"), }; UrlFetchApp.fetch(CHEGADA_ZAPIER_WEBHOOK_URL, { method: 'post', contentType: 'application/json', payload: JSON.stringify(payload), muteHttpExceptions: true, }); } catch (e) { Logger.log('sendAgendaStatusWebhook error: ' + e); } } // ── doPost action=updateMileage ─────────────────────────────────── // body: { ro_number, mileage, tech } // Open to ANY tech (no Aprovador gate) — the whole point is that // techs catch missing/placeholder mileage that Approvers didn't // check when opening the RO, not that Approvers themselves fix it. // // Validates mileage as a whole-number integer > 1 (rejects blanks, // non-numeric input, and the placeholder value '1' itself — a tech // re-submitting "1" would defeat the entire point of this feature). // // Writes the new value in TWO places: // 1. THIS spreadsheet's ROs tab — mileage column, and stamps // mileage_source='pwa' so syncROs() (separate sync_all.gs // project) never overwrites it again, regardless of what the // source sheet's column J says afterward. See sync_all.gs // v1.10.0 for the corresponding read-side protection logic. // 2. The OI source spreadsheet itself (ROS_SOURCE_SPREADSHEET_ID, // sheet GID 144677966, column J) — same row, matched by RO // number in column A — so the correction is visible there too, // not just inside the PWA. This does NOT create a feedback loop // with syncROs(): once mileage_source='pwa', sync simply skips // re-reading column J for this RO ever again, so writing here // doesn't get immediately overwritten back by the next 5-min // sync (which is the entire reason mileage_source exists). // // If the source-sheet write fails for any reason (sheet locked, // transient API error, etc.), the ROs tab write is NOT rolled back — // the PWA's own copy is the more important one for techs to see // immediately, and is intentionally written first/independently. The // failure is logged and returned in the response so the caller can // at least be told, but the overall action still reports ok:true // since the PWA-visible state did update successfully. const ROS_SOURCE_SPREADSHEET_ID = '1GVjgwMXSELSdB5p4sJiN8Ffj4vm3UXDxq2ReVKwo4Bc'; const ROS_SOURCE_SHEET_GID = 144677966; const ROS_SOURCE_MILEAGE_COL = 10; // column J, 1-indexed for getRange function updateMileage(body) { try { const roNumber = String(body.ro_number || '').trim(); if (!roNumber) return { ok: false, error: 'missing_ro_number' }; const mileageNum = Number(body.mileage); const isValidInteger = Number.isFinite(mileageNum) && Number.isInteger(mileageNum); if (!isValidInteger || mileageNum <= 1) { return { ok: false, error: 'invalid_mileage' }; } // ── 1. Write to THIS spreadsheet's ROs tab ────────────────── const data = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const roCol = headers.indexOf('ro_number'); const mileageCol = headers.indexOf('mileage'); const mileageSourceCol = headers.indexOf('mileage_source'); if (roCol === -1 || mileageCol === -1 || mileageSourceCol === -1) { return { ok: false, error: 'ros_sheet_missing_mileage_columns' }; } let foundRow = -1; for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (normaliseRO(data[i][roCol]) === normaliseRO(roNumber)) { foundRow = i + 1; break; } } if (foundRow === -1) return { ok: false, error: 'ro_not_found' }; SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, mileageCol + 1).setValue(mileageNum); SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, mileageSourceCol + 1).setValue('pwa'); // ── 2. Write to the OI source spreadsheet, column J ───────── // Best-effort — logged on failure, never blocks the result above // from being reported as a success (see header comment). let sourceWriteOk = true; try { const srcSS = SpreadsheetApp.openById(ROS_SOURCE_SPREADSHEET_ID); const srcSheets = srcSS.getSheets(); let srcSheet = null; for (let i = 0; i < srcSheets.length; i++) { if (srcSheets[i].getSheetId() === ROS_SOURCE_SHEET_GID) { srcSheet = srcSheets[i]; break; } } if (!srcSheet) { Logger.log('updateMileage: source sheet GID ' + ROS_SOURCE_SHEET_GID + ' not found'); sourceWriteOk = false; } else { const srcData = srcSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); let srcRowIndex = -1; for (let i = 0; i < srcData.length; i++) { if (normaliseRO(srcData[i][0]) === normaliseRO(roNumber)) { srcRowIndex = i + 1; break; } } if (srcRowIndex === -1) { Logger.log('updateMileage: RO ' + roNumber + ' not found in source sheet — PWA ROs tab still updated'); sourceWriteOk = false; } else { srcSheet.getRange(srcRowIndex, ROS_SOURCE_MILEAGE_COL).setValue(mileageNum); } } } catch (sourceErr) { Logger.log('updateMileage: source sheet write failed — ' + sourceErr); sourceWriteOk = false; } return { ok: true, mileage: mileageNum, source_write_ok: sourceWriteOk }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('updateMileage error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── doPost action=syncRoNow ─────────────────────────────────────── // body: { ro_number, tech } // // APROVADOR-GATED. The ↻ icon is hidden for non-Aprovadores in // index.html, but that is cosmetic — this check is the real gate. // Anyone can POST to the web app URL. // // Purpose: an RO was updated in OI and a tech is standing at the car // waiting for the joblines. The scheduled pipeline takes up to ~15 // minutes end to end, and for any RO whose source row sits below // `syncROs_lastSourceRow` the update may never arrive at all (the // RO 29845 failure mode). This gives an Aprovador a one-tap pull. // // What it does NOT do: it cannot conjure data that hasn't reached the // OLD spreadsheet yet. If OI → NEW (RoSync.gs) or NEW → OLD (the // bridge) haven't run, this honestly reports "nothing new". See // SOD_UPSTREAM_KICK_URL below for wiring those legs in later. // // Every call is logged to the SyncNow tab — who, which RO, what // changed. In a system where an unexplained write cost two days of // forensics, a manual write path that leaves no trace is not worth // having. const SYNC_NOW_COOLDOWN_SEC = 45; const SYNC_NOW_LOG_TAB = 'SyncNow'; // Set this once the OI → NEW leg is exposed as a web app in the // RoSync/NEW-database project (see notes at the bottom of this file). // Leave '' and the upstream kick is skipped, cleanly and visibly. const SOD_UPSTREAM_KICK_URL = ''; const SOD_UPSTREAM_KICK_TOKEN = ''; function syncRoNow(body) { const tech = String((body && body.tech) || '').trim(); const ro = normaliseRO((body && body.ro_number) || ''); let result = null; let upstream = { attempted: false }; try { if (!ro) { result = { ok: false, error: 'missing_ro_number' }; } else if (!tech) { result = { ok: false, error: 'missing_tech' }; } else if (!isApproverSync(tech)) { result = { ok: false, error: 'not_approver' }; } if (!result) { // Cooldown — stops an impatient double-tap from firing two full // source scans back to back (each is ~9s of whole-sheet reads). // Per RO, not per user: two Aprovadores hammering the same card // is the same load on the same sheets. const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache(); const cacheKey = 'syncnow_' + ro; if (cache.get(cacheKey)) { result = { ok: false, error: 'cooldown', retry_in: SYNC_NOW_COOLDOWN_SEC }; } else { cache.put(cacheKey, '1', SYNC_NOW_COOLDOWN_SEC); // Optional upstream kick: OI → NEW → OLD. Best-effort and // non-blocking — the OLD → PWA leg below is useful on its own. if (SOD_UPSTREAM_KICK_URL) { try { const resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(SOD_UPSTREAM_KICK_URL, { method: 'post', contentType: 'application/json', muteHttpExceptions: true, payload: JSON.stringify({ action: 'syncRoNowUpstream', ro_number: ro, token: SOD_UPSTREAM_KICK_TOKEN }), }); upstream = { attempted: true, code: resp.getResponseCode(), body: resp.getContentText().slice(0, 500) }; } catch (upErr) { upstream = { attempted: true, error: String(upErr) }; } } // Direct call — SyncOnDemand.gs is in this same project. // Checked rather than assumed so a missing file reports itself // instead of throwing into the catch below. if (typeof syncSingleRO !== 'function') { result = { ok: false, error: 'syncSingleRO_not_found' }; } else { result = syncSingleRO(ro); } } } } catch (e) { Logger.log('syncRoNow error: ' + e); result = { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } // Logged for EVERY outcome, including failures — the point of the // audit tab is that a manual write path leaves a trace even when it // writes nothing. _syncNowLog(tech, ro, result, upstream); if (result && result.ok) { result.upstream = upstream; result.summary = _syncNowSummary(result); } return result; } // Human-readable one-liner for the toast. Written from the // Aprovador's side of the screen: what changed on the car, not what // happened to the spreadsheet. function _syncNowSummary(r) { const jl = r.joblines || {}; const parts = []; if (jl.created) parts.push(jl.created + (jl.created === 1 ? ' jobline nova' : ' joblines novas')); if (jl.updated) parts.push(jl.updated + (jl.updated === 1 ? ' jobline atualizada' : ' joblines atualizadas')); const ros = r.ros || {}; if (ros.action === 'created') { parts.push('RO adicionada'); } else if (ros.action === 'updated' && ros.changed && ros.changed.length) { parts.push('RO: ' + ros.changed.join(', ')); } else if (ros.action === 'skipped') { return 'RO ' + String(ros.status || '').toLowerCase() + ' na origem — nada a sincronizar.'; } else if (ros.action === 'absent') { return 'Ainda não chegou nada novo da OI para esta RO.'; } if (!parts.length) return 'Já estava atualizada — nada mudou.'; return 'Atualizado: ' + parts.join(' · '); } function _syncNowLog(tech, ro, result, upstream) { try { let sheet = SS.getSheetByName(SYNC_NOW_LOG_TAB); if (!sheet) { sheet = SS.insertSheet(SYNC_NOW_LOG_TAB); sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 6).setValues([[ 'timestamp', 'tech', 'ro_number', 'ok', 'result', 'upstream' ]]); } sheet.appendRow([ Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm:ss'), tech, ro, result && result.ok ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE', JSON.stringify(result || {}).slice(0, 4000), JSON.stringify(upstream || {}).slice(0, 1000), ]); } catch (e) { // Logging must never break the sync itself. Logger.log('_syncNowLog failed: ' + e); } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── PATIO TAB — DURABLE SOURCE OF TRUTH (v3.7.13) ───────────────── // Replaces plate/date heuristics with one row per RO, keyed directly // by ro_number. Root problem this fixes: ro.chegada used to be // entirely RE-COMPUTED on every getROs() call by scanning a ±14-day // Calendar window and picking whichever SERVICOS event's start time // was closest to today for that plate (see getChegadaByPlateMap // above) — nothing durable was ever written per-RO. That meant an // event outside the window, a plate that didn't normalise identically // on both sides, or a "closest" event that wasn't actually the right // one could all silently make an already-arrived RO read as NOT in // patio — which is exactly why some ROs were missing from techs' // lists despite the pill being tapped. // // PATIO sheet columns: ro_number | in_patio | patio_at | source | tech | updated_at // One row per RO (upserted, not append-only — AgendaLog above already // covers the historical audit trail for the calendar-driven toggles). // source is one of: 'calendar' (real Confirmar Chegada), 'manual' // (Aprovador manual override, mirrors setManualPatio), 'entrega_clear' // (auto-cleared because the vehicle was delivered). const PATIO_HEADERS = ['ro_number', 'in_patio', 'patio_at', 'source', 'tech', 'updated_at']; // Self-creates the PATIO tab + headers if missing, and refreshes // SHEETS.patio for the current execution (mirrors ensureManualPatio- // Columns' self-healing pattern, but for a whole tab rather than // columns on an existing one). Safe to call on every read/write — // cheap no-op once the tab exists. function ensurePatioSheet() { let sheet = SS.getSheetByName('PATIO'); if (!sheet) { sheet = SS.insertSheet('PATIO'); sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, PATIO_HEADERS.length).setValues([PATIO_HEADERS]); } SHEETS.patio = sheet; return sheet; } // Reads the PATIO tab ONCE → { byRoNumber: { ro_number(normalised): { in_patio, patio_at, source } } }. // Same batching pattern as every other *Map() helper in getROs() — // one sheet read per request, not one per RO. function getPatioMap() { const byRoNumber = {}; try { const sheet = ensurePatioSheet(); const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); if (data.length < 2) return { byRoNumber: byRoNumber }; const headers = data[0]; const roCol = headers.indexOf('ro_number'); const flagCol = headers.indexOf('in_patio'); const atCol = headers.indexOf('patio_at'); const sourceCol = headers.indexOf('source'); data.slice(1).forEach(function(row) { const roNum = normaliseRO(row[roCol]); if (!roNum) return; byRoNumber[roNum] = { in_patio: row[flagCol] === true || String(row[flagCol]).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE', patio_at: atCol !== -1 ? row[atCol] : null, source: sourceCol !== -1 ? row[sourceCol] : null, }; }); } catch (e) { Logger.log('getPatioMap error: ' + e); } return { byRoNumber: byRoNumber }; } // Upserts the single current-state row for one RO — finds an existing // row by ro_number and overwrites it, or appends a new one. Called // from toggleChegada, toggleEntrega (clear side-effect), and // setManualPatio, so every path that can change "in patio" status // writes through this one function. Silently no-ops on a blank // ro_number (e.g. a SERVICOS event with no RO number tagged/parsed // yet) — nothing to key the row on; the old plate/date-matching path // still covers that edge case as a fallback in getROs() below, so // this never made behaviour worse than the original for that case. function upsertPatioRow(roNumber, inPatio, source, tech) { try { const roNorm = normaliseRO(roNumber); if (!roNorm) return; const sheet = ensurePatioSheet(); const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data.length ? data[0] : PATIO_HEADERS; const roCol = headers.indexOf('ro_number'); const flagCol = headers.indexOf('in_patio'); const atCol = headers.indexOf('patio_at'); const sourceCol = headers.indexOf('source'); const techCol = headers.indexOf('tech'); const updatedCol = headers.indexOf('updated_at'); const nowIso = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX"); let foundRow = -1; for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (normaliseRO(data[i][roCol]) === roNorm) { foundRow = i + 1; break; } } const rowValues = [roNumber, inPatio, inPatio ? nowIso : (foundRow === -1 ? null : data[foundRow - 1][atCol]), source, tech || '', nowIso]; if (foundRow === -1) { sheet.appendRow(rowValues); } else { sheet.getRange(foundRow, roCol + 1).setValue(roNumber); sheet.getRange(foundRow, flagCol + 1).setValue(inPatio); // patio_at: only overwrite when arriving, same "leave last known // time in place" rule as EVT_PROP_CHEGADA_AT / manual_patio_at. if (inPatio) sheet.getRange(foundRow, atCol + 1).setValue(nowIso); sheet.getRange(foundRow, sourceCol + 1).setValue(source); sheet.getRange(foundRow, techCol + 1).setValue(tech || ''); sheet.getRange(foundRow, updatedCol + 1).setValue(nowIso); } } catch (e) { Logger.log('upsertPatioRow error: ' + e); } } // ── ONE-TIME BACKFILL: seed PATIO from currently-computed chegada ── // Run ONCE manually from the Apps Script editor after deploying // v3.7.13. Walks every RO exactly like getROs() does today (same // plate/RO-number calendar match + manual_patio OR), and for every RO // that currently computes chegada=true, writes a row into PATIO — // so vehicles already (correctly or not) marked as arrived get an // immediate, durable, reliable row instead of waiting for someone to // re-tap the pill. Safe to re-run: upsertPatioRow always overwrites // with the same computed state, never duplicates rows. function backfillPatioFromExistingSignals() { try { const roSheet = SHEETS.ros; const data = roSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const rows = data.slice(1).map(function(r) { return rowToObj(headers, r); }); const chegadaMaps = getChegadaByPlateMap(); let seeded = 0; rows.forEach(function(ro) { const roPlateNormalised = normalisePlate(ro.plate); let chegadaMatch = chegadaMaps.byPlate[roPlateNormalised]; if (!chegadaMatch && !roPlateNormalised) { chegadaMatch = chegadaMaps.byRoNumber[normaliseRO(ro.ro_number)]; } const manualPatioOn = ro.manual_patio === true || String(ro.manual_patio).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'; const isInPatio = (chegadaMatch ? chegadaMatch.chegada : false) || manualPatioOn; if (!isInPatio) return; // only seeding currently-arrived ROs — absence just means "never marked", no row needed upsertPatioRow(ro.ro_number, true, manualPatioOn ? 'manual' : 'calendar', 'backfill'); seeded++; }); Logger.log('✅ backfillPatioFromExistingSignals done — seeded ' + seeded + ' PATIO row(s).'); } catch (e) { Logger.log('❌ backfillPatioFromExistingSignals error: ' + e); } } // ── WIDE BACKFILL — fixes the narrow-window blind spot above ─────── // backfillPatioFromExistingSignals() (above) reuses getChegadaByPlateMap(), // which only scans Calendar events in a ±14-day window around today — // same window getROs() uses on every request, for the same performance // reason. That's fine for LIVE lookups, but means the narrow backfill // silently skipped any RO whose SERVICOS appointment date falls outside // that window even though the vehicle is still physically on-site and // still open — e.g. a car that arrived 6 weeks ago and hasn't been // delivered yet. Confirmed in practice: the narrow backfill only found // 45 vehicles, which is almost certainly "appointment date within 2 // weeks of today", not "actually in the patio right now". // // This version is ONE-TIME-ONLY (never called from getROs() or any // live path), so it can afford to be slow and wide: scans a much // larger window (default 180 days back, 14 days forward — covers // basically any RO that's realistically still open) and, unlike // getChegadaByPlateMap's "closest event wins" rule, takes an OR across // EVERY matching event for a plate/RO — if ANY event for that vehicle // is currently tagged chegada=1, it counts, regardless of how far its // date is from today. Also re-checks manual_patio (not date-limited, // so already fully covered by the narrow version, but no harm re- // running it here for a single source of truth in one pass). // // Run this ONCE manually from the Apps Script editor. Safe to re-run — // upsertPatioRow always overwrites with the freshly-computed state. function backfillPatioWide(daysBack, daysForward) { daysBack = daysBack || 180; daysForward = daysForward || 14; try { const cal = getAgendaCalendar('servicos'); const start = new Date(Date.now() - daysBack * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const end = new Date(Date.now() + daysForward * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const events = cal.getEvents(start, end); // OR across every event for a given plate/RO — any tagged chegada=1 // wins, no "closest to today" narrowing like the live-path version. const arrivedByPlate = {}; // plate(normalised) -> true const arrivedByRoNumber = {}; // ro_number(normalised) -> true events.forEach(function(ev) { const obj = agendaEventToObj(ev, 'servicos'); if (!obj.chegada) return; const plate = normalisePlate(obj.plate); if (plate) arrivedByPlate[plate] = true; const roNum = normaliseRO(obj.ro_number); if (roNum) arrivedByRoNumber[roNum] = true; }); Logger.log('ℹ backfillPatioWide: scanned ' + events.length + ' SERVICOS events (' + daysBack + 'd back / ' + daysForward + 'd forward), found ' + Object.keys(arrivedByPlate).length + ' distinct arrived plate(s) and ' + Object.keys(arrivedByRoNumber).length + ' distinct arrived RO number(s) (no-plate fallback).'); const roSheet = SHEETS.ros; const data = roSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const rows = data.slice(1).map(function(r) { return rowToObj(headers, r); }); let seeded = 0, alreadyHad = 0; const existingPatio = getPatioMap().byRoNumber; rows.forEach(function(ro) { const roNorm = normaliseRO(ro.ro_number); if (existingPatio[roNorm] && existingPatio[roNorm].in_patio) { alreadyHad++; return; } const roPlateNormalised = normalisePlate(ro.plate); const calendarHit = (roPlateNormalised && arrivedByPlate[roPlateNormalised]) || (!roPlateNormalised && arrivedByRoNumber[roNorm]); const manualPatioOn = ro.manual_patio === true || String(ro.manual_patio).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'; if (!calendarHit && !manualPatioOn) return; upsertPatioRow(ro.ro_number, true, manualPatioOn ? 'manual' : 'calendar', 'backfill_wide'); seeded++; }); Logger.log('✅ backfillPatioWide done — seeded ' + seeded + ' additional PATIO row(s) (' + alreadyHad + ' RO(s) already had an in_patio row from the narrow backfill).'); } catch (e) { Logger.log('❌ backfillPatioWide error: ' + e); } } // One-time migration tool: lets an Aprovador manually flag an old // RO as "no Pátio" when it predates the SERVICOS/Confirmar Chegada // workflow (or has an event too old/awkward to backfill live) — // see RO 28682. Deliberately NOT a live/automatic mechanism: it // never touches Calendar, never scans a date range, and costs // nothing on every RO-list load (same reasoning as mileage's manual // override, which this closely mirrors — new_manual_patio/_at // columns on the ROs sheet, same shape as mileage/mileage_source). // // getROs() ORs this flag into ro.chegada — a manually-tagged RO // shows 🅿️ NO PÁTIO exactly like a calendar-confirmed one, on the // RO list, in "Veículos no Pátio", on the Gantt ("Data de entrada" // falls back to manual_patio_at when no calendar chegada_at exists), // AND on the Patio dashboard — that last one for free, since Patio // reads ro.chegada straight from this PWA's ?action=ros endpoint // (see getChegadaStatusForROs in Patio's Code.gs); no Patio-side // change needed at all. // // New ROs should never need this — they get chegada from the real // SERVICOS Confirmar Chegada workflow. This exists purely to make // legacy vehicles compatible with the app during the migration. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Adds manual_patio / manual_patio_at columns to the ROs sheet if // they don't exist yet — safe, additive, non-destructive (same // migration pattern as ensurePartsRequestsHeaders' received_quantity // backfill). Existing rows are untouched; rowToObj() picks up the // new columns by header name as soon as they exist. function ensureManualPatioColumns() { const sheet = SHEETS.ros; if (!sheet) return; const lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); const headerRow = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; if (headerRow.indexOf('manual_patio') === -1) { sheet.getRange(1, lastCol + 1).setValue('manual_patio'); } const headerRow2 = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0]; if (headerRow2.indexOf('manual_patio_at') === -1) { sheet.getRange(1, sheet.getLastColumn() + 1).setValue('manual_patio_at'); } } // ── doPost action=setManualPatio ────────────────────────────────── // body: { ro_number, on (bool), tech } // Aprovador-gated, toggleable both directions (same tap-pattern as // Confirmar Chegada — mirrors toggleChegada's UX, just without any // Calendar involvement at all). Stamps manual_patio_at only when // turning ON, same rule as EVT_PROP_CHEGADA_AT — turning off leaves // the last known tag time in place rather than clearing it. // v4.7.3 — escrita direta da coluna manual_patio, sem o gate de // Aprovador nem o upsert de PATIO que setManualPatio faz. Existe // porque markEntregaSemAgendamento/revertEntregaSemAgendamento já // validaram o Aprovador e já cuidam da linha do PATIO por conta // própria — chamar setManualPatio dali sobrescreveria a linha do // PATIO com o valor errado no meio da operação. Devolve o valor // ANTERIOR (bool), para o marcar poder carimbá-lo no evento, ou null // se a RO não existe na aba. function _setManualPatioFlag_(roNumber, on) { try { const roNorm = normaliseRO(roNumber); if (!roNorm) return null; ensureManualPatioColumns(); const data = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const roCol = headers.indexOf('ro_number'); const flagCol = headers.indexOf('manual_patio'); const atCol = headers.indexOf('manual_patio_at'); if (roCol === -1 || flagCol === -1) return null; for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (normaliseRO(data[i][roCol]) !== roNorm) continue; const anterior = data[i][flagCol] === true || String(data[i][flagCol]).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'; SHEETS.ros.getRange(i + 1, flagCol + 1).setValue(on); // manual_patio_at segue a mesma regra do resto do sistema: só se // escreve ao LIGAR, nunca ao desligar — preserva a última hora // de chegada conhecida (ver upsertPatioRow / EVT_PROP_CHEGADA_AT). if (on && atCol !== -1) { SHEETS.ros.getRange(i + 1, atCol + 1).setValue( Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX") ); } return anterior; } return null; // RO não encontrada na aba ROs } catch (e) { Logger.log('_setManualPatioFlag_ error: ' + e); return null; } } function setManualPatio(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_authorized' }; const roNumber = String(body.ro_number || '').trim(); if (!roNumber) return { ok: false, error: 'missing_ro_number' }; const on = !!(body.on === true || body.on === '1' || body.on === 1); ensureManualPatioColumns(); const data = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const roCol = headers.indexOf('ro_number'); const flagCol = headers.indexOf('manual_patio'); const atCol = headers.indexOf('manual_patio_at'); if (roCol === -1 || flagCol === -1 || atCol === -1) { return { ok: false, error: 'ros_sheet_missing_manual_patio_columns' }; } let foundRow = -1; for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (normaliseRO(data[i][roCol]) === normaliseRO(roNumber)) { foundRow = i + 1; break; } } if (foundRow === -1) return { ok: false, error: 'ro_not_found' }; SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, flagCol + 1).setValue(on); if (on) { SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, atCol + 1).setValue( Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX") ); } // v3.7.13 — also upsert the unified PATIO row, so a manual tag is // just as durable/reliable as a calendar-confirmed one, both read // from the same place in getROs(). The legacy manual_patio/_at // columns on the ROs sheet are left in place (unchanged) since the // existing "NO PÁTIO (manual)" pill on the RO detail screen still // reads ro.manual_patio directly — no frontend change needed. upsertPatioRow(roNumber, on, 'manual', body.tech); return { ok: true, manual_patio: on }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('setManualPatio error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // EM_ESPERA — RO hold / postponement // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Lets an Aprovador take an RO out of the daily allocation without // closing it: awaiting payment, awaiting technical info, awaiting an // insurer, etc. While the flag is on, capCalcularAlocacao drops the // RO from the candidate pool BEFORE sorting — it is not given a low // priority. Low priority still competes, still consumes reasoning, // and resurfaces the moment the shop runs dry, which is exactly wrong // for a car nobody is allowed to touch. // // The RO never disappears from view: capEscreverFlagsEmROs writes // capacity_flag = EM_ESPERA, and the Patio board reads hold_flag off // action=ros directly. Out of the queue, never out of sight. // // Modelled on setManualPatio throughout — same Aprovador gate, same // additive column migration, same two-way toggle.
