The one addition is // the HoldAudit tab: who postponed what, why, and when is exactly the // thing someone will want to reconstruct three weeks later. // Canonical list. index.html carries an identical copy (HOLD_REASONS // there) — the same deliberate duplication MOTIVO_OPTIONS already has // for priority. Difference: the server VALIDATES here, so a stale // client copy produces a visible invalid_reason error rather than // quietly writing dirty data. // // "Aguardando peça" is deliberately ABSENT. The parts gate already // exists in capCalcularAlocacao (osBloqueadaPecas, derived from the // Parts tab). If the same real-world fact can be expressed through two // independent mechanisms, they will eventually disagree, and then // neither one deserves to be trusted. Parts stay a Parts-tab concern. const HOLD_REASONS = [ 'Aguardando aprovação do cliente', 'Aguardando pagamento', 'Aguardando informação técnica (Porsche / PIWIS / TSB)', 'Aguardando autorização de garantia', 'Aguardando seguradora', 'Aguardando terceiro (funilaria, retífica, tapeçaria, vidros)', 'Veículo indisponível / cliente com o carro', 'Aguardando decisão interna', 'Outro', ]; // Additive column migration on ROs — same idempotent pattern as // ensureManualPatioColumns. Existing rows are untouched; rowToObj() // starts seeing the columns by name as soon as they exist. // // Why dedicated columns instead of capacity_flag: capacity_flag is // owned by the engine and rewritten in full on every hourly run // (capEscreverFlagsEmROs). State decided by a human cannot live in a // column that a trigger blanks every hour. function ensureHoldColumns() { const sheet = SHEETS.ros; if (!sheet) return; ['hold_flag', 'hold_reason', 'hold_note', 'hold_since', 'hold_by'].forEach(function(col) { const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0]; if (headers.indexOf(col) === -1) { sheet.getRange(1, sheet.getLastColumn() + 1).setValue(col); Logger.log('ensureHoldColumns: coluna adicionada em ROs: ' + col); } }); } // History tab. The columns on ROs hold CURRENT state; this tab holds // what happened. That split is what lets releasing a hold clear the // reason from the RO row without losing anything. function ensureHoldAuditSheet() { const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); let sheet = ss.getSheetByName('HoldAudit'); if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet('HoldAudit'); sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 6).setValues([['ro_number', 'timestamp', 'aprovador', 'acao', 'motivo', 'nota']]); sheet.setFrozenRows(1); } return sheet; } function logHold(roNumber, approver, acao, motivo, nota) { try { const sheet = ensureHoldAuditSheet(); sheet.appendRow([ String(roNumber), Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX"), String(approver || ''), acao, // 'ESPERA_ON' | 'ESPERA_OFF' String(motivo || ''), String(nota || ''), ]); } catch (e) { // Audit never takes down the operation: the flag itself is what the // engine reads. A failure here becomes a log line, not a user error. Logger.log('logHold falhou: ' + e); } } // ── doPost action=setHold ───────────────────────────────────────── // body: { ro_number, on (bool), reason, note, tech } // // Gated server-side by isApproverSync — does not trust the frontend to // have hidden the button. Same posture as createAgendamento/setPriority. function setHold(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_authorized' }; const roNumber = String(body.ro_number || '').trim(); if (!roNumber) return { ok: false, error: 'missing_ro_number' }; const on = !!(body.on === true || body.on === '1' || body.on === 1); const reason = String(body.reason || '').trim(); const note = String(body.note || '').trim(); if (on) { if (HOLD_REASONS.indexOf(reason) === -1) return { ok: false, error: 'invalid_reason' }; // "Outro" with no note is a hold with no explanation at all — // three weeks later nobody can reconstruct why. Mandatory. if (reason === 'Outro' && !note) return { ok: false, error: 'note_required_for_outro' }; } ensureHoldColumns(); const data = SHEETS.ros.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const roCol = headers.indexOf('ro_number'); const flagCol = headers.indexOf('hold_flag'); const reasonCol = headers.indexOf('hold_reason'); const noteCol = headers.indexOf('hold_note'); const sinceCol = headers.indexOf('hold_since'); const byCol = headers.indexOf('hold_by'); if ([roCol, flagCol, reasonCol, noteCol, sinceCol, byCol].indexOf(-1) !== -1) { return { ok: false, error: 'ros_sheet_missing_hold_columns' }; } let foundRow = -1; for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (normaliseRO(data[i][roCol]) === normaliseRO(roNumber)) { foundRow = i + 1; break; } } if (foundRow === -1) return { ok: false, error: 'ro_not_found' }; const since = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'America/Sao_Paulo', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX"); if (on) { SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, flagCol + 1).setValue(true); SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, reasonCol + 1).setValue(reason); SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, noteCol + 1).setValue(note); SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, sinceCol + 1).setValue(since); SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, byCol + 1).setValue(String(body.tech || '')); logHold(roNumber, body.tech, 'ESPERA_ON', reason, note); } else { // Releasing clears EVERYTHING. Deliberate divergence from // setManualPatio (which preserves manual_patio_at on toggle-off): // a stale manual_patio_at is still a true fact about when the car // arrived, whereas a stale hold_reason on an RO that is no longer // held is just noise waiting to mislead someone. The history // lives in HoldAudit. const anterior = String(data[foundRow - 1][reasonCol] || ''); SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, flagCol + 1).setValue(false); SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, reasonCol + 1).setValue(''); SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, noteCol + 1).setValue(''); SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, sinceCol + 1).setValue(''); SHEETS.ros.getRange(foundRow, byCol + 1).setValue(''); logHold(roNumber, body.tech, 'ESPERA_OFF', anterior, ''); } // Latency note: the engine runs on the hourly trigger // (alocarCapacidadeHoraria), so the RO leaves the allocation on the // NEXT run, not instantly — and that trigger has a business-day // guard, so a Saturday hold does not reach capacity_flag until // Monday. Irrelevant in practice for something measured in days, // but worth knowing while testing so a working toggle isn't // mistaken for a broken one. The PWA pill and the Patio banner both // read hold_flag directly and update immediately. return { ok: true, hold_flag: on, hold_reason: on ? reason : '' }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('setHold error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── TIME-BASED TRIGGER: Agendamento WhatsApp reminders ──────────── // Run this manually ONCE from the Apps Script editor (function // dropdown → installAgendaReminderTrigger → Run) to install an // hourly time-based trigger. Do NOT call checkAgendaReminders() // directly as a trigger target without going through this — it sets // up the actual Apps Script trigger that calls it. function installAgendaReminderTrigger() { // Remove any existing trigger for this function first, so running // this twice doesn't create duplicate triggers (which would send // every reminder twice per hour). ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function(t) { if (t.getHandlerFunction() === 'checkAgendaReminders') { ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); } }); ScriptApp.newTrigger('checkAgendaReminders').timeBased().everyHours(1).create(); Logger.log('Installed hourly trigger for checkAgendaReminders.'); } // Triggers 2-4 of 4: scheduled reminders (2 days / 1 day / 4 hours // before). Runs hourly (see installAgendaReminderTrigger). For each // reminder definition in AGENDA_REMINDERS, scans both calendars for // events whose start time falls within that reminder's window from // now, and — if that reminder's tag isn't already stamped on the // event — sends the WhatsApp message and stamps the tag so an hourly // re-run never double-sends. Each event is independent; one event's // messaging failure doesn't affect any other event in the same run. function checkAgendaReminders() { const now = new Date(); ['servicos', 'entrega'].forEach(function(which) { let cal; try { cal = getAgendaCalendar(which); } catch (e) { Logger.log('checkAgendaReminders: ' + e); return; } // Widest window across all reminder definitions, so one // getEvents() call covers every reminder type for this calendar. const maxHoursBefore = Math.max.apply(null, AGENDA_REMINDERS.map(function(r) { return r.hoursBefore + r.windowHours / 2; })); const rangeEnd = new Date(now.getTime() + maxHoursBefore * 60 * 60 * 1000); const events = cal.getEvents(now, rangeEnd); events.forEach(function(ev) { AGENDA_REMINDERS.forEach(function(reminder) { try { if (ev.getTag(reminder.tag)) return; // already sent const hoursUntilStart = (ev.getStartTime().getTime() - now.getTime()) / (60 * 60 * 1000); const windowStart = reminder.hoursBefore - reminder.windowHours / 2; const windowEnd = reminder.hoursBefore + reminder.windowHours / 2; if (hoursUntilStart < windowStart || hoursUntilStart > windowEnd) return; // outside this reminder's window const data = agendaEventToObj(ev, which); // v2.7.13: passes `which` as cardType — same fix as // createAgendamento, so ENTREGA reminders also get the // green "pronto para retirada" card instead of the red // SERVICOS design. const sent = sendAgendaWhatsApp( data.mobile, data.client_name, [data.modelo, data.year].filter(Boolean).join(' '), formatAgendaDateTimePt(ev.getStartTime()), data.location_address, data.ro_number, which ); if (sent) ev.setTag(reminder.tag, 'true'); } catch (e) { Logger.log('checkAgendaReminders: error on event ' + ev.getId() + ': ' + e); } }); }); }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── PRIORITY SYSTEM (v2.7.47, merged) ───────────────────────── // Priorities tab: one row per RO. Col A = ro_number. Subsequent // columns are appended per reassignment batch — header row stores // the ISO timestamp of each batch, data rows store the priority // number assigned to that RO in that batch. Current priority = // rightmost non-empty value in each row. // // Reorder logic (insert-at-N, shift others down): // - Assigning priority 2 to an RO that was 5 inserts it at 2 // and shifts all ROs with priority >= 2 (except the target) // down by 1, maintaining a clean sequential list. // - Unpriorized ROs are never affected by a reorder. // - All changes for a batch are written as one new column, // stamped with the current timestamp. // // Only Aprovadores can call setPriority. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function ensurePrioritiesSheet() { const ss = SS; let sheet = ss.getSheetByName('Priorities'); if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet('Priorities'); sheet.getRange(1, 1).setValue('ro_number'); } return sheet; } // Read the full Priorities tab and return a map: // { ro_number: currentPriority (number) | null } // currentPriority = rightmost non-empty value in that row (col 2+) function readPriorityMap() { const sheet = ensurePrioritiesSheet(); const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); const lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); const map = {}; // ro -> priority (number) | null if (lastRow < 2 || lastCol < 2) return map; const data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues(); data.forEach(function(row) { const ro = String(row[0]).trim(); if (!ro) return; let current = null; for (let c = 1; c < row.length; c++) { const v = row[c]; if (v !== '' && v !== null && v !== undefined) current = Number(v); } // v3.7.2 — 0 is a sentinel written by removeFromPriorityQueue() // when an RO is confirmed delivered: setPriority() already // rejects 0 as an invalid rank (newPri < 1), so no real priority // could ever legitimately be 0 — safe to repurpose without // touching Priorities' own append-only column structure at all, // per the standing "never touch that structure" rule. Treated // identically to null here (and everywhere readPriorityMap feeds // into — getROs' ro.priority, setPriority's own future reorder // math), so a delivered RO cleanly drops out of the priority // queue instead of leaving a stale, confusing rank number behind. map[ro] = (current === 0) ? null : current; }); return map; } // Returns { ok: true, priority: N | null } for one RO function getPriority(roNumber) { try { const ro = String(roNumber || '').trim(); if (!ro) return { ok: false, error: 'missing ro_number' }; const map = readPriorityMap(); return { ok: true, ro_number: ro, priority: map[ro] !== undefined ? map[ro] : null }; } catch(e) { Logger.log('getPriority error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // Returns { ok: true, priorities: { ro: priority } } — all ROs with a priority set function getAllPriorities() { try { const map = readPriorityMap(); const result = {}; Object.keys(map).forEach(function(ro) { if (map[ro] !== null) result[ro] = map[ro]; }); return { ok: true, priorities: result }; } catch(e) { Logger.log('getAllPriorities error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // doPost action=setPriority { ro_number, priority, tech } // Aprovador-gated. Inserts the RO at the given priority position and // shifts all other prioritized ROs >= that position down by 1. // Unpriorized ROs are not affected. All changes written as one new // column (header = current ISO timestamp) appended to Priorities tab. function setPriority(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_authorized' }; const ro = String(body.ro_number || '').trim(); const newPri = parseInt(body.priority, 10); if (!ro) return { ok: false, error: 'missing ro_number' }; if (isNaN(newPri) || newPri < 1) return { ok: false, error: 'invalid_priority (must be >= 1)' }; const sheet = ensurePrioritiesSheet(); const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); const lastCol = Math.max(sheet.getLastColumn(), 1); // Read current state const currentMap = readPriorityMap(); // { ro: currentPriority | null } const allROs = Object.keys(currentMap); // all ROs already in the sheet // Ensure the target RO has a row (add it if new) let roRowIndex = -1; if (lastRow >= 2) { const roCol = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues(); for (let i = 0; i < roCol.length; i++) { if (String(roCol[i][0]).trim() === ro) { roRowIndex = i + 2; break; } } } if (roRowIndex === -1) { // New RO — append a row sheet.getRange(lastRow + 1, 1).setValue(ro); roRowIndex = lastRow + 1; currentMap[ro] = null; allROs.push(ro); } // Compute the new priority map after the reorder: // 1. Remove the target RO from the ordered list temporarily // 2. All ROs with priority >= newPri shift down by 1 // 3. Insert target at newPri const prioritized = allROs .filter(function(r) { return currentMap[r] !== null && r !== ro; }) .sort(function(a, b) { return currentMap[a] - currentMap[b]; }); // Insert target at position (newPri - 1) in the sorted list prioritized.splice(newPri - 1, 0, ro); // Build the new priority assignments: position in array + 1 = new priority const newAssignments = {}; // { ro: newPriority } prioritized.forEach(function(r, i) { newAssignments[r] = i + 1; }); // Find only the ROs whose priority actually changed (need a new column entry) const changed = {}; allROs.forEach(function(r) { const oldP = currentMap[r]; const newP = newAssignments[r] !== undefined ? newAssignments[r] : null; if (oldP !== newP && newP !== null) changed[r] = newP; }); // Also include the target explicitly even if it had no prior priority changed[ro] = newAssignments[ro]; if (Object.keys(changed).length === 0) { return { ok: true, message: 'No change needed', priorities: newAssignments }; } // Append a new column for this batch const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const newCol = lastCol + 1; // Write header (timestamp) in row 1 sheet.getRange(1, newCol).setValue(ts); // Write changed priorities — need row indices for each affected RO const allRows = lastRow >= 2 ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues() : []; const roToRow = {}; allRows.forEach(function(r, i) { roToRow[String(r[0]).trim()] = i + 2; }); roToRow[ro] = roRowIndex; // ensure target is mapped Object.keys(changed).forEach(function(r) { const rowIdx = roToRow[r]; if (rowIdx) sheet.getRange(rowIdx, newCol).setValue(changed[r]); }); // v3.4.5 — logs quem/porquê no PriorityAudit (Capacidade.gs), sem // alterar a estrutura append-only da Priorities tab em si. registrarPriorityAudit(ro, body.tech, body.motivo); return { ok: true, ro_number: ro, new_priority: newAssignments[ro], priorities: newAssignments, batch_ts: ts }; } catch(e) { Logger.log('setPriority error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // v3.7.2 — mirrors setPriority()'s reorder mechanics above, but for // REMOVAL instead of insertion: called from toggleEntrega() when an // RO is confirmed delivered and currently holds a manual priority // rank. Per Eytan: if RO #8 gets delivered, RO #9 should become #8, // #10 should become #9, and so on — not leave a gap. Writes rank=0 // for the delivered RO (readPriorityMap's sentinel for "removed", // see that function's comment) and shifts every RO that was ranked // higher down by one, in the SAME single new Priorities column/batch // — one append-only entry for the whole reshuffle, same pattern // setPriority already uses. No-op (returns early, writes nothing) if // the RO had no manual priority at all — most delivered ROs won't. // Never touches Priorities' own column structure — only ever appends // a new column, exactly like every other write to this tab. function removeFromPriorityQueue(roNumber, actor) { try { const ro = String(roNumber || '').trim(); if (!ro) return; const sheet = ensurePrioritiesSheet(); const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); const lastCol = Math.max(sheet.getLastColumn(), 1); const currentMap = readPriorityMap(); if (currentMap[ro] === null || currentMap[ro] === undefined) return; // not prioritized — nothing to shift const allROs = Object.keys(currentMap); const prioritized = allROs .filter(function(r) { return currentMap[r] !== null && r !== ro; }) .sort(function(a, b) { return currentMap[a] - currentMap[b]; }); // ro is simply left out — not re-inserted anywhere, unlike setPriority's splice const newAssignments = {}; prioritized.forEach(function(r, i) { newAssignments[r] = i + 1; }); const changed = {}; allROs.forEach(function(r) { const oldP = currentMap[r]; const newP = newAssignments[r] !== undefined ? newAssignments[r] : null; if (oldP !== newP && newP !== null) changed[r] = newP; }); changed[ro] = 0; // the sentinel — see readPriorityMap's comment const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const newCol = lastCol + 1; sheet.getRange(1, newCol).setValue(ts); const allRows = lastRow >= 2 ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues() : []; const roToRow = {}; allRows.forEach(function(r, i) { roToRow[String(r[0]).trim()] = i + 2; }); Object.keys(changed).forEach(function(r) { const rowIdx = roToRow[r]; if (rowIdx) sheet.getRange(rowIdx, newCol).setValue(changed[r]); }); Logger.log('removeFromPriorityQueue: RO ' + ro + ' removida da fila manual (era #' + currentMap[ro] + '), ' + (Object.keys(changed).length - 1) + ' RO(s) reordenada(s) — acionado por ' + (actor || '?')); } catch (e) { Logger.log('removeFromPriorityQueue error: ' + e); } } // v4.7.y — doPost action=removePriority { ro_number, tech, motivo } // Porta de entrada de usuário pra removeFromPriorityQueue, que até // agora só era chamada pelo fluxo de entrega. Mesmo gate de Aprovador // do setPriority: a função de baixo não gateia nada por conta própria. function removePriority(body) { try { if (!isApproverSync(body.tech)) return { ok: false, error: 'not_authorized' }; const ro = String(body.ro_number || '').trim(); if (!ro) return { ok: false, error: 'missing ro_number' }; // Lido ANTES de remover: é o rank que vai pro audit e pra resposta, // e é o que distingue "não tinha prioridade" de "removi" — sem // isso a tela não teria como dizer nada útil ao Aprovador. const antes = readPriorityMap()[ro]; if (antes === null || antes === undefined) { return { ok: false, error: 'sem_prioridade', message: 'Esta RO não tem prioridade manual.' }; } removeFromPriorityQueue(ro, body.tech); // Confere no dado, não na suposição: removeFromPriorityQueue engole // as próprias exceções, então um erro lá dentro voltaria como // sucesso silencioso se a gente só confiasse no retorno dela. const depois = readPriorityMap()[ro]; if (depois !== null && depois !== undefined) { return { ok: false, error: 'falha_na_remocao', message: 'A prioridade não foi removida — ver o log da execução.' }; } // Mesmo padrão do setPriority. O motivo default deixa a linha do // audit legível sozinha: a aba não tem coluna de ação, então sem // texto uma remoção pareceria uma inserção qualquer. try { registrarPriorityAudit(ro, body.tech, body.motivo || ('PRIORIDADE REMOVIDA (era #' + antes + ')')); } catch (eAudit) { Logger.log('removePriority: falha ao gravar PriorityAudit — ' + eAudit); } return { ok: true, ro_number: ro, previous_priority: antes, priorities: getAllPriorities().priorities }; } catch (e) { Logger.log('removePriority error: ' + e); return { ok: false, error: String(e) }; } } // ── HELPERS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function respond(obj) { return ContentService .createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(obj)) .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); } // v3.9 — pequeno escape HTML pro lado do servidor. Diferente do // escapeHtml() do index.html (que roda no navegador) — essa página é // montada como string aqui dentro do Apps Script e devolvida pronta, // então precisa da própria versão, não pode reusar a do cliente. function escapeHtmlServer_(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s) .replace(/&/g, '&').replace(//g, '>') .replace(/"/g, '"'); } // v3.9 — página web dedicada mostrando Alocacao_Diaria de forma // legível, pro gerente da oficina (Raynan) não precisar abrir a // planilha crua. sheet.appendRow(['timestamp', 'label', 'detail']); } sheet.appendRow([new Date().toISOString(), label, detail]); } catch (e) { // Never let debug logging itself break the real request. } } function rowToObj(headers, row) { const obj = {}; headers.forEach(function(h, i) { obj[h] = row[i]; }); return obj; } function getJoblineRows(roNumber) { const data = SHEETS.joblines.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; return data.slice(1) .map(function(r) { return rowToObj(headers, r); }) .filter(function(r) { return !roNumber || String(r.ro_number) === String(roNumber); }); } function getLockMap() { const data = SHEETS.lockStatus.getDataRange().getValues(); const map = {}; data.slice(1).forEach(function(r, i) { map[r[1]] = { ro_number: r[0], jobline_id: r[1], status: r[2], tech: r[3], started: r[4], released: r[5], rowIndex: i + 2, }; }); return map; } function getCompletedDefaultsMap() { const data = SHEETS.completedDefaults.getDataRange().getValues(); if (data.length < 2) return {}; const headers = data[0]; const map = {}; data.slice(1).forEach(function(r) { const obj = rowToObj(headers, r); if (obj.jobline_id) map[obj.jobline_id] = obj; }); return map; } function getLastTechMap() { const data = SHEETS.workLog.getDataRange().getValues(); if (data.length < 2) return {}; const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('jobline_id'); const techCol = headers.indexOf('tech'); if (idCol === -1 || techCol === -1) return {}; const map = {}; data.slice(1).forEach(function(r) { if (r[idCol]) map[r[idCol]] = r[techCol]; }); return map; } function findLockRow(joblineId) { return getLockMap()[joblineId] || null; } function updateLockRow(rowIndex, values) { SHEETS.lockStatus.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, values.length).setValues([values]); } function updateCompletion(joblineId, pct, elapsedSeconds) { const data = SHEETS.joblines.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('jobline_id'); const pctCol = headers.indexOf('completion_pct'); const timeCol = headers.indexOf('elapsed_seconds'); if (idCol === -1) return; data.slice(1).forEach(function(r, i) { if (String(r[idCol]) === String(joblineId)) { if (pctCol > -1) SHEETS.joblines.getRange(i+2, pctCol+1).setValue(pct); if (timeCol > -1) SHEETS.joblines.getRange(i+2, timeCol+1).setValue(elapsedSeconds); } }); } function ensureWorkLogHeaders() { const sheet = SHEETS.workLog; if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) { sheet.appendRow([ 'timestamp', 'tech', 'ro_number', 'jobline_id', 'job_description', 'event', 'completion_pct', 'elapsed_seconds', 'gps_start', 'gps_stop', 'app_version', ]); } } function ensureCompletedDefaultsHeaders() { const sheet = SHEETS.completedDefaults; if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) { sheet.appendRow([ 'ro_number', 'jobline_id', 'job_description', 'tech', 'completed_at', 'completion_pct', ]); } } function getDefaultAssignmentsMap() { const data = SHEETS.defaultAssignments.getDataRange().getValues(); if (data.length < 2) return {}; const headers = data[0]; const descCol = headers.indexOf('keyword') > -1 ? headers.indexOf('keyword') : headers.indexOf('jobline_description'); const allowedCol = headers.indexOf('allowed_techs'); if (descCol === -1 || allowedCol === -1) return {}; const map = {}; data.slice(1).forEach(function(r) { const kw = String(r[descCol]).trim().toLowerCase(); const allowed = String(r[allowedCol]).split(',').map(function(t) { return t.trim(); }).filter(Boolean); if (kw && allowed.length) map[kw] = allowed; }); return map; } function sanitiseName(str) { return String(str) .normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '') .replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9 _-]/g, '') .replace(/\s+/g, '_') .substring(0, 50); } // ── END-OF-DAY AUTO-RELEASE ────────────────────────────────────── // Releases all locked joblines at 20:00 every day. // Run installEndOfDayTrigger() ONCE manually in the editor to activate. function releaseAllAtEndOfDay() { const data = SHEETS.lockStatus.getDataRange().getValues(); const headers = data[0]; const idCol = headers.indexOf('jobline_id'); const stCol = headers.indexOf('status'); const techCol = headers.indexOf('tech'); const staCol = headers.indexOf('started'); let released = 0; data.slice(1).forEach(function(row, i) { if (String(row[stCol]).toLowerCase() !== 'locked') return; const rowIndex = i + 2; SHEETS.lockStatus.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, 6).setValues([[ row[0], // ro_number row[idCol], // jobline_id 'free', // status → free '', // tech cleared row[staCol], // original start kept new Date().toISOString(), // released_at = now ]]); SHEETS.workLog.appendRow([ new Date().toISOString(), row[techCol] || '', row[0] || '', row[idCol] || '', '', 'auto_released_eod', 0, 0, '', '', 'system', ]); released++; }); Logger.log('releaseAllAtEndOfDay: released ' + released + ' locked jobline(s).'); return released; } // Run this ONCE manually in the Apps Script editor (function dropdown → Run). // Do NOT call on every deploy — it installs a permanent daily trigger. function installEndOfDayTrigger() { ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function(t) { if (t.getHandlerFunction() === 'releaseAllAtEndOfDay') { ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); } }); ScriptApp.newTrigger('releaseAllAtEndOfDay') .timeBased() .everyDays(1) .atHour(20) .create(); Logger.log('Trigger installed: releaseAllAtEndOfDay at 20:00 daily.'); } // ── DAILY AUTO-RECONCILIATION (v2.7.5) ──────────────────────────── // Runs reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog() once a day, automatically, // as a safety net on top of the v2.7.4 reliable-release fix (which // should catch the vast majority of cases live, at the moment a // pause/complete happens — this is just cleanup for whatever still // slips through, e.g. a tech closing the app mid-retry). // // Scheduled for 20:15 — 15 minutes after releaseAllAtEndOfDay (20:00) // — deliberately offset, not because of any real ordering dependency // (releaseAllAtEndOfDay only touches Lock Status, never // completion_pct, so there's no actual conflict either order), but // to keep each run's Logger.log output cleanly separated for anyone // reviewing the Executions log later. // // This is a thin wrapper, not a behavior change: the underlying // reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog() function is UNCHANGED and remains // independently runnable manually from the editor at any time — this // trigger just calls the exact same function on a schedule. function runDailyReconciliation() { Logger.log('▶ runDailyReconciliation (scheduled) — calling reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog()'); reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog(); } // Run this ONCE manually from the Apps Script editor (function // dropdown → installReconcileTrigger → Run) to activate the daily // 20:15 schedule. Safe to re-run — removes any existing trigger for // this handler first, so running it twice never creates duplicates // (which would otherwise run the reconciliation twice per day). function installReconcileTrigger() { ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function(t) { if (t.getHandlerFunction() === 'runDailyReconciliation') { ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); } }); ScriptApp.newTrigger('runDailyReconciliation') .timeBased() .everyDays(1) .atHour(20) .nearMinute(15) .create(); Logger.log('Trigger installed: runDailyReconciliation at ~20:15 daily.'); } /** Remove the daily reconciliation trigger, reverting to manual-only. */ function removeReconcileTrigger() { ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function(t) { if (t.getHandlerFunction() === 'runDailyReconciliation') { ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); Logger.log('🗑 Trigger removed: ' + t.getUniqueId()); } }); } // ============================================================ // JOBLINE COMPLETION RECONCILIATION — rebuild completion_pct / // elapsed_seconds in the Joblines sheet from Work Log history, // for any jobline whose completion_pct is currently blank. // // Originally written (as recoverCompletionFromWorkLog) as a // ONE-TIME fix for the old syncJoblines() destructive clear+rewrite // bug (fixed in the sync project — syncJoblines now merges instead // of overwriting). Renamed in v2.7.4 to reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog // because the same gap can resurface from a DIFFERENT cause: the // PWA's 'release' action (POST, mode:'no-cors') is fire-and-forget — // if that specific request fails to complete on the server (slow // full-sheet scan in updateCompletion(), execution timeout, dropped // connection mid-request, etc.) while the separate 'log_work' POST // for the same action succeeds, Work Log ends up with the correct // pct/elapsed_seconds for a paused/completed event, but the Joblines // sheet's own completion_pct cell never gets written and stays // blank — same end symptom as the old sync bug, different root // cause. Confirmed case: RO 29912, jobline // 29912_003_diagnostico_ar_condicionado_nao_funciona — Work Log // shows a 'paused' event at 50%, Joblines completion_pct was blank. // // This function reconstructs that lost state from Work Log, which // is unaffected by either failure mode — every 'paused' and // 'completed' event ever logged is still there, in full, with pct // and elapsed_seconds. For each jobline_id, it finds the MOST RECENT // paused/completed event and writes that pct + elapsed_seconds back // into the matching Joblines row. // // Rules: // - Only considers Work Log events with event = 'paused' or // 'completed' (ignores 'started', which always has pct = 0 // and would incorrectly stomp a real recovered value if it // happened to be the latest row for some other reason). // - Skips jobline_id values starting with 'DEF-' (default // joblines live in the Completed Defaults sheet, not here). // - Only writes a row if a matching jobline_id exists in the // Joblines sheet AND that jobline currently has a BLANK // completion_pct — NEVER overwrites a value that's already // present, even if it disagrees with Work Log's most recent // entry. This is a deliberate, conservative choice: this // function can only ADD missing information, never silently // override something the app (or a human) already wrote // correctly. Safe to run any number of times. // - Does NOT touch lock_status in the Lock Status sheet — this // only repairs the Joblines sheet's own columns. If a jobline // should also show as "free" (not paused/locked) in Lock // Status, check that separately. // // Can be run manually from the Apps Script editor at any time // (select reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog in the function dropdown, // then Run) whenever you suspect this gap. Always safe to re-run; // it only ever fills in cells that are still blank, never overwrites // an existing non-blank value. // As of v2.7.5, ALSO runs automatically once a day (~20:15) as a // safety net on top of the v2.7.4 reliable-release fix — see // runDailyReconciliation() / installReconcileTrigger() below. This // is NOT wired into the separate 5-min sync_all.gs project's trigger // (different Apps Script project entirely) — it's its own daily // trigger inside THIS project, same pattern as installEndOfDayTrigger. // ============================================================ function reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog() { const jlSheet = SHEETS.joblines; const wlSheet = SHEETS.workLog; // ── Read Joblines sheet ────────────────────────────────────── const jlData = jlSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const jlHeaders = jlData[0]; const jlIdCol = jlHeaders.indexOf('jobline_id'); const jlPctCol = jlHeaders.indexOf('completion_pct'); const jlTimeCol = jlHeaders.indexOf('elapsed_seconds'); if (jlIdCol === -1 || jlPctCol === -1 || jlTimeCol === -1) { Logger.log('❌ reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog: Joblines sheet missing expected columns. Aborting.'); return; } // Map jobline_id -> sheet row number (1-indexed), but only for // rows where completion_pct is currently BLANK (don't touch // anything already filled in, whether by the old or new sync). const blankRowsByJoblineId = {}; jlData.slice(1).forEach(function(row, i) { const id = String(row[jlIdCol]).trim(); if (!id || id.indexOf('DEF-') === 0) return; // skip defaults const pct = row[jlPctCol]; const isBlank = (pct === '' || pct === null || pct === undefined); if (isBlank) { blankRowsByJoblineId[id] = i + 2; // +2: header row + 0-index offset } }); Logger.log('ℹ reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog: ' + Object.keys(blankRowsByJoblineId).length + ' jobline(s) in Joblines sheet currently have a blank completion_pct and are eligible for recovery'); if (Object.keys(blankRowsByJoblineId).length === 0) { Logger.log('✅ reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog: nothing to recover, exiting.'); return; } // ── Read Work Log sheet ────────────────────────────────────── const wlData = wlSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); const wlHeaders = wlData[0]; const wlTsCol = wlHeaders.indexOf('timestamp'); const wlIdCol = wlHeaders.indexOf('jobline_id'); const wlEventCol = wlHeaders.indexOf('event'); const wlPctCol = wlHeaders.indexOf('completion_pct'); const wlTimeCol = wlHeaders.indexOf('elapsed_seconds'); if (wlTsCol === -1 || wlIdCol === -1 || wlEventCol === -1 || wlPctCol === -1 || wlTimeCol === -1) { Logger.log('❌ reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog: Work Log sheet missing expected columns. Aborting.'); return; } // For each jobline_id we care about, find the most recent // paused/completed event. const latestByJoblineId = {}; // jobline_id -> { ts, pct, elapsed } wlData.slice(1).forEach(function(row) { const id = String(row[wlIdCol]).trim(); if (!blankRowsByJoblineId.hasOwnProperty(id)) return; // not a jobline we need to recover const event = String(row[wlEventCol]).trim().toLowerCase(); if (event !== 'paused' && event !== 'completed') return; // ignore 'started' etc. const ts = new Date(row[wlTsCol]); if (isNaN(ts.getTime())) return; // skip malformed timestamps const existing = latestByJoblineId[id]; if (!existing || ts > existing.ts) { latestByJoblineId[id] = { ts: ts, pct: row[wlPctCol], elapsed: row[wlTimeCol], }; } }); Logger.log('ℹ reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog: found Work Log history for ' + Object.keys(latestByJoblineId).length + ' of those jobline(s)'); // ── Write recovered values back into Joblines sheet ────────── let recoveredCount = 0; Object.keys(latestByJoblineId).forEach(function(id) { const rowIndex = blankRowsByJoblineId[id]; const recovered = latestByJoblineId[id]; jlSheet.getRange(rowIndex, jlPctCol + 1).setValue(recovered.pct); jlSheet.getRange(rowIndex, jlTimeCol + 1).setValue(recovered.elapsed); Logger.log(' ↳ recovered ' + id + ' → pct=' + recovered.pct + ', elapsed=' + recovered.elapsed + ' (from Work Log event at ' + recovered.ts.toISOString() + ')'); recoveredCount++; }); const notFoundCount = Object.keys(blankRowsByJoblineId).length - recoveredCount; Logger.log('✅ reconcileCompletionFromWorkLog done — ' + recoveredCount + ' jobline(s) recovered, ' + notFoundCount + ' had no paused/completed Work Log history (left blank, nothing to recover).'); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── ELAPSED_SECONDS REPAIR (v3.7.7) ──────────────────────────── // One-time backfill for Work Log rows recorded before the v3.7.6 // timer fix (index.html), when elapsed_seconds was undercounted by // setInterval throttling during backgrounding — real case that // motivated this: a jobline logged started→completed 409s apart by // wall clock, elapsed_seconds only recorded 245s. // // IMPORTANT CAVEAT, not a full recovery: the recalculated value is // (completed/paused timestamp − started timestamp), the TOTAL wall- // clock duration of the session — NOT necessarily true active-work // time. If a tech genuinely had the phone backgrounded for a few // minutes mid-session (not just a brief interruption), that gap is // indistinguishable after the fact from active work — there's no // historical record of exactly when backgrounding started or // stopped. This is a BETTER estimate than the undercounted original // in most cases, not a perfect one. No way to retroactively recover // the true value with certainty. // // Two-phase, manual-run only, same pattern as every other Work Log // repair tool in this file: // 1. repairElapsedSecondsDryRun() — analyzes every jobline_id's // started→paused/completed pairs, logs proposed changes, writes // NOTHING. Run this first and review carefully. // 2. repairElapsedSecondsApply() — re-runs the same analysis and // actually writes the corrected values. Only run after // reviewing the dry run. Irreversible — the old (undercounted) // values are only visible in the dry-run log output, not // recoverable from the sheet itself once overwritten. // // Pairing logic mirrors Code.gs's own reconstructSessionsForRO(): // group all rows by jobline_id, walk chronologically, pair each // 'started' with the NEXT 'paused'/'completed' for that same // jobline_id. A 5-second tolerance skips negligible float/rounding // differences that aren't worth rewriting. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function _analyzeElapsedSecondsDiscrepancies() { const sheet = SHEETS.workLog; const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); if (data.length < 2) return []; const headers = data[0]; const tsCol = headers.indexOf('timestamp'); const jlCol = headers.indexOf('jobline_id'); const evCol = headers.indexOf('event'); const elapsedCol = headers.indexOf('elapsed_seconds'); if ([tsCol, jlCol, evCol, elapsedCol].indexOf(-1) > -1) { Logger.log('_analyzeElapsedSecondsDiscrepancies: Work Log missing expected columns. Abortando.'); return []; } const byJobline = {}; // jobline_id -> [row indices, 0-indexed into data[]] for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { const jl = String(data[r][jlCol]).trim(); if (!jl) continue; if (!byJobline[jl]) byJobline[jl] = []; byJobline[jl].push(r); } const fixes = []; // { row (1-indexed sheet row), jobline_id, oldValue, newValue, startedAt, endedAt } const TOLERANCIA_SEGUNDOS = 5; Object.keys(byJobline).forEach(function(jl) { const rows = byJobline[jl].sort(function(a, b) { return new Date(data[a][tsCol]) - new Date(data[b][tsCol]); }); let openStartRow = null, openStartTs = null; rows.forEach(function(r) { const evento = String(data[r][evCol]).trim().toLowerCase(); const ts = new Date(data[r][tsCol]); if (isNaN(ts.getTime())) return; if (evento === 'started') { openStartRow = r; openStartTs = ts; } else if (evento === 'paused' || evento === 'completed') { if (openStartRow !== null) { const realElapsed = Math.round((ts - openStartTs) / 1000); const storedElapsed = Number(data[r][elapsedCol]) || 0; if (Math.abs(realElapsed - storedElapsed) > TOLERANCIA_SEGUNDOS) { fixes.push({ row: r + 1, jobline_id: jl, oldValue: storedElapsed, newValue: realElapsed, startedAt: openStartTs.toISOString(), endedAt: ts.toISOString(), }); } openStartRow = null; openStartTs = null; } // um paused/completed sem 'started' aberto correspondente é // ignorado — não há como calcular duração sem um início // conhecido, mesmo comportamento de reconstructSessionsForRO. } }); }); return fixes; } function repairElapsedSecondsDryRun() { const fixes = _analyzeElapsedSecondsDiscrepancies(); Logger.log('═══ elapsed_seconds repair — DRY RUN, nada escrito ═══'); Logger.log('Encontradas ' + fixes.length + ' linha(s) com diferença > 5s entre o valor gravado e a duração real (fim − início).'); fixes.forEach(function(f) { Logger.log('Linha ' + f.row + ' | jobline ' + f.jobline_id + ' | ' + f.startedAt + ' → ' + f.endedAt + ' | antigo=' + f.oldValue + 's → novo=' + f.newValue + 's (diferença de ' + (f.newValue - f.oldValue) + 's)'); }); Logger.log('─────────────────────────────────────────────'); Logger.log('IMPORTANTE: o valor novo é a duração TOTAL do relógio de ponta a ponta,'); Logger.log('não necessariamente tempo ativo real — se o técnico ficou com o app em'); Logger.log('segundo plano no MEIO da sessão (não só um soluço breve de throttling),'); Logger.log('esse tempo também entra na conta. Sem forma de recuperar retroativamente'); Logger.log('só o tempo ativo de verdade — essa é a melhor aproximação disponível.'); Logger.log('repairElapsedSecondsApply() vai escrever essas ' + fixes.length + ' correção(ões).'); } function repairElapsedSecondsApply() { const sheet = SHEETS.workLog; const headers = sheet.getDataRange().getValues()[0]; const elapsedCol = headers.indexOf('elapsed_seconds'); if (elapsedCol === -1) { Logger.log('repairElapsedSecondsApply: coluna elapsed_seconds não encontrada. Abortando.'); return; } const fixes = _analyzeElapsedSecondsDiscrepancies(); Logger.log('═══ elapsed_seconds repair — APLICANDO ' + fixes.length + ' correção(ões) ═══'); fixes.forEach(function(f) { sheet.getRange(f.row, elapsedCol + 1).setValue(f.newValue); Logger.log('✅ Linha ' + f.row + ' (jobline ' + f.jobline_id + ') corrigida → ' + f.newValue + 's (era ' + f.oldValue + 's)'); }); Logger.log('Concluído. Rode repairElapsedSecondsDryRun() de novo pra confirmar 0 diferenças > 5s restantes.'); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── WORK LOG TECH REASSIGNMENT (v3.7.8) ──────────────────────── // One-time repair: some Work Log rows have the wrong tech attributed // for certain job types — real cases per Eytan: "Checklist entrada" // and "PQIS" should always be Joao Pedro; anything with "Higienizar"/ // "Higienização" in the description should always be Silvania. // // EASILY EDITABLE — if this needs to run again later with different // columns or rules, everything that needs changing lives in the // CONFIG block at the top of _analyzeWorkLogTechCorrections() below: // COL_TECH_NAME/COL_DESC_NAME (column HEADER NAMES, not fixed letters // — robust against the sheet ever getting a column reordered) and the // REGRAS array (add/remove/edit match rules freely — first rule that // matches a row wins, later rules are skipped for that row). // // Matching is case/accent-insensitive via the existing // normaliseSearchStr() (already used elsewhere in this file for // Customers search) — reused here rather than reimplementing the same // normalization a second time. // // Two-phase, manual-run only, same convention as every other Work Log // repair tool in this file: // 1. repairWorkLogTechDryRun() — logs every proposed change, writes // NOTHING. Run this first and review carefully. // 2. repairWorkLogTechApply() — writes only after reviewing the // dry run. Irreversible — the old tech name is only visible in // the dry-run log output, not recoverable from the sheet once // overwritten. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function _analyzeWorkLogTechCorrections() { // ── CONFIG — edit here if this needs to run again differently ── var COL_TECH_NAME = 'tech'; var COL_DESC_NAME = 'job_description'; var REGRAS = [ { tech: 'Joao Pedro', test: function(descNorm) { return descNorm === 'checklist entrada' || descNorm === 'pqis'; } }, { tech: 'Silvania', test: function(descNorm) { return descNorm.indexOf('higieniz') >= 0; } }, // pega "higienizar" e "higienizacao" (acentos já removidos por normaliseSearchStr) { tech: 'Mike Luciano', test: function(descNorm) { return descNorm.indexOf('pintar') >= 0 || descNorm.indexOf('pintura') >= 0; } }, // duas keywords porque "pintar" não é substring de "pintura" nem vice-versa ]; // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── var sheet = SHEETS.workLog; var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); if (data.length < 2) return []; var headers = data[0]; var colTech = headers.indexOf(COL_TECH_NAME); var colDesc = headers.indexOf(COL_DESC_NAME); if (colTech === -1 || colDesc === -1) { Logger.log('_analyzeWorkLogTechCorrections: coluna "' + COL_TECH_NAME + '" ou "' + COL_DESC_NAME + '" não encontrada em Work Log. Abortando.'); return []; } var fixes = []; // { row (1-indexed), job_description, oldTech, newTech } for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { var descRaw = String(data[r][colDesc] || '').trim(); if (!descRaw) continue; var descNorm = normaliseSearchStr(descRaw); var currentTech = String(data[r][colTech] || '').trim(); for (var i = 0; i < REGRAS.length; i++) { if (REGRAS[i].test(descNorm)) { if (currentTech !== REGRAS[i].tech) { fixes.push({ row: r + 1, job_description: descRaw, oldTech: currentTech, newTech: REGRAS[i].tech }); } break; // primeira regra que bate vence — não aplica duas regras na mesma linha } } } return fixes; } function repairWorkLogTechDryRun() { var fixes = _analyzeWorkLogTechCorrections(); Logger.log('═══ Work Log tech repair — DRY RUN, nada escrito ═══'); Logger.log(fixes.length + ' linha(s) seriam corrigidas.'); fixes.forEach(function(f) { Logger.log('Linha ' + f.row + ' | "' + f.job_description + '" | tech atual="' + f.oldTech + '" → novo="' + f.newTech + '"'); }); Logger.log('repairWorkLogTechApply() vai escrever essas ' + fixes.length + ' correção(ões).'); } function repairWorkLogTechApply() { var sheet = SHEETS.workLog; var headers = sheet.getDataRange().getValues()[0]; var colTech = headers.indexOf('tech'); if (colTech === -1) { Logger.log('repairWorkLogTechApply: coluna tech não encontrada. Abortando.'); return; } var fixes = _analyzeWorkLogTechCorrections(); Logger.log('═══ Work Log tech repair — APLICANDO ' + fixes.length + ' correção(ões) ═══'); fixes.forEach(function(f) { sheet.getRange(f.row, colTech + 1).setValue(f.newTech); Logger.log('✅ Linha ' + f.row + ' ("' + f.job_description + '") corrigida → ' + f.newTech + ' (era "' + f.oldTech + '")'); }); Logger.log('Concluído. Rode repairWorkLogTechDryRun() de novo pra confirmar 0 correções restantes.'); } // ── EXPERIMENTAL — ONE-TIME MANUAL TEST for the proposed CTA-URL- // button template (NOT yet implemented in sendAgendaWhatsApp/the // live flow). Standalone and self-contained on purpose — does not // touch sendAgendaWhatsApp at all, so the live creation/reminder // flow keeps working exactly as it does today no matter what // happens here. // // Proposed new template (still needs Meta approval before this can // actually deliver anything — until then this only proves out the // webhook → Zapier → WABA wiring; the WABA send itself will fail or // no-op against a template name that doesn't exist yet): // Body: "Olá {{1}}, o agendamento do seu Porsche {{2}} está // confirmado para {{3}}." // Button: type=URL (dynamic), text="Ver localização no mapa", // url=https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query={{1}} // — NOTE: button {{1}} is a SEPARATE variable namespace // from the body's {{1}}; Zapier's WABA action exposes it // as its own mapped field (button_var_1 below), not // shared with template_var_1. // // Run from the editor (function dropdown → testSendWebhookCtaButton // → Run). Update WABA_TEMPLATE_NAME_CTA below to match whatever name // the new template actually gets in Meta Business Manager once // submitted/approved, and map button_var_1 to the button's dynamic- // URL variable inside Zapier's Catch Hook → WABA action step. const WABA_TEMPLATE_NAME_CTA = 'agendamento_confirmado_mapa'; // placeholder — replace with real approved template name function testSendWebhookCtaButton() { try { const toPhone = '+5521994494183'; // replace with YOUR real number to actually receive it const clientName = 'Teste Botão Mapa'; const vehicleModel = 'Cayenne Teste'; const dateTimeLabel = formatAgendaDateTimePt(new Date(Date.now() + 2 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)); const locationKey = 'service_rio'; // try 'pre_owned_rio' for the other address const address = resolveAgendaLocationAddress(locationKey); const phone = String(toPhone || '').replace(/\D/g, ''); if (!phone) { Logger.log('testSendWebhookCtaButton: no phone number, aborting'); return false; } // Button's dynamic-URL variable: per Meta's rules this is the // SUFFIX appended to the button's fixed base URL only — not a // full URL of its own. Zapier/WABA appends this to whatever base // URL is configured on the button at template-approval time // (https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=), so this // value must be just the query content, URL-encoded. const buttonVar1 = encodeURIComponent(address); const payload = { to: phone, template_name: WABA_TEMPLATE_NAME_CTA, // included so this can be routed to a DIFFERENT Zapier WABA action step than the live text-only send, if the Zap is set up to branch on it template_var_1: clientName, template_var_2: vehicleModel, template_var_3: dateTimeLabel, button_var_1: buttonVar1, // Included for convenience when eyeballing the Catch Hook // payload in Zapier — NOT one of the template's own variables. _debug_full_address: address, _debug_maps_url_preview: 'https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=' + buttonVar1, }; Logger.log('testSendWebhookCtaButton payload: ' + JSON.stringify(payload)); const response = UrlFetchApp.fetch(WABA_ZAPIER_WEBHOOK_URL, { method: 'post', contentType: 'application/json', payload: JSON.stringify(payload), muteHttpExceptions: true, }); Logger.log('testSendWebhookCtaButton response code: ' + response.getResponseCode() + ', body: ' + response.getContentText()); return true; } catch (e) { Logger.log('testSendWebhookCtaButton error: ' + e); return false; } } // ============================================================ // ONE-TIME BACKFILL — seed "NOT APPROVED" in Aprovacoes for every // jobline that currently has no approval row at all. // // Context: syncJoblines() now seeds a NOT APPROVED row in // Aprovacoes the moment a brand-new jobline is first synced (see // sync script v1.9.2). That only covers joblines created AFTER // that fix went live. Every jobline that already existed in the // Joblines tab before then — and that nobody has explicitly // approved/declined yet — still has NO Aprovacoes row, which the // PWA renders as a blank/pending state instead of NOT APPROVED. // // This function finds every (ro_number, job_description) pair in // the Joblines tab that has no matching row in Aprovacoes, and // appends one NOT APPROVED row for it — same shape saveApprovals() // writes: ro_number, job_description, approved, timestamp, approver. // // Rules: // - Matches on ro_number + job_description, NOT jobline_id — // Aprovacoes has always keyed on job_description text (see // getApprovedJoblineSetByRO() and getApprovals() above), never // jobline_id. Matching on jobline_id here would create // duplicate/orphaned rows for joblines that already have a // real decision recorded under their description. // - DEFAULT_JOBLINES (VAL de entrada, Checklist, Diagnostico, // etc.) are NEVER seeded — they're not eligible for approval // anywhere else in the system (getApprovedJoblineSetByRO never // counts them either), so creating Aprovacoes rows for them // would invent data nothing downstream expects. // - Skips any (ro_number, job_description) pair that already has // an Aprovacoes row, approved TRUE or FALSE — never touches or // duplicates an existing decision. Safe to re-run any number of // times; only ever adds rows for pairs with zero existing rows. // - Does NOT skip closed/fechada ROs — if a jobline row still // exists in the Joblines tab, it gets backfilled regardless of // RO status, since the goal is "every jobline ever synced has // an explicit approval state." // // Run this ONCE manually from the Apps Script editor (function // dropdown → backfillNotApprovedForExistingJoblines → Run). // ============================================================ function backfillNotApprovedForExistingJoblines() { try { const jlData = SHEETS.joblines.getDataRange().getValues(); if (jlData.length < 2) { Logger.log('ℹ backfillNotApprovedForExistingJoblines: Joblines tab is empty, nothing to do.'); return; } const jlHeaders = jlData[0]; const roCol = jlHeaders.indexOf('ro_number'); const descCol = jlHeaders.indexOf('job_description'); if (roCol === -1 || descCol === -1) { Logger.log('❌ backfillNotApprovedForExistingJoblines: Joblines tab missing ro_number/job_description columns. Aborting.'); return; } // Build the set of DEFAULT_JOBLINES descriptions (lowercased, // trimmed) for fast exclusion — these are never eligible for // approval, same rule as getApprovedJoblineSetByRO(). const defaultDescSet = new Set(DEFAULT_JOBLINES.map(function(d) { return String(d).trim().toLowerCase(); })); // Collect every distinct (ro_number, job_description) pair that // exists in the Joblines tab, excluding defaults and blanks. const existingPairs = new Set(); // "ro|||desc" lowercased desc let totalJoblineRows = 0, skippedDefaults = 0, skippedBlank = 0; jlData.slice(1).forEach(function(row) { const ro = String(row[roCol]).trim(); const desc = String(row[descCol]).trim(); if (!ro || !desc) { skippedBlank++; return; } if (defaultDescSet.has(desc.toLowerCase())) { skippedDefaults++; return; } existingPairs.add(ro + '|||' + desc.toLowerCase()); totalJoblineRows++; }); Logger.log('ℹ backfillNotApprovedForExistingJoblines: ' + existingPairs.size + ' distinct eligible (ro, jobline) pairs found in Joblines tab (' + skippedDefaults + ' default-jobline rows skipped, ' + skippedBlank + ' blank rows skipped)'); if (existingPairs.size === 0) { Logger.log('✅ backfillNotApprovedForExistingJoblines: nothing eligible to backfill, exiting.'); return; } // Build the set of pairs that ALREADY have an Aprovacoes row — // regardless of approved TRUE or FALSE, any existing row means // "already has an explicit decision" and must not be touched. const apvData = SHEETS.aprovacoes.getDataRange().getValues(); const alreadyHasRow = new Set(); if (apvData.length >= 2) { apvData.slice(1).forEach(function(row) { const ro = String(row[0]).trim(); const desc = String(row[1]).trim(); if (!ro || !desc) return; alreadyHasRow.add(ro + '|||' + desc.toLowerCase()); }); } Logger.log('ℹ backfillNotApprovedForExistingJoblines: ' + alreadyHasRow.size + ' (ro, jobline) pairs already have an Aprovacoes row'); // Pairs needing a seeded NOT APPROVED row = eligible pairs minus // pairs that already have a row. const now = new Date(); // v2.7.51 — same fix as saveApprovals(), see comment there const rowsToAppend = []; // Re-walk the Joblines tab (rather than the Set, which lost the // original-case job_description) so the seeded row preserves // the exact original text, consistent with how syncJoblines() // and saveApprovals() write job_description elsewhere. const seenThisRun = new Set(); // avoid duplicate seeds if the same pair appears on multiple Joblines rows jlData.slice(1).forEach(function(row) { const ro = String(row[roCol]).trim(); const desc = String(row[descCol]).trim(); if (!ro || !desc) return; if (defaultDescSet.has(desc.toLowerCase())) return; const key = ro + '|||' + desc.toLowerCase(); if (alreadyHasRow.has(key)) return; if (seenThisRun.has(key)) return; seenThisRun.add(key); rowsToAppend.push([ro, desc, false, now, 'system']); }); if (rowsToAppend.length === 0) { Logger.log('✅ backfillNotApprovedForExistingJoblines: every eligible jobline already has an Aprovacoes row, nothing to backfill.'); return; } SHEETS.aprovacoes.getRange(SHEETS.aprovacoes.getLastRow() + 1, 1, rowsToAppend.length, 5) .setValues(rowsToAppend); Logger.log('✅ backfillNotApprovedForExistingJoblines done — seeded ' + rowsToAppend.length + ' NOT APPROVED row(s) for joblines that previously had no approval record.'); } catch (e) { Logger.log('❌ backfillNotApprovedForExistingJoblines error: ' + e); } } function TEMP_runDiagnostic() { diagnoseEntregaMatch("29619"); }
